+# AgentRec-X — Final Engineering Polish Report
+
+**Date:** 2026-11-10 (Asia/Shanghai)  
+**Scope:** Git archival, Web/MCP candidate consistency, BGE first-query stability/performance  
+**Final verdict:** **PASS** for all three requested phases.
+
+All work is local. No push, release, deployment, model training, benchmark expansion, Reserved Test,
+catalogue/index rebuild, V0 weight change, M9 permanent-history semantic change, new MCP tool or
+AutoDL lifecycle operation was performed.
+
+---
+
+## 1. Git archive
+
+### 1.1 Audited starting state
+
+- Branch: `archive/agentrecx-web-demo-v1`
+- Baseline HEAD: `c24b138bbc003689e3d75cd1f8dcae35d9dcad41`
+- Uncommitted at start: **32 modified + 18 untracked = 50 entries**
+- The only BGE performance implementation WIP was
+  `experiments/p14c1/search_backend.py` (single-flight/warmup draft).
+- `.gitignore` correctly excludes `data/`, `models/`, `runs/`, checkpoints, `.env*`, SQLite DBs and
+  large adapters. No large model/data/index artifact was staged.
+- Staged review found no secret-like patterns. Licence/notice files were untouched and retained.
+
+### 1.2 Local commits
+
+All three Qwen layers touch the same MCP/BFF/Web files. Per the brief's allowance for file overlap,
+they were archived as one coherent verified Qwen commit rather than unsafe hunk splitting.
+
+| Commit | Purpose |
+|---|---|
+| `3c89e7e62335fb9f5c0e526b5a2b46871e69136c` | Qwen Base Live, real ASK→M9→Resume, task-scoped clarification consistency, tests, E2E, contracts and reports (46 files) |
+| `93360debe004282f29aaffc549d5a6b0ae2eaa49` | Historical Qwen acceptance evidence source-diff snapshots (documentation only) |

+| `a40ec881d986bb0d9ae4c28ff83544ccb21fe6ff` | Validated BGE single-flight/warm-up/Search Ready performance implementation (6 files) |
+
+The BGE implementation was excluded from the Qwen commit while it was unverified, kept intact in
+the worktree, then validated and committed separately as `a40ec88`.
+
+### 1.3 Safety

+- No `git reset --hard`, `git clean`, forced checkout or blind `git add .`.
+- Staged file list and diff were inspected before each commit.

+- The final working tree is clean after the documentation commit.

+## 2. Web vs Direct MCP candidate diagnosis
+
+### 2.1 Actual result
+
+The earlier apparent difference was **not a candidate-quality or ranking defect**.
+
+- Web and Direct MCP both had **15 input candidates**, **14 eligible**, with
+  `B091SF1545` excluded by the current `avoid wired` hard exclusion.
+- Both used the **same eligible candidate ID list**:
+  `B00M0KUF44, B0BVZKKYBS, B00NFL0E2A, B00B93N0PO, B015M9NKUK, B07ZF8893F,
+  B0C3LYY9NL, B017ILTARW, B000P93MYM, B007ZG32I4, B01N7S0IPR, B00FCUCMLI,
+  B09XCWX4JG, B01LS2IG6I`.
+- Both Gate-selected the **same final product: `B00M0KUF44`**
+  (`RunPhones Wireless Headphones (Graphite Gray, Medium).`).
+- `B00NFL0E2A` (`Philmore Headphones.`) was the **first initial catalogue-result card** on the
+  shared turn, not the Gate-selected item. The old E2E assertion read the first
+  `product-card`, producing the incorrect "Web selected Philmore" conclusion.
+- The E2E now reads the dedicated `gate-selected-item` banner. The runtime V0/Scorer/Gate code
+  required **no change** for candidate ordering.
+
+Evidence: `runs/web_demo/final_polish/candidate_diagnosis.json`, plus the corrected E2E
+`qwen_fix.reevaluation_pool` and `qwen_fix.final_product_not_wired` checks.
+
+### 2.2 Soft preference behavior
+
+- `avoid wired` remains a task-scoped hard exclusion.
+- `prefer wireless` is a soft preference.
+- In the same pool, `B00M0KUF44` has explicit catalogue `wireless` evidence and ranks before
+  `B00NFL0E2A`, whose connection evidence is UNKNOWN. UNKNOWN is not treated as a positive match.
+- Hard exclusion is applied before Gate; no ASIN special case and no weight tuning.
+
+**Diagnosis:** PASS. **Runtime repair required:** none; only the E2E/reporting assertion was fixed.
+
+---
+
+## 3. BGE first-query performance and initialization stability
+
+### 3.1 Implementation
+
+`experiments/p14c1/search_backend.py` + `recommendation/control/catalog_search.py`:
+
+- Single-flight encoder initialization with a lock + ready event.
+- Double-checked ready path; model/tokenizer published only after both load and `eval()` succeed.
+- Clear attempt-limited failure state (2 attempts) and no half-initialized model publication.
+- One controlled recovery retry in `warmup()`.
+- A real warm-up query plus a real dense retrieval check before MCP readiness.
+- Encoder status/timings exposed to the MCP manifest/log: catalogue, configure, warm-up,
+  pre-handshake and Search Ready.
+- Injectable encoder loader for deterministic failure/single-flight tests; no production behavior
+  change.
+
+`recommendation/mcp/server.py` + `web_demo/mcp_manager.py`:
+
+- BGE profiles receive `--warmup` from the Web BFF; startup synchronously reaches Search Ready even
+  if the encoder is still cold.
+- `AGENTRECX_WEB_DEMO_BGE_WARMUP=0` is available only for controlled before/after measurements.
+- No fake readiness: if warm-up fails after the bounded retries, MCP startup fails explicitly; the
+  BFF shows the error and can be restarted.
+
+`scripts/run_web_demo.ps1` forwards the controlled BGE warm-up switch.
+
+No BGE model weights or BGE index were changed or rebuilt.
+
+### 3.2 Measured cold starts (three Web runs each, same host/assets/query)
+
+Query: `wireless headphones for commuting`.
+
+| Metric | Baseline (no prewarm) | Warmed BGE | Change |
+|---|---:|---:|---:|
+| BFF ready (`/api/health`) | 39.60–62.20 s, avg **49.53 s** | 45.73–58.47 s, avg **50.14 s** | +0.61 s avg (ranges overlap) |
+| MCP per-server startup | 30.0–48.9 s | 36.0–49.1 s | includes encoder warm-up |
+| **First real user query** | 15.63–17.12 s, avg **16.28 s** | 0.54–0.56 s, avg **0.552 s** | **−15.73 s / −96.6%** |
+| **Ready → first result** | 55.69–79.32 s, avg **65.81 s** | 46.28–59.04 s, avg **50.69 s** | **−15.12 s / −23.0%** |
+| Warm query | 0.283–0.300 s, avg **0.288 s** | 0.280–0.298 s, avg **0.288 s** | unchanged |
+
+MCP internal warm segments (current manifests):
+
+- Catalogue load: **~7.95–8.33 s**
+- Index/configure: **~5.19–5.79 s**
+- Encoder warm-up: **~8.91–18.42 s** (OS/file-cache dependent)
+- MCP pre-handshake total: **~13.38 s** baseline vs **~22.95–32.01 s** warmed
+- MCP startup including transport/client handshake: **~36–49 s** warmed.
+
+Direct source measurement (same fixed query):
+
+- No prewarm: first query **17.56 s**, total process **31.94 s**
+- Prewarm: warmup **15.95 s**, first query **0.092 s**, total process **31.40 s**
+- First 10 hit IDs identical in both runs; search results/evidence unchanged.
+
+Frontend: the final warmed CPU browser suite's first real turn measured **~1.7 s**
+(`01-search-recommend` chat.turn.latency), after page readiness.
+
+### 3.3 Concurrency / cancellation / failure
+
+- **4 concurrent real first queries**: all completed in **17.41 s**; `encoder_status.attempts=1`;
+  all four hit lists identical.
+- **Abandoned requester then retry**: retry **233 ms** after initialization, identical hits,
+  `attempts=1`; no half-initialized state.
+- **Real BFF client abort then retry**: retry **570 ms** after cancellation/25 s wait; same query
+  completed. This test intentionally closed the SSE stream after the initial event; it does not
+  claim a full mid-model-load cancellation was measured.
+- **Failure/recovery unit tests**: first load failure → second attempt succeeds; repeated failure
+  stops after 2 attempts with explicit error; ready event only after success.
+- Failure at MCP warm-up is a visible startup error, not fake ready.
+
+### 3.4 Resource and isolation cost
+
+At Search Ready with two independent profiles:
+
+| Item | Baseline ready (encoder not yet loaded) | Warmed ready |
+|---|---:|---:|
+| Each MCP RSS | ~1.124 GB | ~1.548 GB |
+| Two MCP RSS | ~2.25 GB | ~3.10 GB |
+| BFF RSS | ~70 MB | ~70 MB |
+| Host RAM | 7.7 GB total | 7.7 GB total |
+
+The encoder costs **~0.42 GB per profile**; two profiles pay **~0.85 GB** extra at readiness.
+This is the same encoder that the baseline would load on first user query; the cost is shifted
+into startup and reported, not hidden. No shared-encoder/public arbitrary-user process was created;
+P17 profile isolation remains one MCP process per fixed user key.
+

+## 6. Required final answers
+
+1. **Are all ~50 modifications safely saved?**
+   Yes. The verified Qwen, ASK/Resume, clarification and BGE changes are local commits; the
+   pre-existing uncommitted work was preserved and committed, and the BGE WIP was not lost. The
+   final worktree is clean.
+2. **What is committed vs WIP?**
+   All requested Qwen and BGE implementation work is committed in the four SHAs above. There is no
+   implementation WIP left; the only non-Git content is intentionally ignored runtime evidence
+   under `runs/`.
+3. **Why did Web and Direct MCP pick different products?**
+   They did not. Both selected `B00M0KUF44` from the same 14 eligible IDs after task re-evaluation.
+   The earlier `B00NFL0E2A` claim came from an E2E assertion reading the first initial product card
+   instead of the Gate-selected banner. The assertion/report were corrected; no runtime change.
+4. **Is an evidence-backed wireless product correctly preferred in the same pool?**
+   Yes. `B00M0KUF44` has explicit wireless catalogue evidence and ranks ahead of the UNKNOWN
+   `B00NFL0E2A`; UNKNOWN is not treated as MATCH. No ASIN special case or weight change.
+5. **How much faster is the first search?**
+   BFF first real user query: **16.28 s avg (15.63–17.12) → 0.552 s avg (0.540–0.563)**,
+   about **96.6%** less wait. Direct BGE first query: **17.56 s → 0.092 s**. Ready→first result
+   average: **65.81 s → 50.69 s** (**23.0%** lower). Warm queries are unchanged at ~0.288 s.
+6. **Did resource/startup total cost increase?**
+   The encoder now loads during startup: two-profile MCP RSS at ready rises **~2.25 GB → ~3.10 GB**
+   (**~0.85 GB extra**, ~0.42 GB per isolated profile). BFF ready average changed only
+   **+0.61 s** in these three-run samples (ranges **39.6–62.2 s → 45.7–58.5 s**, overlapping), while
+   ready→first result dropped ~15.1 s. The startup warm-up is included in the reported cost; no
+   calculation was hidden.
+7. **Did BGE changes break Qwen ASK → Memory → Resume?**
+   No contract change was made; Qwen unit/BFF contract tests pass and the previously archived real
+   ASK/Resume evidence remains. The real Qwen GPU service was not restarted for this phase, so no new
+   real GPU ASK test is claimed; CPU Live/Qwen Replay and the shared MCP/search/Gate paths passed.
+8. **Can the project move to final demo/resume polish?**
+   Yes for the engineering scope: three phases PASS, local Git archive complete, candidate
+   consistency explained, first search substantially faster and stable. The remaining external
+   requirement is to start the GPU Qwen service and tunnel for a live Qwen demo; those are
+   intentionally stopped now.
+

+# Qwen ASK → M9 → Resume — Real GPU Acceptance Report
+
+> **Update 2026-11-10 — superseded for the task-constraint scope:** the follow-up
+> [`QWEN_CLARIFICATION_FIX_REPORT.md`](QWEN_CLARIFICATION_FIX_REPORT.md) fixes the two limitations
+> recorded below. The prescribed single reply now creates a task-scoped hard exclusion, the
+> historical wired preference remains in M9, the preserved candidates are re-scored, and the real
+> resumed Qwen turn finalizes a product with no trustworthy wired evidence. The original report
+> remains the historical record of the pre-fix LIMITED state.
+
+**Date:** 2026-11-10 (Asia/Shanghai)  
+**Final verdict:** **LIMITED** — the real Qwen ASK branch is now exercised, but the exact prescribed
+single user reply does **not** clear the old `wired` preference under the existing M9 semantics;
+one additional **real browser-authored retraction** was required. The final Gate allowed
+finalization, but selected a product whose catalogue title/evidence is explicitly wired. No Memory,
+Gate or V0 semantics were changed to make this pass.
+
+**Scope:** real Qwen3.5-9B Base, real GPU, real P17 MCP execution, real M9 writes, real BFF/SSE/Web,
+real browser replies. No Rule-generated ASK, no HTTP double, no Replay, no hard-coded PlannerAction,
+no forced FINALIZE.
+
+---
+
+## 1. Environment and model identity
+
+| Item | Verified value |
+|---|---|
+| GPU | NVIDIA RTX 4090, 24,564 MiB, driver **570.124.04** |
+| Model | **Qwen3.5-9B Base**, `adapter=None` |
+| Model path | `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3.5-9B-ms` |
+| Torch / CUDA | `torch 2.11.0+cu128`; CUDA available **True**; CUDA 12.8 |
+| Model service | `training/agent_sft/model_server.py`, port 8009; temporary SSH tunnel `18009` |
+| Planner | real `POST /plan` → valid `PlannerAction` → existing `run_planner` / `ElectronicsPlannerExecutor` |
+| Runtime owner | existing P17 MCP server; **no second Agent runtime** |
+| Service state after evidence | model service and tunnel stopped; GPU back to **1 MiB** |
+| AutoDL lifecycle | no create/stop/restart/release/replace; existing instance reused |
+
+`runs/web_demo/qwen_ask_resume/gpu_service_info.txt` contains the raw capture.
+
+### Planner prompt correction (one generic correction)
+
+The only Planner-prompt change is a general Decision-Gate contract rule; it contains no
+wired/wireless special case:
+
+- Gate `NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION` / `needs_user_input=true` → choose `ASK`; do not finalize.
+- After `USER_CLARIFICATION_REPLIED` with no unresolved conflicts → use the candidates and
+  `FINALIZE`, rather than repeating the question.
+- `ASK` is forbidden when `needs_user_input=false` and `gate_action` is not
+  `NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION`; a surprising budget/typo alone is not an ASK signal.
+- FINALIZE only with candidates and no Gate user-input requirement.
+
+| Prompt | SHA-256 |
+|---|---|
+| Before | `c84e25e18d1d7370809b2906ae08e62cb87f49bf8184e7c2134b81f0fd4feecb` |
+| After | `d1d2c7f6dc9d342e187b337b908a8ae481534351955afdf7b695d8c3b639896f` |
+
+See `planner_prompt_sha.txt`. The same final prompt was deployed to the remote model service and
+verified by remote SHA.
+
+### Qwen service robustness fixes (not business semantics)
+
+Two service-level issues surfaced during real-GPU regression; both were fixed without changing
+Planner/Gate/Memory semantics:
+
+1. **Generation serialization:** `ThreadingHTTPServer` let the BFF health probe and a real turn
+   interleave tokenizer/model generation. A process-local `generate_lock` now serializes generation.
+2. **Single missing JSON closing brace:** Qwen Base occasionally emitted a valid Planner action
+   object without the final `}`. The parser now completes only that single missing closing brace,
+   preserving every model-authored field; any other malformed JSON remains a hard failure. The raw
+   model text is retained in the planner trace. This repair is recorded as a service workaround,
+   not as a rewritten action.
+
+These fixes restored the existing real Qwen Live E2E (2/2) and are included in the final 9/9
+regression.
+
+---
+

+- Added an **opt-in-only** profile `profile_0056_ask` (user key `p14e2_lite_0056`) behind
+  `AGENTRECX_WEB_DEMO_ASK_TEST_PROFILE=1`.
+- Normal demo profiles remain exactly `profile_0049` and `profile_0057`; the normal regression
+  asserts two profiles and uses no test-profile memory.
+- `p14e2_lite_0056` is an existing benchmark fixture user whose history is ingested through the
+  **real accepted M9 `process_turn` path** (`ShoppingCompanionMemory.ingest_sessions`). Its active
+  state contains both:
+  - `wired` — prefer, source turn `s1t0`, memory id `0d3d33ea23044b31b304c2e0`;
+  - `wireless for my keyboard` — prefer, source turn `s0t0`.

+  `wireless vs wired / mutually_exclusive_positive_preferences`.
+- The existing demo profiles 0049/0057 were not used for this ASK-memory test and their persisted
+  memory was not modified as part of the acceptance.

+## 3. Real Qwen ASK — PASS

+| 1 | `RETRIEVE {"query":"headphones","source_policy":"balanced"}` | 3,684.65 ms | 867 | 86 |

+| 3 | `FINALIZE {}` | 2,766.28 ms | 1,093 | 88 |
+| 4 | `ASK {"question":"I found conflicting preferences in your history (wired, wireless). Which one should take priority for this purchase?"}` | 3,854.53 ms | 1,339 | 94 |

+The ASK followed a real Gate decision:
+
+```text
+Gate action: NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION
+reason_code: preference_conflict_unresolved
+allow_finalize: false
+needs_user_input: true
+```

+The question is not Rule-generated, not a BFF rewrite, not a Gate-generated question, and not a
+hard-coded test string. Raw request/response envelopes are in

+## 5. First real browser reply and the M9 semantic limit
+
+The acceptance text was submitted through the real browser input and real HTTP POST:
+
+```text
+For this purchase, I prefer wireless headphones for commuting. Please avoid wired headphones.
+```
+
+BFF confirmation source: `web_ui_explicit_user_reply`. M9 result:
+
+```text
+added=2  superseded=0  removed=0  skipped_duplicates=0  removal_directives=0
+```
+
+The two added entries are:
+
+- `wireless headphones for commuting` — **prefer**;
+- `wired headphones` — **avoid**.
+
+However the old `wired` entry remains **active**:
+
+```text
+wired — prefer — active — memory_id=0d3d33ea23044b31b304c2e0 — source_turn_id=s1t0
+```
+
+This is an existing M9 semantic behavior: `avoid` is a new avoidance preference, **not** an explicit
+retraction. `process_turn` reported `removed=0` and `removal_directives=0`; `conflicts()` therefore
+still returned `wireless vs wired`, and the Gate still required user clarification. The real Qwen
+resume therefore produced a **second real ASK**, not a FINALIZE. This was not forced or hidden.
+
+> **Failure against the literal one-reply acceptance criterion:** after the exact prescribed first
+> reply, the old wired preference is still active. Changing Memory semantics to make an `avoid`
+> entry retract an older `prefer` entry was explicitly out of scope and was not done.
+
+**M9_REAL_UPDATE = PARTIAL** for the exact prescribed single reply (real write succeeded; the old
+wired preference was not removed). **MEMORY_STATE_VERIFIED = NOT ACHIEVED** at this point.
+
+This behavior is also visible in the browser:
+`browser_final/qwen-ask-after-first-reply.png` and the `qwen_ask.followup_ask` check.
+
+---
+
+## 6. Second real-browser reply → M9 update → verified state
+
+To complete the task truthfully, the same isolated test user submitted a second **real browser
+message** (not a fabricated reply, not a direct DB edit):
+
+```text
+Forget my wired preference.
+```
+
+That is an explicit M9 retraction and the accepted `process_turn` path applied it:
+
+```text
+added=0  superseded=0  removed=1  skipped_duplicates=0  removal_directives=1
+```
+
+Final M9 state (independent `HistoryStateReader` / BFF `/api/preferences`):
+
+| State | Value | Polarity | Status |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| Active | `wireless headphones for commuting` | prefer | active |
+| Active | `wireless for my keyboard` | prefer | active |
+| Active | `from a keyboard` | prefer | active |
+| Active | `wired headphones` | **avoid** | active |
+| Inactive | `wired` | prefer | **removed** |
+
+- old wired positive preference is no longer active;
+- the audit/inactive entry remains visible as removed;
+- user key is `p14e2_lite_0056` throughout;
+- no cross-user state is included.
+
+**MEMORY_STATE_VERIFIED = PASS after the second real user reply; PARTIAL/FAIL for the exact
+prescribed single-reply-only flow.**
+
+---
+
+## 7. Qwen Resume — PASS (technical continuation)
+
+The continuation is implemented in the existing MCP server: a clarification handle retains the
+**same executor instance**, CandidateLedger, candidate IDs, tool observations, retrieval/Gate
+budgets, clarification ID and original query. On reply, only the M9-derived preference list and
+memory history are refreshed, then a **new real Qwen `/plan` call** runs. It never reuses an old
+PlannerAction and never starts the turn from a new unrelated query.
+
+After the first reply, the real resume PlannerAction was:
+
+```text
+ASK
+```
+
+After the second (retraction) reply, the real resume PlannerAction was:
+
+```text
+FINALIZE
+```
+
+The final Gate decision:
+
+```text
+action: ALLOW_FINALIZE
+allow_finalize: true
+reason_code: evidence_sufficient
+selected_candidate_id: B091SF1545
+unknown_fields: ["wireless"]
+candidate_presentable_ids: [B091SF1545, B00M0KUF44, B00NFL0E2A, ...]
+```
+
+P15 evidence/`evidence_bundle` was present. The browser displayed a product card and Gate card.
+
+**QWEN_RESUME = PASS** (same task/ledger/budget, real new inference).
+**GATE_FINALIZATION = PASS** (existing Gate returned ALLOW_FINALIZE; no Gate change).
+
+### Final recommendation preference compliance — LIMITED / FAIL
+
+The Gate-selected product is **B091SF1545**, whose real catalogue title is:
+
+```text
+Earphones Wired Headphones Headphones Earbuds.
+```
+
+Its own P13R evidence includes the `wired` attribute at a lexical span. The final Gate reason is a
+*limited* recommendation with `unknown_fields=["wireless"]`; it allowed finalization even though the
+user's current active state contains `wired headphones = avoid` and `wireless = prefer`. No attempt
+was made to rewrite the selected item or bypass the Gate. This is a real final-recommendation
+quality/compliance limitation, not a fabricated successful recommendation.
+
+Evidence:
+
+- `selected_product_gate.json` (catalogue title, wired evidence, UNKNOWN fields);
+- `browser_final/qwen-ask-resume.png` (UI shows the selected wired-title product);
+- `browser_final/summary.json` check `qwen_ask.gate_final` (Gate action `ALLOW_FINALIZE`, 11 cards).
+
+**PREFERENCE_CONSISTENT_FINAL_RECOMMENDATION = LIMITED/FAIL.**
+

+-## 6. Tests and verification
++## 6. Qwen Base Live (real GPU validated 2026-11-09)
++
++This section now has **real GPU validation**, not just a contract wiring:
++RTX 4090 24 GB, Qwen3.5-9B Base (`/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3.5-9B-ms`, no SFT adapter), real
++`/plan` calls, real MCP `run_agent_turn`, real executor/Gate/P15, and two real browser Qwen Live
++turns. Full evidence: [`QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md`](QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md) and
++`runs/web_demo/qwen_live/`.
++
++This is an **optional** live mode over the existing P17 MCP / ElectronicsPlannerExecutor stack.
++It is not a new Agent loop: the real Qwen3.5-9B Base returns a PlannerAction (`ASK`, `RETRIEVE`,
++`RERANK`, `FINALIZE`), and the existing executor/search/scorer/ledger/eligibility/Gate/P15/M9
++components execute it. A Rule→Qwen rewrite or Qwen→Rule fallback is not implemented.
++
++### Configuration
++
++Set these in the **same Windows PowerShell session** that launches the demo (the launcher forwards
++them into WSL; the endpoint is never shown in the browser):
++
++```powershell
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED = "1"                       # explicit opt-in
++$env:QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1:18009"  # existing model server, NOT auto-started
++$env:QWEN_MODEL_NAME = "Qwen3.5-9B-Base"
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_TIMEOUT_S = "180"                    # each /plan call
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_S = "60"             # real PlannerAction health probe
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_TURN_TIMEOUT_S = "600"              # bounds the complete multi-step turn
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_MAX_STEPS = "8"
++powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts/run_web_demo.ps1
++```
++
++Availability is deliberately strict: the BFF performs a real `POST /plan` health probe and only
++shows Qwen Live as available after it parses a valid PlannerAction. A configured-but-unreachable
++service leaves **Qwen Live Unavailable**; CPU Live and Qwen Replay continue to work, and a Qwen
++request never silently becomes a Rule request. The BFF does **not** start/stop AutoDL/GPU, create
++SSH tunnels, download or quantize a model. Start the model service yourself, using the repository's
++`training/agent_sft/model_server.py` or your existing endpoint.
++
++The MCP server exposes the original 7 tools plus one conditional 8th tool,
++`run_agent_turn`, only when Qwen Live is configured. `run_agent_turn` returns the model's real
++actions, tool observations, Gate result, P15 evidence, planner/explainer latencies and the
++optional Qwen clarification handle. `/api/chat/stream` accepts
++`{"mode":"qwen_live", ...}` and emits the same SSE event names plus planner metadata on `done`.
++

++  model load **12.203 s**; GPU peak observed **19,845 MiB**; `/plan` 2.6–3.6 s direct/tunnel

++## 8. P20 Qwen Base Live (optional real mode)
++
++This section extends, and does not replace, the CPU/Replay contract above.
++
++- **Explicit opt-in only:** `QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED=1` AND a configured `QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT`
++  (aliases accepted internally: `AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER`, `AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER_URL`).
++  Optional: `QWEN_MODEL_NAME`, `QWEN_LIVE_TIMEOUT_S`, `QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_S`,
++  `QWEN_LIVE_TURN_TIMEOUT_S`, `QWEN_LIVE_MAX_STEPS`, `QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_INTERVAL_S`,
++  `QWEN_LIVE_EXPLAIN`. The endpoint is never exposed in health/config/SSE or logs.
++- **Health:** the BFF performs a real `POST {endpoint}/plan` with the existing planner contract and
++  only marks `qwen_live.available=true` when the response contains a valid PlannerAction for that
++  observation. The snapshot (`/api/health` → `qwen_live`) reports enabled/configured/status/
++  available/model/planner_action/latency_ms/checked_at/detail. No health pass → Unavailable.
++- **No fallback:** Qwen mode requests are sent only after availability; a `run_agent_turn` failure
++  is a real `qwen_*` error. The BFF never runs Rule and labels it Qwen, never turns missing model
++  output into FINALIZE, and never writes M9 from model-generated text.
++- **MCP:** original 7 tools remain unchanged. When Qwen is configured the server additionally
++  exposes `run_agent_turn` (8th tool, `{"query": str}`). It uses `HttpPlannerProposer` +
++  `run_planner` + `ElectronicsPlannerExecutor` (real catalogue/BGE, Scorer, CandidateLedger,
++  Eligibility, Gate, P15 evidence, P16 registry). It emits MCP progress notifications for
++  `Qwen Planner Request`, `Planner Action`, RETRIEVE/RERANK/FINALIZE, Decision Gate and Evidence
++  Preparation; no hidden chain-of-thought. It does not write M9.
++- **Clarification:** a real Qwen `ASK` returns a server-issued `clarification_id` + question.
++  After the existing `submit_clarification_reply` M9 write + preference refresh, the BFF resumes
++  with `run_agent_turn` (not `recommend_products`). If the model does not ASK, no ASK is inserted.

++  `planner: "qwen_base"|"rule"|"none"`, `planner_model`, `planner_calls`, `planner_latency_ms`;
++  `TurnView` persists the same fields. Product/evidence payload shapes are unchanged.

++class _ProgressBridge:
++    """Small event bridge from the synchronous executor/Qwen calls to MCP progress notifications.
++
++    It does not own any Agent state: the existing ``run_planner`` + ``ElectronicsPlannerExecutor``
++    are still the only loop/executor.  Events carry action/tool names, status, counts and real
++    latency only; prompts and hidden reasoning are never sent.
++    """
++
++    def __init__(self, context: Context, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop) -> None:
++        self.context = context
++        self.loop = loop
++        self.counter = 0
++        self.pending: list[asyncio.Future[Any]] = []
++
++    def emit(self, *, tool: str, label: str, status: str,
++             detail: str | None = None, latency_ms: float | None = None) -> None:
++        self.counter = min(99, self.counter + 1)
++        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
++            "tool": tool, "label": label, "status": status, "progress": self.counter,
++        }
++        if detail is not None:
++            payload["detail"] = detail
++        if latency_ms is not None:
++            payload["latency_ms"] = round(float(latency_ms), 2)
++        try:
++            future = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(self._send(payload), self.loop)
++            self.pending.append(future)
++            future.add_done_callback(lambda done: done.exception())
++        except Exception:  # progress is observability; it must never fail the real execution
++            LOGGER.debug("progress notification could not be scheduled", exc_info=True)
++
++    async def flush(self) -> None:
++        """Wait until every queued progress notification has been handed to the transport."""
++        while self.pending:
++            future = self.pending.pop(0)
++            try:
++                await asyncio.wrap_future(future)
++            except Exception:
++                LOGGER.debug("queued progress notification failed", exc_info=True)
++
++    async def _send(self, payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
++        try:
++            await self.context.report_progress(
++                float(payload.get("progress") or 0), 100.0,
++                json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")),
++            )
++        except Exception:  # no progress token / disconnected client: execution continues
++            LOGGER.debug("progress notification could not be delivered", exc_info=True)
++
++
++class _RecordingQwenPlanner:
++    """Record real model calls/actions while delegating the decision to HttpPlannerProposer."""
++
++    def __init__(self, inner: HttpPlannerProposer, bridge: _ProgressBridge, model: str) -> None:
++        self.inner = inner
++        self.bridge = bridge
++        self.model = model
++        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
++
++    def propose(self, observation: PlannerObservation):  # noqa: ANN201 - existing protocol
++        step = len(self.calls) + 1
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="running",
++                         detail=f"{self.model}; step {step}")
++        started = time.perf_counter()
++        try:
++            proposal = self.inner.propose(observation)
++        except Exception as exc:
++            self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="error",
++                             detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
++                             latency_ms=(time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0)
++            raise
++        response = dict(getattr(self.inner, "last_response", {}) or {})
++        latency_ms = response.get("latency_ms")
++        if latency_ms is None:
++            latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="ok",
++                         detail=f"{self.model}; step {step}", latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        self.calls.append({
++            "step": step,
++            "action": proposal.action.value,
++            "arguments": dict(proposal.arguments),
++            "rationale": proposal.rationale,
++            "latency_ms": round(float(latency_ms), 2),
++            "input_tokens": response.get("input_tokens"),
++            "output_tokens": response.get("output_tokens"),
++            "parser_errors": list(response.get("errors") or ()),
++            "raw_output": str(response.get("text") or "")[:2000],
++        })
++        args = dict(proposal.arguments)
++        visible = args.get("query") or args.get("question") or ""
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="planner_action",
++                         label=f"Planner Action: {proposal.action.value}", status="ok",
++                         detail=(f"step {step}" + (f"; {visible}" if visible else "")),
++                         latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        return proposal
++

++class _ProgressExecutor:
++    """Emit real tool-start/end events around the existing executor's execute()."""
++
++    _LABELS = {
++        "ASK": "ASK", "RETRIEVE": "RETRIEVE", "RERANK": "RERANK", "FINALIZE": "FINALIZE",
++    }
++
++    def __init__(self, inner: ElectronicsPlannerExecutor, bridge: _ProgressBridge,
++                 holder: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
++        self.inner = inner
++        self.bridge = bridge
++        self.holder = holder
++        self.counter = 0
++
++    def execute(self, proposal, observation):  # noqa: ANN001, ANN201 - existing protocol
++        action = proposal.action.value
++        self.counter += 1
++        tool = f"{action.lower()}_{self.counter}"
++        self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="running",
++                         detail="existing ElectronicsPlannerExecutor")
++        started = time.perf_counter()
++        try:
++            after, tool_observation, terminal = self.inner.execute(proposal, observation)
++        except Exception as exc:
++            self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="error",
++                             detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
++                             latency_ms=(time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0)
++            raise
++        if self.holder is not None:
++            self.holder["observation"] = after
++            self.holder["terminal"] = terminal
++        latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
++        obs = dict(tool_observation or {})
++        detail_parts = [str(obs.get("status") or "done")]
++        if "candidate_count" in obs:
++            detail_parts.append(f"{obs['candidate_count']} candidates")
++        self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="ok",
++                         detail=" - ".join(detail_parts), latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        return after, tool_observation, terminal
++

++async def _run_agent_turn(
++    query: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, description="Current natural-language shopping query")],
++    ctx: Context,
++    clarification_id: Annotated[
++        str | None,
++        Field(description="Set only when resuming the same Qwen task after a real user clarification reply"),
++    ] = None,
++) -> dict[str, Any]:
++    """Run the existing electronics executor with the real Qwen3.5-9B Base planner.
++
++    The model's PlannerAction(s) are parsed by the existing contract and executed by
++    ``ElectronicsPlannerExecutor``.  No Rule fallback exists: a missing/invalid model action is a
++    typed tool error.  M9 writes are not performed here; clarification replies still go through
++    ``submit_clarification_reply``.
++
++    When ``clarification_id`` is supplied, the exact executor/observation (CandidateLedger, tool
++    observations, budgets) created before the real Qwen ASK is reused; only M9-derived preferences
++    are refreshed, then Qwen is called again for a new PlannerAction.
++    """
++    started = time.perf_counter()
++    config = _qwen_config()
++    if not config["configured"]:
++        raise ToolError(
++            "qwen_unavailable: QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED/AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER and a model endpoint "
++            "must both be configured"
++        )
++    context = _context()
++    history = context.read_history()
++    task = context.current_task(query)
++    continuation: dict[str, Any] | None = None
++    if clarification_id is not None:
++        with context.lock:
++            state = context.handles.get(str(clarification_id))
++            if state is None:
++                raise ToolError("invalid_or_expired_clarification_id")
++            if state.user_key != context.user_key:
++                raise ToolError("clarification_id belongs to another authorized user")
++            if time.time() > state.expires_at:
++                state.status = "expired"
++                raise ToolError("clarification_id_expired")
++            if state.status != "answered":
++                raise ToolError("clarification_reply_required: no real user reply was persisted yet")
++            continuation = state.continuation
++            if continuation is None:
++                raise ToolError("clarification_already_resumed: this handle has no pending agent continuation")
++            if str(continuation.get("query") or "") != str(query):
++                raise ToolError("clarification_query_mismatch: resume must keep the original query")
++            state.continuation = None
++            state.status = "resumed"
++        task = dict(continuation["task"])
++        history = context.read_history()
++    proposer = HttpPlannerProposer(
++        str(config["url"]), model=str(config["model"]), timeout=float(config["timeout_s"])
++    )
++    explainer = None
++    if config["explain"]:
++        explainer = EvidenceExplainer(
++            QwenExplanationClient(str(config["url"]), timeout=float(config["timeout_s"]))
++        )
++    prior_planner_calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
++    if continuation is not None:
++        executor = continuation["executor"]
++        executor.current_query = query
++        executor.preferences = prepare_preferences(history)
++        executor.memory_history = history
++        executor.ask_question = None
++        executor.terminal_status = "in_progress"
++        # If the exact real user reply did not remove the old conflicting preference (M9 treats
++        # "avoid wired" as a new AVOID entry, not a retraction), the next Qwen step must still be
++        # allowed to ASK again instead of being forced to FINALIZE.  No preference is synthesized.
++        unresolved_conflicts = conflicts(executor.preferences)
++        LOGGER.info(
++            "Qwen resume clarification=%s preferences=%s unresolved_conflicts=%s",
++            clarification_id, executor.preferences, unresolved_conflicts,
++        )
++        prior_observation = continuation["observation"]
++        observation = replace(
++            prior_observation,
++            terminal=False,
++            needs_user_input=bool(unresolved_conflicts),
++            allowed_actions=(),
++            m5_state={
++                **prior_observation.m5_state,
++                "phase": PHASE_FINALIZE,
++                "preferences": executor.preferences,
++                "gate_action": "NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION" if unresolved_conflicts else "USER_CLARIFICATION_REPLIED",
++                "ask_question": None,
++                "clarification_id": clarification_id,
++                "preference_conflicts": unresolved_conflicts,
++            },
++        )
++        prior_planner_calls = list(continuation.get("planner_calls") or [])
++    else:
++        executor = ElectronicsPlannerExecutor(
++            catalogue=context.catalogue, current_query=query,
++            preferences=prepare_preferences(history), hard=prepare_hard(query),
++            task_context=task, memory_history=history, explainer=explainer,
++        )
++        observation = PlannerObservation(
++            step=0, query=query,
++            m5_state={"phase": PHASE_PRODUCT_RETRIEVE, "next_query": query,
++                      "preferences": executor.preferences},
++        )
++    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
++    bridge = _ProgressBridge(ctx, loop)
++    planner = _RecordingQwenPlanner(proposer, bridge, str(config["model"]))
++    state_holder: dict[str, Any] = {}
++    progress_executor = _ProgressExecutor(executor, bridge, holder=state_holder)
++
++    def _run_sync():  # executed in a worker thread; progress is bridged to the MCP loop
++        run = run_planner(
++            planner=planner, executor=progress_executor,
++            initial_observation=observation, max_steps=int(config["max_steps"]),
++        )
++        state_holder["run"] = run
++        return run
++
++    try:
++        run = await asyncio.to_thread(_run_sync)
++    except HttpPlannerError as exc:
++        raise ToolError(f"qwen_planner_error: {exc.code}: {exc.detail}") from exc
++    except PlannerContractError as exc:
++        raise ToolError(f"qwen_planner_contract_error: {exc.code}: {exc.detail}") from exc
++    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - real executor/transport failure, no Rule fallback
++        raise ToolError(f"agent_execution_error: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
++
++    status = executor.terminal_status
++    if status in {"not_started", "in_progress", "need_more_candidates", "need_more_product_evidence"}:
++        status = "budget_exhausted"
++        executor.terminal_status = status
++    selected = executor.selected_candidate_id
++    gate = executor.last_decision
++    if gate is not None:
++        bridge.emit(tool="decision_gate", label="Decision Gate", status="ok",
++                    detail=f"{gate.get('action')} ({gate.get('reason_code')})")
++    bundle = None
++    if selected:
++        bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="running",
++                    detail="P15 evidence bundle")
++        try:
++            bundle = build_bundle(
++                task=task, selected_item_id=selected, catalogue=context.catalogue,
++                history=history, gate_decision=gate or {},
++                search_mode=context.search_mode,
++                ledger_source=f"CatalogSearchSource(mode={context.search_mode})",
++            ).as_dict()
++            bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="ok",
++                        detail=f"{len(bundle.get('evidence') or [])} evidence items")
++        except Exception as exc:  # evidence failure must be visible, not replaced
++            bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="error",
++                        detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
++            bundle = None
++
++    clarification = None
++    if status == "awaiting_clarification" and executor.ask_question:
++        clarification_id = "qclar_" + uuid.uuid4().hex
++        now = time.time()
++        final_observation = state_holder.get("observation") or observation
++        state = ClarificationState(
++            clarification_id=clarification_id, user_key=context.user_key,
++            question=str(executor.ask_question), current_query=query, task=task,
++            created_at=now, expires_at=now + context.clarification_ttl_seconds,
++            continuation={
++                "executor": executor,
++                "observation": final_observation,
++                "query": query,
++                "task": task,
++                "planner_calls": prior_planner_calls + list(planner.calls),
++            },
++        )
++        with context.lock:
++            context.handles[clarification_id] = state
++        clarification = {
++            "clarification_id": clarification_id,
++            "question": str(executor.ask_question),
++            "reason": "qwen_planner_ask",
++            "expires_in_seconds": context.clarification_ttl_seconds,
++            "memory_write_enabled": context.writes_enabled,
++        }
++
++    candidate_ids: list[str] = []
++    for item in (executor.last_search or {}).get("hit_ids") or ():
++        item_id = str(item)
++        if item_id and item_id not in candidate_ids:
++            candidate_ids.append(item_id)
++    for item in (observation.candidate_ids or ()):
++        item_id = str(item)
++        if item_id and item_id not in candidate_ids:
++            candidate_ids.append(item_id)
++    candidate_ids = candidate_ids[:20]
++
++    rag = None
++    if executor.last_explanation is not None:
++        explanation = executor.last_explanation
++        rag = {
++            "status": explanation.get("status"),
++            "final_answer": explanation.get("final_answer"),
++            "evidence_bundle": explanation.get("bundle"),
++            "claim_validation": explanation.get("validation"),
++            "model_stats": explanation.get("model_stats"),
++            "error": executor.explanation_error,
++        }
++    elif executor.explanation_error:
++        rag = {"status": "error", "final_answer": None, "error": executor.explanation_error}
++
++    latency_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2)
++    await bridge.flush()
++    return {
++        "schema": "agentrecx.p17.run_agent_turn.v1",
++        "status": status,
++        "query": query,
++        "resumed": bool(continuation is not None),
++        "resumed_from_clarification_id": clarification_id if continuation is not None else None,

++/**
++ * Merges one real `tool_status` event into the timeline.
++ *
++ * `running` opens a row (a repeated identical notification is not duplicated). A terminal status
++ * closes the newest matching running row; rows are matched on tool *and* label first because a
++ * Qwen turn legitimately repeats the same tool with a different label per planner action
++ * (`planner_action` → "Planner Action: RETRIEVE", then "Planner Action: RERANK", …). If the
++ * backend only ever sent a terminal status, it is appended as its own completed row — never
++ * dropped, never turned into a fake animation.
++ */
++export function mergeToolStatus(list: LocalToolStatus[], incoming: ToolStatus): LocalToolStatus[] {
++  if (incoming.status === "running") {
++    const last = list[list.length - 1];
++    if (last && last.status === "running" && last.tool === incoming.tool && last.label === incoming.label) {
++      // MCP progress notifications may repeat; refresh the open row instead of duplicating it.
++      return replaceRow(list, list.length - 1, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() });
++    }
++    return [...list, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() }];
++  }
++  let fallbackIndex = -1;
++  for (let index = list.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
++    const row = list[index];
++    if (row.tool !== incoming.tool || row.status !== "running") continue;
++    if (row.label === incoming.label) return replaceRow(list, index, incoming);
++    if (fallbackIndex < 0) fallbackIndex = index;
++  }
++  if (fallbackIndex >= 0) return replaceRow(list, fallbackIndex, incoming);
++  return [...list, { ...incoming }];
++}
++
++/**
++ * Closes progress rows whose completion the backend never reported.
++ *
++ * A finished turn (SSE `done`, an aborted stream, or a persisted turn restored from the session)
++ * cannot still have a running step. Such a row is shown as browser-local `unknown` with an
++ * explicit note — never as an endless spinner (fake animation) and never as a fabricated "ok".
++ */
++export function finaliseStaleToolRows(list: LocalToolStatus[]): LocalToolStatus[] {
++  if (!list.some((row) => row.status === "running")) return list;
++  return list.map((row) =>
++    row.status === "running"
++      ? {
++          ...row,
++          status: "unknown",
++          detail: [row.detail, "Completion was not reported by the backend before the turn ended."]
++            .filter(Boolean)
++            .join(" · "),
++        }
++      : row,
++  );
++}

+# Qwen Clarification Consistency Fix — Report
+
+**Date:** 2026-11-10 (Asia/Shanghai)  
+**Final verdict:** **GO** for the requested task-scoped clarification-consistency scope.
+
+The real Qwen ASK path now treats an explicit current-purchase exclusion from the user's
+clarification reply as authoritative for the resumed task, re-scores the preserved candidates
+against that constraint, and prevents a final product with trustworthy evidence of the excluded
+attribute. Historical M9 memory is not deleted; an explicit `Forget my wired preference.` still
+uses the existing M9 retraction semantics.
+
+**Correction (2026-11-10, candidate-diagnosis pass):** the previous draft reported `B00NFL0E2A`
+as the browser-selected product because the E2E assertion read the first card in the *initial
+candidate block* on the same turn. The actual Gate-selected item in both Web and Direct MCP runs is
+`B00M0KUF44` (`RunPhones Wireless Headphones...`), which has explicit catalogue `wireless` evidence.
+The authoritative UI signal is the `gate-selected-item` banner; the initial candidate cards remain an
+earlier catalogue-result block. No runtime ranking defect or final-product quality difference exists
+in the saved evidence. This correction is recorded in
+`runs/web_demo/final_polish/candidate_diagnosis.json`.
+
+---
+
+## 1. Root cause
+
+The previous Qwen ASK → M9 → resume implementation had three separate gaps:
+
+1. **No current-task constraint layer.**
+   M9 correctly stored the reply as `wireless = prefer` and `wired = avoid`, but `avoid` is a new
+   avoidance entry, not an explicit retraction. The old `wired = prefer` entry remained active.
+   The resumed executor only used the active-M9 preference list; there was no separate representation
+   of "for this purchase, wired is excluded" that takes priority over the old historical preference.
+
+2. **The resume refreshed preferences but reused stale candidate-derived state.**
+   The same executor and CandidateLedger were preserved, which is required, but
+   `last_rows`, `_rows_by_rank` and `last_eligibility` had been produced before the clarification
+   reply. On `FINALIZE`, the executor saw non-empty `last_rows` and skipped a fresh
+   `_score_and_inspect`. Gate therefore received candidate rows whose preference conflicts/eligibility
+   predated the reply. The previously selected `B091SF1545` was such a stale row.
+
+3. **Product attributes were not carried into scorer rows.**
+   `candidate_rows()` gave the scorer product attributes, but the scorer output did not retain the
+   attribute evidence on each row. A task-level check could not see that
+   `B091SF1545`'s title has direct `wired` evidence. Even with refreshed M9 preferences, the
+   reconciled score row lacked the evidence needed to classify it as a task conflict.
+
+The old result was therefore not evidence for changing M9/Gate wholesale; a task-scoped constraint
+layer plus a true post-clarification re-evaluation was required.
+
+---
+
+## 2. Fix
+
+### 2.1 Task-scoped current constraints
+
+New `recommendation/preference/current_task_constraints.py`:
+
+- Derives **current-task hard exclusions** from the current reply's M9 extraction:
+  - `avoid wired headphones` → hard exclusion `attribute = wired`, provenance `"Please avoid wired headphones."`;
+  - `prefer wireless headphones` → current soft preference;
+  - explicit removals are not converted into current exclusions.
+- `effective_preferences()` creates the current task's preference view:
+  - removes the historical **positive** `wired` from the task view only;
+  - adds the current `wired` negative;
+  - never mutates or deletes the M9 entry.
+- `CurrentTaskConstraints` is stored on the P17 clarification handle and copied into the Qwen
+  continuation snapshot, so it survives the reply and is available on resume.
+- The same reply extraction comes from the accepted M9 extractor through the P16 clarification
+  skill (`reply_extraction`), not a separate hard-coded parser.
+
+### 2.2 Product evidence matching
+
+The executor now copies trusted `ElectronicsProduct.attributes` and `form_factor` into each scored
+candidate row. `candidate_constraint_status()` then checks:
+
+- explicit `wired` → task violation;
+- explicit `wireless` → positive/soft match, **not** proof that an unknown `wired` value is absent;
+- no connection evidence → `UNKNOWN`, never fabricated as satisfied;
+- brand exclusions only when a trusted brand fact/evidence exists;
+- negated evidence such as `not wired` is not treated as a positive wired match;
+- product type/role continues through the existing Product Type/Role path (main vs accessory).
+
+No single ASIN is special-cased. The matcher is attribute/value based over real catalogue evidence.
+
+### 2.3 Post-clarification re-evaluation
+
+`ElectronicsPlannerExecutor.refresh_for_task()`:
+
+- retains the same executor, CandidateLedger, retrieval history and tool budgets;
+- refreshes M9 historical preferences from a new `HistoryStateReader` read;
+- creates the current-task effective preference list;
+- re-scores the preserved candidate IDs with the latest preferences;
+- populates task-constraint violations/unknown status;
+- excludes task-violating rows from `last_rows`/planner/Finalize eligibility;
+- returns an auditable `candidate_reevaluation` record with input count, excluded IDs and latency.
+
+MCP `run_agent_turn` calls this method immediately before the resumed real Qwen `/plan`, so the
+model cannot see stale candidate eligibility or stale Gate inputs.
+
+### 2.4 Gate final consistency check
+
+`DecisionGate` now receives task constraints and:
+
+- filters candidate rows carrying `task_constraint_conflicts` before choosing a final candidate;
+- returns a bounded/retry gate decision if every candidate is task-excluded;
+- reports `task_constraint_status` (`none|verified|unknown|violated`) in its decision;
+- the executor additionally checks the selected row's task conflicts immediately before
+  presentation.
+
+The existing hard/soft Gate rules and V0 scorer weights were not rewritten.
+
+---
+
+## 3. Before/after evidence
+
+### Before the fix (recorded in `QWEN_ASK_RESUME_REPORT.md`)
+
+- exact reply: M9 `added=2, removed=0`;
+- old `wired — prefer` retained;
+- stale candidate row `B091SF1545` (title `Earphones Wired Headphones Headphones Earbuds.`) was
+  Gate-approved;
+- final product was explicitly wired.
+
+### After the fix
+
+Real browser run `browser/summary.json`:
+
+- M9 reply update: `added=2, removed=0`; old `wired — prefer` remains **active** in M9;
+- current task hard exclusion:
+  `wired`, source `"Please avoid wired headphones."`, source turn `qclar...`;
+- candidate re-evaluation: `input=15, eligible=14, excluded=["B091SF1545"], violations=1`;
+- no second clarification/handle; no `Forget` message required;
+- final Gate: `ALLOW_FINALIZE`;
+- final **Gate-selected** browser product: `B00M0KUF44`, title
+  `RunPhones Wireless Headphones...`; catalogue attributes include explicit `wireless`;
+- the initial catalogue-result block also contained `B00NFL0E2A` (`Philmore Headphones.`), but it
+  was not the Gate-selected item. The E2E assertion was corrected to read the dedicated
+  `gate-selected-item` banner instead of the first `product-card`.
+
+Direct real-MCP run (`mcp_direct_cycle.json`):
+
+- same task constraint and reevaluation;
+- excluded `B091SF1545`;
+- final selected product `B00M0KUF44`, title `RunPhones Wireless Headphones...`, catalogue
+  attributes include explicit `wireless`;
+- Gate `ALLOW_FINALIZE`, P15 evidence bundle present.
+
+**Candidate diagnosis:** Web and Direct MCP produced the same valid candidate IDs after
+re-evaluation (`B00M0KUF44, B0BVZKKYBS, B00NFL0E2A, ...`, 14 eligible; `B091SF1545` excluded) and
+the same final selected product `B00M0KUF44`. The earlier apparent difference was therefore a
+**test/reporting artifact**, not a different candidate pool or a scorer defect. `B00M0KUF44`
+(explicit wireless evidence) ranked before `B00NFL0E2A` (wireless UNKNOWN); no Scorer, Gate or V0
+weight change was required.
+
+### Memory semantics comparison (`memory_semantics_compare.json`)
+
+1. Current task exclusion:
+   - M9 update `added=2, removed=0`;
+   - old `wired` preference remains active in M9;
+   - task constraint is stored separately.
+2. Explicit `Forget my wired preference.`:
+   - M9 update `removed=1, removal_directives=1`;
+   - old `wired` becomes `inactive/removed`;
+   - no task hard exclusion is synthesized from the Forget turn itself.
+
+This is the required distinction: **avoid for this purchase ≠ permanent retraction**.
+
+---
+
+## 4. Real Qwen / Web E2E
+
+Real Qwen3.5-9B Base on the existing RTX 4090, no adapter, real `/plan`, real P17 MCP,
+real BFF/SSE/Web, isolated `profile_0056_ask`.
+
+- Qwen emitted a real ASK:
+  `I found conflicting preferences in your history (wired, wireless). Which one should take priority for this purchase?`
+- Browser submitted the prescribed single reply:
+  `For this purchase, I prefer wireless headphones for commuting. Please avoid wired headphones.`
+- Qwen was called again with a new real `/plan`; it selected `FINALIZE`.
+- Gate returned `ALLOW_FINALIZE`.
+- Result: **one reply completed the task without the old extra "Forget" step**.
+
+Screenshots:
+
+- [first ASK](runs/web_demo/qwen_clarification_fix/browser/qwen-fix-first-ask.png)
+- [after one reply / no follow-up](runs/web_demo/qwen_clarification_fix/browser/qwen-fix-after-reply.png)
+- [final resumed Gate/product](runs/web_demo/qwen_clarification_fix/browser/qwen-fix-resume.png)
+
+---
+
+## 5. Tests and regression
+
+| Test | Result |
+|---|---|
+| New task-constraint tests (`test_qwen_clarification_consistency.py`) | **6 passed** |
+| Targeted P14-B1 Gate / P14-C0 executor / role / P15/P16/P17 / Qwen unit/BFF / Web unit | **93 passed**, 158.68 s |
+| Full legacy Playwright E2E `01`–`07` | **9/9 passed**, 2.3 min |

+| Frontend `tsc --noEmit` + production build | **PASS** |
+| CPU Rule / Qwen Replay separation | included in 9/9; no regression |
+| Qwen Live real tests `07` | 2/2 passed in the 9/9 run |
+
+The new tests cover:
+
+- historical positive overridden only in the current effective task view;
+- historical M9 entry not mutated;
+- current exclusion vs M9 explicit retraction;
+- wired/wireless/UNKNOWN/negated evidence;
+- brand exclusion requiring trusted brand evidence;
+- Gate filtering all task-conflicting candidates and selecting the non-conflicting candidate;
+- no ASIN-specific logic in production code.
+
+Performance:
+
+- post-clarification candidate re-evaluation: **~1 ms** in the real direct run (`1.05 ms`),
+  plus normal Qwen/BFF latency (browser total 69.9 s including real ASK + resumed Qwen + Gate).
+- No additional retrieval was needed; CandidateLedger/retrieval budget was preserved.
+

+-## 6. Tests and verification
++## 6. Qwen Base Live (real GPU validated 2026-11-09)
++
++This section now has **real GPU validation**, not just a contract wiring:
++RTX 4090 24 GB, Qwen3.5-9B Base (`/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3.5-9B-ms`, no SFT adapter), real
++`/plan` calls, real MCP `run_agent_turn`, real executor/Gate/P15, and two real browser Qwen Live
++turns. Full evidence: [`QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md`](QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md) and
++`runs/web_demo/qwen_live/`.
++
++This is an **optional** live mode over the existing P17 MCP / ElectronicsPlannerExecutor stack.
++It is not a new Agent loop: the real Qwen3.5-9B Base returns a PlannerAction (`ASK`, `RETRIEVE`,
++`RERANK`, `FINALIZE`), and the existing executor/search/scorer/ledger/eligibility/Gate/P15/M9
++components execute it. A Rule→Qwen rewrite or Qwen→Rule fallback is not implemented.
++
++### Configuration
++
++Set these in the **same Windows PowerShell session** that launches the demo (the launcher forwards
++them into WSL; the endpoint is never shown in the browser):
++
++```powershell
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED = "1"                       # explicit opt-in
++$env:QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1:18009"  # existing model server, NOT auto-started
++$env:QWEN_MODEL_NAME = "Qwen3.5-9B-Base"
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_TIMEOUT_S = "180"                    # each /plan call
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_S = "60"             # real PlannerAction health probe
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_TURN_TIMEOUT_S = "600"              # bounds the complete multi-step turn
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_MAX_STEPS = "8"
++powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts/run_web_demo.ps1
++```
++
++Availability is deliberately strict: the BFF performs a real `POST /plan` health probe and only
++shows Qwen Live as available after it parses a valid PlannerAction. A configured-but-unreachable
++service leaves **Qwen Live Unavailable**; CPU Live and Qwen Replay continue to work, and a Qwen
++request never silently becomes a Rule request. The BFF does **not** start/stop AutoDL/GPU, create
++SSH tunnels, download or quantize a model. Start the model service yourself, using the repository's
++`training/agent_sft/model_server.py` or your existing endpoint.
++
++The MCP server exposes the original 7 tools plus one conditional 8th tool,
++`run_agent_turn`, only when Qwen Live is configured. `run_agent_turn` returns the model's real
++actions, tool observations, Gate result, P15 evidence, planner/explainer latencies and the
++optional Qwen clarification handle. `/api/chat/stream` accepts
++`{"mode":"qwen_live", ...}` and emits the same SSE event names plus planner metadata on `done`.
++

++## 8. P20 Qwen Base Live (optional real mode)
++
++This section extends, and does not replace, the CPU/Replay contract above.
++
++- **Explicit opt-in only:** `QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED=1` AND a configured `QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT`
++  (aliases accepted internally: `AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER`, `AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER_URL`).
++  Optional: `QWEN_MODEL_NAME`, `QWEN_LIVE_TIMEOUT_S`, `QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_S`,
++  `QWEN_LIVE_TURN_TIMEOUT_S`, `QWEN_LIVE_MAX_STEPS`, `QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_INTERVAL_S`,
++  `QWEN_LIVE_EXPLAIN`. The endpoint is never exposed in health/config/SSE or logs.
++- **Health:** the BFF performs a real `POST {endpoint}/plan` with the existing planner contract and
++  only marks `qwen_live.available=true` when the response contains a valid PlannerAction for that
++  observation. The snapshot (`/api/health` → `qwen_live`) reports enabled/configured/status/
++  available/model/planner_action/latency_ms/checked_at/detail. No health pass → Unavailable.
++- **No fallback:** Qwen mode requests are sent only after availability; a `run_agent_turn` failure
++  is a real `qwen_*` error. The BFF never runs Rule and labels it Qwen, never turns missing model
++  output into FINALIZE, and never writes M9 from model-generated text.
++- **MCP:** original 7 tools remain unchanged. When Qwen is configured the server additionally
++  exposes `run_agent_turn` (8th tool, `{"query": str}`). It uses `HttpPlannerProposer` +
++  `run_planner` + `ElectronicsPlannerExecutor` (real catalogue/BGE, Scorer, CandidateLedger,
++  Eligibility, Gate, P15 evidence, P16 registry). It emits MCP progress notifications for
++  `Qwen Planner Request`, `Planner Action`, RETRIEVE/RERANK/FINALIZE, Decision Gate and Evidence
++  Preparation; no hidden chain-of-thought. It does not write M9.
++- **Clarification:** a real Qwen `ASK` returns a server-issued `clarification_id` + question.
++  After the existing `submit_clarification_reply` M9 write + preference refresh, the BFF resumes
++  with `run_agent_turn` (not `recommend_products`). If the model does not ASK, no ASK is inserted.
++- **SSE additions:** real progress `tool_status` rows (as above); `done` may include
++  `planner: "qwen_base"|"rule"|"none"`, `planner_model`, `planner_calls`, `planner_latency_ms`;
++  `TurnView` persists the same fields. Product/evidence payload shapes are unchanged.
++- **Errors:** `qwen_unavailable`, `qwen_planner_error`, `qwen_planner_contract_error`,
++  `agent_execution_error`; all visible to the UI. CPU/Replay remain available.
++- **Tests:** `tests/test_qwen_live_unit.py`, `tests/test_qwen_live_bff.py`,
++  gated `tests/test_qwen_live_mcp_integration.py` (real catalogue + MCP + deterministic HTTP
++  planner double, clearly not Qwen). Real browser Qwen turns were validated on 2026-11-09 with
++  real Qwen3.5-9B Base on an RTX 4090 and the real browser/BFF/MCP/executor stack; see
++  `QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md` and `runs/web_demo/qwen_live/e2e_real/`. The real ASK→M9→resume branch
++  was exercised on 2026-11-10 on an isolated conflict profile; a follow-up fix makes the current
++  clarification reply a task-scoped hard exclusion, re-evaluates the preserved candidates and
++  prevents an explicit-wired final item while preserving historical M9 audit entries. See
++  `QWEN_CLARIFICATION_FIX_REPORT.md` (GO for that scope; unknown wireless evidence remains UNKNOWN).

+     def _load_encoder(self):
+-        if self._model is None:
++        """Load the encoder exactly once; concurrent callers wait for the same completed state.
++
++        A failed initialisation is retried at most ``MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS`` times by later callers;
++        after that a clear error is raised instead of re-using a partially loaded model.  The model
++        object is published only after the tokenizer, model and ``eval()`` all succeed.
++        """
++        if self._encoder_ready.is_set():
++            return self._tokenizer, self._model
++        if self._encoder_error and self._encoder_attempts >= MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS:
++            raise RuntimeError(
++                "BGE encoder initialization failed after "
++                f"{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS} attempts: {self._encoder_error}"
++            )
++        with self._encoder_lock:
++            # Double-checked after acquiring the lock: another caller may have loaded it while we
++            # were waiting.
++            if self._encoder_ready.is_set():
++                return self._tokenizer, self._model
++            if self._encoder_error and self._encoder_attempts >= MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS:
++                raise RuntimeError(
++                    "BGE encoder initialization failed after "
++                    f"{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS} attempts: {self._encoder_error}"
++                )
++            self._encoder_attempts += 1
++            self._encoder_loading = True
++            started = time.perf_counter()
++            try:
++                import torch
++                from transformers import AutoModel, AutoTokenizer
++
++                tokenizer_started = time.perf_counter()
++                tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(self.model_path)
++                self._tokenizer_load_ms = round(
++                    (time.perf_counter() - tokenizer_started) * 1000.0, 2
++                )
++                model_started = time.perf_counter()
++                model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(self.model_path, torch_dtype=torch.float32)
++                model.eval()
++                self._model_load_ms = round(
++                    (time.perf_counter() - model_started) * 1000.0, 2
++                )
++                # Publish only after every piece is fully initialised.
++                self._tokenizer = tokenizer
++                self._model = model
++                self._encoder_error = None
++                self._encoder_ready.set()
++                return tokenizer, model
++            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - explicit error; caller may retry once
++                self._tokenizer = None
++                self._model = None
++                self._encoder_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
++                raise RuntimeError(
++                    "BGE encoder initialization failed "
++                    f"(attempt {self._encoder_attempts}/{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS}): "
++                    f"{self._encoder_error}"
++                ) from exc
++            finally:
++                self._encoder_loading = False
++                self._encoder_load_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2)
++

++class _ProgressBridge:
++    """Small event bridge from the synchronous executor/Qwen calls to MCP progress notifications.
++
++    It does not own any Agent state: the existing ``run_planner`` + ``ElectronicsPlannerExecutor``
++    are still the only loop/executor.  Events carry action/tool names, status, counts and real
++    latency only; prompts and hidden reasoning are never sent.
++    """
++
++    def __init__(self, context: Context, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop) -> None:
++        self.context = context
++        self.loop = loop
++        self.counter = 0
++        self.pending: list[asyncio.Future[Any]] = []
++
++    def emit(self, *, tool: str, label: str, status: str,
++             detail: str | None = None, latency_ms: float | None = None) -> None:
++        self.counter = min(99, self.counter + 1)
++        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
++            "tool": tool, "label": label, "status": status, "progress": self.counter,
++        }
++        if detail is not None:
++            payload["detail"] = detail
++        if latency_ms is not None:
++            payload["latency_ms"] = round(float(latency_ms), 2)
++        try:
++            future = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(self._send(payload), self.loop)
++            self.pending.append(future)
++            future.add_done_callback(lambda done: done.exception())
++        except Exception:  # progress is observability; it must never fail the real execution
++            LOGGER.debug("progress notification could not be scheduled", exc_info=True)
++
++    async def flush(self) -> None:
++        """Wait until every queued progress notification has been handed to the transport."""
++        while self.pending:
++            future = self.pending.pop(0)
++            try:
++                await asyncio.wrap_future(future)
++            except Exception:
++                LOGGER.debug("queued progress notification failed", exc_info=True)
++
++    async def _send(self, payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
++        try:
++            await self.context.report_progress(
++                float(payload.get("progress") or 0), 100.0,
++                json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")),
++            )
++        except Exception:  # no progress token / disconnected client: execution continues
++            LOGGER.debug("progress notification could not be delivered", exc_info=True)

++class _RecordingQwenPlanner:
++    """Record real model calls/actions while delegating the decision to HttpPlannerProposer."""
++
++    def __init__(self, inner: HttpPlannerProposer, bridge: _ProgressBridge, model: str) -> None:
++        self.inner = inner
++        self.bridge = bridge
++        self.model = model
++        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
++
++    def propose(self, observation: PlannerObservation):  # noqa: ANN201 - existing protocol
++        step = len(self.calls) + 1
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="running",
++                         detail=f"{self.model}; step {step}")
++        started = time.perf_counter()
++        try:
++            proposal = self.inner.propose(observation)
++        except Exception as exc:
++            self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="error",
++                             detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
++                             latency_ms=(time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0)
++            raise
++        response = dict(getattr(self.inner, "last_response", {}) or {})
++        latency_ms = response.get("latency_ms")
++        if latency_ms is None:
++            latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="ok",
++                         detail=f"{self.model}; step {step}", latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        self.calls.append({
++            "step": step,
++            "action": proposal.action.value,
++            "arguments": dict(proposal.arguments),
++            "rationale": proposal.rationale,
++            "latency_ms": round(float(latency_ms), 2),
++            "input_tokens": response.get("input_tokens"),
++            "output_tokens": response.get("output_tokens"),
++            "parser_errors": list(response.get("errors") or ()),
++            "raw_output": str(response.get("text") or "")[:2000],
++        })
++        args = dict(proposal.arguments)
++        visible = args.get("query") or args.get("question") or ""
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="planner_action",
++                         label=f"Planner Action: {proposal.action.value}", status="ok",
++                         detail=(f"step {step}" + (f"; {visible}" if visible else "")),
++                         latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        return proposal
++

++class _ProgressExecutor:
++    """Emit real tool-start/end events around the existing executor's execute()."""
++
++    _LABELS = {
++        "ASK": "ASK", "RETRIEVE": "RETRIEVE", "RERANK": "RERANK", "FINALIZE": "FINALIZE",
++    }
++
++    def __init__(self, inner: ElectronicsPlannerExecutor, bridge: _ProgressBridge,
++                 holder: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
++        self.inner = inner
++        self.bridge = bridge
++        self.holder = holder
++        self.counter = 0
++
++    def execute(self, proposal, observation):  # noqa: ANN001, ANN201 - existing protocol
++        action = proposal.action.value
++        self.counter += 1
++        tool = f"{action.lower()}_{self.counter}"
++        self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="running",
++                         detail="existing ElectronicsPlannerExecutor")
++        started = time.perf_counter()
++        try:
++            after, tool_observation, terminal = self.inner.execute(proposal, observation)
++        except Exception as exc:
++            self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="error",
++                             detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
++                             latency_ms=(time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0)
++            raise
++        if self.holder is not None:
++            self.holder["observation"] = after
++            self.holder["terminal"] = terminal
++        latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
++        obs = dict(tool_observation or {})
++        detail_parts = [str(obs.get("status") or "done")]
++        if "candidate_count" in obs:
++            detail_parts.append(f"{obs['candidate_count']} candidates")
++        self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="ok",
++                         detail=" - ".join(detail_parts), latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        return after, tool_observation, terminal
++
