++async def _run_agent_turn(
++    query: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, description="Current natural-language shopping query")],
++    ctx: Context,
++    clarification_id: Annotated[
++        str | None,
++        Field(description="Set only when resuming the same Qwen task after a real user clarification reply"),
++    ] = None,
++) -> dict[str, Any]:
++    """Run the existing electronics executor with the real Qwen3.5-9B Base planner.
++
++    The model's PlannerAction(s) are parsed by the existing contract and executed by
++    ``ElectronicsPlannerExecutor``.  No Rule fallback exists: a missing/invalid model action is a
++    typed tool error.  M9 writes are not performed here; clarification replies still go through
++    ``submit_clarification_reply``.
++
++    When ``clarification_id`` is supplied, the exact executor/observation (CandidateLedger, tool
++    observations, budgets) created before the real Qwen ASK is reused; only M9-derived preferences
++    are refreshed, then Qwen is called again for a new PlannerAction.
++    """
++    started = time.perf_counter()
++    config = _qwen_config()
++    if not config["configured"]:
++        raise ToolError(
++            "qwen_unavailable: QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED/AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER and a model endpoint "
++            "must both be configured"
++        )
++    context = _context()
++    history = context.read_history()
++    task = context.current_task(query)
++    continuation: dict[str, Any] | None = None
++    resume_task_constraints = CurrentTaskConstraints()
++    if clarification_id is not None:
++        with context.lock:
++            state = context.handles.get(str(clarification_id))
++            if state is None:
++                raise ToolError("invalid_or_expired_clarification_id")
++            if state.user_key != context.user_key:
++                raise ToolError("clarification_id belongs to another authorized user")
++            if time.time() > state.expires_at:
++                state.status = "expired"
++                raise ToolError("clarification_id_expired")
++            if state.status != "answered":
++                raise ToolError("clarification_reply_required: no real user reply was persisted yet")
++            continuation = state.continuation
++            if continuation is None:
++                raise ToolError("clarification_already_resumed: this handle has no pending agent continuation")
++            if str(continuation.get("query") or "") != str(query):
++                raise ToolError("clarification_query_mismatch: resume must keep the original query")
++            resume_task_constraints = state.task_constraints
++            state.continuation = None
++            state.status = "resumed"
++        task = dict(continuation["task"])
++        history = context.read_history()
++    proposer = HttpPlannerProposer(
++        str(config["url"]), model=str(config["model"]), timeout=float(config["timeout_s"])
++    )
++    explainer = None
++    if config["explain"]:
++        explainer = EvidenceExplainer(
++            QwenExplanationClient(str(config["url"]), timeout=float(config["timeout_s"]))
++        )
++    prior_planner_calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
++    reevaluation: dict[str, Any] | None = None
++    if continuation is not None:
++        executor = continuation["executor"]
++        executor.current_query = query
++        executor.ask_question = None
++        executor.terminal_status = "in_progress"
++        prior_observation = continuation["observation"]
++        # Re-evaluate the preserved candidate pool against the latest M9 state and the
++        # current task's reply constraints.  CandidateLedger / retrieved IDs / budgets are
++        # untouched; only stale scorer rows, eligibility and Gate-derived state are rebuilt.
++        reevaluation = executor.refresh_for_task(
++            history=history,
++            task_constraints=resume_task_constraints,
++            candidate_ids=list(prior_observation.candidate_ids or ()),
++        )
++        valid_candidate_ids = list(reevaluation.get("candidate_ids") or ())
++        unresolved_conflicts = conflicts(executor.preferences)
++        LOGGER.info(
++            "Qwen resume clarification=%s preferences=%s task_constraints=%s "
++            "reevaluation=%s unresolved_conflicts=%s",
++            clarification_id, executor.preferences,
++            resume_task_constraints.as_dict(), reevaluation, unresolved_conflicts,
++        )
++        observation = replace(
++            prior_observation,
++            terminal=False,
++            needs_user_input=bool(unresolved_conflicts),
++            candidate_ids=tuple(valid_candidate_ids),
++            reranked=bool(valid_candidate_ids),
++            allowed_actions=(),
++            m5_state={
++                **prior_observation.m5_state,
++                "phase": PHASE_FINALIZE if valid_candidate_ids else PHASE_PRODUCT_RETRIEVE,
++                "preferences": executor.preferences,
++                "task_constraints": resume_task_constraints.as_dict(),
++                "gate_action": "NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION" if unresolved_conflicts else "USER_CLARIFICATION_REPLIED",
++                "ask_question": None,
++                "clarification_id": clarification_id,
++                "preference_conflicts": unresolved_conflicts,
++                "candidate_reevaluation": reevaluation,
++            },
++        )
++        prior_planner_calls = list(continuation.get("planner_calls") or [])
++    else:
++        executor = ElectronicsPlannerExecutor(
++            catalogue=context.catalogue, current_query=query,
++            preferences=prepare_preferences(history), hard=prepare_hard(query),
++            task_context=task, memory_history=history, explainer=explainer,
++        )
++        observation = PlannerObservation(
++            step=0, query=query,
++            m5_state={"phase": PHASE_PRODUCT_RETRIEVE, "next_query": query,
++                      "preferences": executor.preferences},
++        )
++    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
++    bridge = _ProgressBridge(ctx, loop)
++    planner = _RecordingQwenPlanner(proposer, bridge, str(config["model"]))
++    state_holder: dict[str, Any] = {}
++    progress_executor = _ProgressExecutor(executor, bridge, holder=state_holder)
++
++    def _run_sync():  # executed in a worker thread; progress is bridged to the MCP loop
++        run = run_planner(
++            planner=planner, executor=progress_executor,
++            initial_observation=observation, max_steps=int(config["max_steps"]),
++        )
++        state_holder["run"] = run
++        return run
++
++    try:
++        run = await asyncio.to_thread(_run_sync)
++    except HttpPlannerError as exc:
++        raise ToolError(f"qwen_planner_error: {exc.code}: {exc.detail}") from exc
++    except PlannerContractError as exc:
++        raise ToolError(f"qwen_planner_contract_error: {exc.code}: {exc.detail}") from exc
++    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - real executor/transport failure, no Rule fallback
++        raise ToolError(f"agent_execution_error: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
++
++    status = executor.terminal_status
++    if status in {"not_started", "in_progress", "need_more_candidates", "need_more_product_evidence"}:
++        status = "budget_exhausted"
++        executor.terminal_status = status
++    selected = executor.selected_candidate_id
++    gate = executor.last_decision
++    if gate is not None:
++        bridge.emit(tool="decision_gate", label="Decision Gate", status="ok",
++                    detail=f"{gate.get('action')} ({gate.get('reason_code')})")
++    bundle = None
++    if selected:
++        bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="running",
++                    detail="P15 evidence bundle")
++        try:
++            bundle = build_bundle(
++                task=task, selected_item_id=selected, catalogue=context.catalogue,
++                history=history, gate_decision=gate or {},
++                search_mode=context.search_mode,
++                ledger_source=f"CatalogSearchSource(mode={context.search_mode})",
++            ).as_dict()
++            bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="ok",
++                        detail=f"{len(bundle.get('evidence') or [])} evidence items")
++        except Exception as exc:  # evidence failure must be visible, not replaced
++            bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="error",
++                        detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
++            bundle = None
++
++    clarification = None
++    if status == "awaiting_clarification" and executor.ask_question:
++        clarification_id = "qclar_" + uuid.uuid4().hex
++        now = time.time()
++        final_observation = state_holder.get("observation") or observation
++        state = ClarificationState(
++            clarification_id=clarification_id, user_key=context.user_key,
++            question=str(executor.ask_question), current_query=query, task=task,
++            created_at=now, expires_at=now + context.clarification_ttl_seconds,
++            task_constraints=executor.task_constraints,
++            continuation={
++                "executor": executor,
++                "observation": final_observation,
++                "query": query,
++                "task": task,
++                "task_constraints": executor.task_constraints.as_dict(),
++                "planner_calls": prior_planner_calls + list(planner.calls),
++            },
++        )
++        with context.lock:
++            context.handles[clarification_id] = state
++        clarification = {
++            "clarification_id": clarification_id,
++            "question": str(executor.ask_question),
++            "reason": "qwen_planner_ask",
++            "expires_in_seconds": context.clarification_ttl_seconds,
++            "memory_write_enabled": context.writes_enabled,
++        }
++
++    candidate_ids: list[str] = []
++    for item in (executor.last_search or {}).get("hit_ids") or ():
++        item_id = str(item)
++        if item_id and item_id not in candidate_ids:
++            candidate_ids.append(item_id)
++    for item in (observation.candidate_ids or ()):
++        item_id = str(item)
++        if item_id and item_id not in candidate_ids:
++            candidate_ids.append(item_id)
++    candidate_ids = candidate_ids[:20]
++
++    rag = None
++    if executor.last_explanation is not None:
++        explanation = executor.last_explanation
++        rag = {
++            "status": explanation.get("status"),
++            "final_answer": explanation.get("final_answer"),
++            "evidence_bundle": explanation.get("bundle"),
++            "claim_validation": explanation.get("validation"),
++            "model_stats": explanation.get("model_stats"),
++            "error": executor.explanation_error,
++        }
++    elif executor.explanation_error:
++        rag = {"status": "error", "final_answer": None, "error": executor.explanation_error}
++
++    latency_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2)
++    await bridge.flush()
++    return {
++        "schema": "agentrecx.p17.run_agent_turn.v1",
++        "status": status,
++        "query": query,
++        "resumed": bool(continuation is not None),
++        "resumed_from_clarification_id": clarification_id if continuation is not None else None,
++        "task_constraints": executor.task_constraints.as_dict(),
++        "candidate_reevaluation": reevaluation,
++        "planner": {
++            "kind": "qwen_base",
++            "model": str(config["model"]),
++            "max_steps": int(config["max_steps"]),
++            "calls": len(planner.calls),
++            "actions": planner.calls,
++            "prior_calls": prior_planner_calls,
++            "latency_ms": [call.get("latency_ms") for call in planner.calls],
++            "input_tokens": sum(int(call.get("input_tokens") or 0) for call in planner.calls),

++            "Decision Gate remains authoritative; awaiting_clarification/no_verified_candidate_in_scope is not a successful recommendation",
++        ],

+-/** Merges a `tool_status` event into the timeline: a final status closes the latest running row. */
+-function mergeToolStatus(list: LocalToolStatus[], incoming: ToolStatus): LocalToolStatus[] {
+-  if (incoming.status === "running") {
+-    return [...list, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() }];
+-  }
+-  for (let index = list.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+-    if (list[index].tool === incoming.tool && list[index].status === "running") {
+-      const next = [...list];
+-      next[index] = { ...next[index], ...incoming };
+-      return next;
+-    }
+-  }
+-  return [...list, { ...incoming }];
+-}

++/**
++ * Merges one real `tool_status` event into the timeline.
++ *
++ * `running` opens a row (a repeated identical notification is not duplicated). A terminal status
++ * closes the newest matching running row; rows are matched on tool *and* label first because a
++ * Qwen turn legitimately repeats the same tool with a different label per planner action
++ * (`planner_action` → "Planner Action: RETRIEVE", then "Planner Action: RERANK", …). If the
++ * backend only ever sent a terminal status, it is appended as its own completed row — never
++ * dropped, never turned into a fake animation.
++ */
++export function mergeToolStatus(list: LocalToolStatus[], incoming: ToolStatus): LocalToolStatus[] {
++  if (incoming.status === "running") {
++    const last = list[list.length - 1];
++    if (last && last.status === "running" && last.tool === incoming.tool && last.label === incoming.label) {
++      // MCP progress notifications may repeat; refresh the open row instead of duplicating it.
++      return replaceRow(list, list.length - 1, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() });
++    }
++    return [...list, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() }];
++  }
++  let fallbackIndex = -1;
++  for (let index = list.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
++    const row = list[index];
++    if (row.tool !== incoming.tool || row.status !== "running") continue;
++    if (row.label === incoming.label) return replaceRow(list, index, incoming);
++    if (fallbackIndex < 0) fallbackIndex = index;
++  }
++  if (fallbackIndex >= 0) return replaceRow(list, fallbackIndex, incoming);
++  return [...list, { ...incoming }];
++}
++
++/**
++ * Closes progress rows whose completion the backend never reported.
++ *
++ * A finished turn (SSE `done`, an aborted stream, or a persisted turn restored from the session)
++ * cannot still have a running step. Such a row is shown as browser-local `unknown` with an
++ * explicit note — never as an endless spinner (fake animation) and never as a fabricated "ok".
++ */
++export function finaliseStaleToolRows(list: LocalToolStatus[]): LocalToolStatus[] {
++  if (!list.some((row) => row.status === "running")) return list;
++  return list.map((row) =>
++    row.status === "running"
++      ? {
++          ...row,
++          status: "unknown",
++          detail: [row.detail, "Completion was not reported by the backend before the turn ended."]
++            .filter(Boolean)
++            .join(" · "),
++        }
++      : row,
++  );
++}

++        unknown = [item.get("attribute") for item in selected_card.get("unknown_fields") or ()]
++        if unknown:
++            lines.append("UNKNOWN fields: " + ", ".join(str(item) for item in unknown) + ".")
++    else:
++        lines.append("No product was finalized by the Decision Gate; nothing was invented.")
++    if catalogue_size:
++        lines.append(f"Catalogue: {int(catalogue_size):,} products; evidence is catalogue-only.")
++    lines.append(
++        "Catalogue evidence only; not a claim about real-world quality, price, stock or ratings."
++    )
++    return "\n".join(lines)
++
++
+ def narrative_clarification(clarification: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
+     lines = [
+         "Recommendation paused: the Decision Gate requires a user clarification before it can select a product.",

+# QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT — real GPU Qwen Base Live acceptance (2026-11-09)
+
+> **Update 2026-11-10:** the Qwen ASK → browser reply → M9 update → Qwen resume branch has now been
+> exercised with the real Base model on an isolated wired/wireless conflict profile. That follow-up
+> acceptance is **LIMITED**, not GO: the prescribed single reply added an AVOID entry but did not
+> retract the old wired preference under current M9 semantics, a second real browser retraction was
+> required, and the final Gate-selected product had a wired catalogue title. See
+> [`QWEN_ASK_RESUME_REPORT.md`](QWEN_ASK_RESUME_REPORT.md). The 2026-11-09 `GO` below remains a
+> historical verdict for the earlier, narrower scope and its ASK caveat is superseded by this
+> update.
+
+> **Overall: `GO`** for the requested Qwen Base Live scope. This is now backed by **real
+> Qwen3.5-9B-Base inference on the user's GPU instance**, the real P17 MCP `run_agent_turn` tool,
+> the real `ElectronicsPlannerExecutor`, the real catalogue/BGE/Gate/P15 path, and two real
+> browser Qwen Live turns. No HTTP double and no Replay trajectory was used for this verdict.
+>
+> Caveat: the real model did **not** choose `ASK` in the prompts tested, so the Qwen ASK →
+> browser-authored reply → M9 update → Qwen resume branch was **not triggered** in this round.
+> The existing CPU clarification/M9 continuation path remained green; no ASK was fabricated.
+>
+> The previous report (2026-11-09 earlier) correctly recorded `BLOCKED` when the instance had no
+> GPU/model service. This file supersedes that status with the evidence below.
+

+| `REAL_QWEN_MCP_EXECUTION` | **PASS** | Real MCP `run_agent_turn` (8 tools with Qwen configured) → real executor → catalogue/BGE/Gate/P15; 3/3 real recommendation queries finalized, plus a real non-FINALIZE Gate rejection. |

+## 10. Limitations / final verdict
+
+- The local demo remains a localhost-only engineering demo: no auth/TLS/rate limiting, no
+  production deployment, no user-satisfaction/A/B/online-quality claim.
+- Qwen ASK and Qwen-mode memory continuation were not triggered by the real model; the CPU memory
+  path passed. This must be stated whenever GO is cited.
+- The model's rationale is not treated as truth; Gate/P15 remain authoritative. The non-FINALIZE
+  query demonstrated a bounded repeated-action tendency that future model-quality work may address.
+- The provided GPU instance and the temporary model server/tunnel were managed only for this
+  validation; the instance lifecycle was not touched. Service and tunnel are stopped now.
+- The worktree also retains an earlier in-progress BGE cold-start single-flight/warmup change from
+  the interrupted performance round. It was not evaluated as part of this Qwen verdict and no
+  commit was made; all real Qwen tests above passed with the current worktree.
+
+**Final verdict: `GO`** for real GPU Qwen Base Live (real inference, real MCP executor, real Gate,
+real browser, saved traces/screenshots, CPU/Replay regression), with the explicit caveat that the
+Qwen ASK/memory-continuation branch was not observed.

++## 6. Qwen Base Live (real GPU validated 2026-11-09)
++
++This section now has **real GPU validation**, not just a contract wiring:
++RTX 4090 24 GB, Qwen3.5-9B Base (`/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3.5-9B-ms`, no SFT adapter), real
++`/plan` calls, real MCP `run_agent_turn`, real executor/Gate/P15, and two real browser Qwen Live
++turns. Full evidence: [`QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md`](QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md) and
++`runs/web_demo/qwen_live/`.
++
++This is an **optional** live mode over the existing P17 MCP / ElectronicsPlannerExecutor stack.
++It is not a new Agent loop: the real Qwen3.5-9B Base returns a PlannerAction (`ASK`, `RETRIEVE`,
++`RERANK`, `FINALIZE`), and the existing executor/search/scorer/ledger/eligibility/Gate/P15/M9
++components execute it. A Rule→Qwen rewrite or Qwen→Rule fallback is not implemented.
++
++### Configuration
++
++Set these in the **same Windows PowerShell session** that launches the demo (the launcher forwards
++them into WSL; the endpoint is never shown in the browser):
++
++```powershell
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED = "1"                       # explicit opt-in
++$env:QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1:18009"  # existing model server, NOT auto-started
++$env:QWEN_MODEL_NAME = "Qwen3.5-9B-Base"
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_TIMEOUT_S = "180"                    # each /plan call
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_S = "60"             # real PlannerAction health probe
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_TURN_TIMEOUT_S = "600"              # bounds the complete multi-step turn
++$env:QWEN_LIVE_MAX_STEPS = "8"
++powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts/run_web_demo.ps1
++```
++
++Availability is deliberately strict: the BFF performs a real `POST /plan` health probe and only
++shows Qwen Live as available after it parses a valid PlannerAction. A configured-but-unreachable
++service leaves **Qwen Live Unavailable**; CPU Live and Qwen Replay continue to work, and a Qwen
++request never silently becomes a Rule request. The BFF does **not** start/stop AutoDL/GPU, create
++SSH tunnels, download or quantize a model. Start the model service yourself, using the repository's
++`training/agent_sft/model_server.py` or your existing endpoint.
++
++The MCP server exposes the original 7 tools plus one conditional 8th tool,
++`run_agent_turn`, only when Qwen Live is configured. `run_agent_turn` returns the model's real
++actions, tool observations, Gate result, P15 evidence, planner/explainer latencies and the
++optional Qwen clarification handle. `/api/chat/stream` accepts
++`{"mode":"qwen_live", ...}` and emits the same SSE event names plus planner metadata on `done`.
++

+     def _load_encoder(self):
+-        if self._model is None:
++        """Load the encoder exactly once; concurrent callers wait for the same completed state.
++
++        A failed initialisation is retried at most ``MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS`` times by later callers;
++        after that a clear error is raised instead of re-using a partially loaded model.  The model
++        object is published only after the tokenizer, model and ``eval()`` all succeed.
++        """
++        if self._encoder_ready.is_set():
++            return self._tokenizer, self._model
++        if self._encoder_error and self._encoder_attempts >= MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS:
++            raise RuntimeError(
++                "BGE encoder initialization failed after "
++                f"{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS} attempts: {self._encoder_error}"
++            )
++        with self._encoder_lock:
++            # Double-checked after acquiring the lock: another caller may have loaded it while we
++            # were waiting.
++            if self._encoder_ready.is_set():
++                return self._tokenizer, self._model
++            if self._encoder_error and self._encoder_attempts >= MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS:
++                raise RuntimeError(
++                    "BGE encoder initialization failed after "
++                    f"{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS} attempts: {self._encoder_error}"
++                )
++            self._encoder_attempts += 1
++            self._encoder_loading = True
++            started = time.perf_counter()
++            try:
++                import torch
++                from transformers import AutoModel, AutoTokenizer
++
++                tokenizer_started = time.perf_counter()
++                tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(self.model_path)
++                self._tokenizer_load_ms = round(
++                    (time.perf_counter() - tokenizer_started) * 1000.0, 2
++                )
++                model_started = time.perf_counter()
++                model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(self.model_path, torch_dtype=torch.float32)
++                model.eval()
++                self._model_load_ms = round(
++                    (time.perf_counter() - model_started) * 1000.0, 2
++                )
++                # Publish only after every piece is fully initialised.
++                self._tokenizer = tokenizer
++                self._model = model
++                self._encoder_error = None
++                self._encoder_ready.set()
++                return tokenizer, model
++            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - explicit error; caller may retry once
++                self._tokenizer = None
++                self._model = None
++                self._encoder_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
++                raise RuntimeError(
++                    "BGE encoder initialization failed "
++                    f"(attempt {self._encoder_attempts}/{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS}): "
++                    f"{self._encoder_error}"
++                ) from exc
++            finally:
++                self._encoder_loading = False
++                self._encoder_load_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2)
++

++class _ProgressBridge:
++    """Small event bridge from the synchronous executor/Qwen calls to MCP progress notifications.
++
++    It does not own any Agent state: the existing ``run_planner`` + ``ElectronicsPlannerExecutor``
++    are still the only loop/executor.  Events carry action/tool names, status, counts and real
++    latency only; prompts and hidden reasoning are never sent.
++    """
++
++    def __init__(self, context: Context, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop) -> None:
++        self.context = context
++        self.loop = loop
++        self.counter = 0
++        self.pending: list[asyncio.Future[Any]] = []
++
++    def emit(self, *, tool: str, label: str, status: str,
++             detail: str | None = None, latency_ms: float | None = None) -> None:
++        self.counter = min(99, self.counter + 1)
++        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
++            "tool": tool, "label": label, "status": status, "progress": self.counter,
++        }
++        if detail is not None:
++            payload["detail"] = detail
++        if latency_ms is not None:
++            payload["latency_ms"] = round(float(latency_ms), 2)
++        try:
++            future = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(self._send(payload), self.loop)
++            self.pending.append(future)
++            future.add_done_callback(lambda done: done.exception())
++        except Exception:  # progress is observability; it must never fail the real execution
++            LOGGER.debug("progress notification could not be scheduled", exc_info=True)
++
++    async def flush(self) -> None:
++        """Wait until every queued progress notification has been handed to the transport."""
++        while self.pending:
++            future = self.pending.pop(0)
++            try:
++                await asyncio.wrap_future(future)
++            except Exception:
++                LOGGER.debug("queued progress notification failed", exc_info=True)
++
++    async def _send(self, payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
++        try:
++            await self.context.report_progress(
++                float(payload.get("progress") or 0), 100.0,
++                json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")),
++            )
++        except Exception:  # no progress token / disconnected client: execution continues
++            LOGGER.debug("progress notification could not be delivered", exc_info=True)
++

++class _RecordingQwenPlanner:
++    """Record real model calls/actions while delegating the decision to HttpPlannerProposer."""
++
++    def __init__(self, inner: HttpPlannerProposer, bridge: _ProgressBridge, model: str) -> None:
++        self.inner = inner
++        self.bridge = bridge
++        self.model = model
++        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
++
++    def propose(self, observation: PlannerObservation):  # noqa: ANN201 - existing protocol
++        step = len(self.calls) + 1
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="running",
++                         detail=f"{self.model}; step {step}")
++        started = time.perf_counter()
++        try:
++            proposal = self.inner.propose(observation)
++        except Exception as exc:
++            self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="error",
++                             detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
++                             latency_ms=(time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0)
++            raise
++        response = dict(getattr(self.inner, "last_response", {}) or {})
++        latency_ms = response.get("latency_ms")
++        if latency_ms is None:
++            latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="ok",
++                         detail=f"{self.model}; step {step}", latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        self.calls.append({
++            "step": step,
++            "action": proposal.action.value,
++            "arguments": dict(proposal.arguments),
++            "rationale": proposal.rationale,
++            "latency_ms": round(float(latency_ms), 2),
++            "input_tokens": response.get("input_tokens"),
++            "output_tokens": response.get("output_tokens"),
++            "parser_errors": list(response.get("errors") or ()),
++            "raw_output": str(response.get("text") or "")[:2000],
++        })
++        args = dict(proposal.arguments)
++        visible = args.get("query") or args.get("question") or ""
++        self.bridge.emit(tool="planner_action",
++                         label=f"Planner Action: {proposal.action.value}", status="ok",
++                         detail=(f"step {step}" + (f"; {visible}" if visible else "")),
++                         latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        return proposal
++
++
++class _ProgressExecutor:
++    """Emit real tool-start/end events around the existing executor's execute()."""
++
++    _LABELS = {
++        "ASK": "ASK", "RETRIEVE": "RETRIEVE", "RERANK": "RERANK", "FINALIZE": "FINALIZE",
++    }
++
++    def __init__(self, inner: ElectronicsPlannerExecutor, bridge: _ProgressBridge) -> None:
++        self.inner = inner
++        self.bridge = bridge
++        self.counter = 0
++
++    def execute(self, proposal, observation):  # noqa: ANN001, ANN201 - existing protocol
++        action = proposal.action.value
++        self.counter += 1
++        tool = f"{action.lower()}_{self.counter}"
++        self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="running",
++                         detail="existing ElectronicsPlannerExecutor")
++        started = time.perf_counter()
++        try:
++            after, tool_observation, terminal = self.inner.execute(proposal, observation)
++        except Exception as exc:
++            self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="error",
++                             detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
++                             latency_ms=(time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0)
++            raise
++        latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
++        obs = dict(tool_observation or {})
++        detail_parts = [str(obs.get("status") or "done")]
++        if "candidate_count" in obs:
++            detail_parts.append(f"{obs['candidate_count']} candidates")
++        self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="ok",
++                         detail=" - ".join(detail_parts), latency_ms=latency_ms)
++        return after, tool_observation, terminal
++

++async def _run_agent_turn(
++    query: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, description="Current natural-language shopping query")],
++    ctx: Context,
++) -> dict[str, Any]:
++    """Run the existing electronics executor with the real Qwen3.5-9B Base planner.
++
++    The model's PlannerAction(s) are parsed by the existing contract and executed by
++    ``ElectronicsPlannerExecutor``.  No Rule fallback exists: a missing/invalid model action is a
++    typed tool error.  M9 writes are not performed here; clarification replies still go through
++    ``submit_clarification_reply``.

++    config = _qwen_config()
++    if not config["configured"]:
++        raise ToolError(
++            "qwen_unavailable: QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED/AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER and a model endpoint "
++            "must both be configured"
++        )

++    history = context.read_history()
++    task = context.current_task(query)
++    proposer = HttpPlannerProposer(
++        str(config["url"]), model=str(config["model"]), timeout=float(config["timeout_s"])
++    )
++    explainer = None
++    if config["explain"]:
++        explainer = EvidenceExplainer(
++            QwenExplanationClient(str(config["url"]), timeout=float(config["timeout_s"]))
++        )

++        catalogue=context.catalogue, current_query=query,
++        preferences=prepare_preferences(history), hard=prepare_hard(query),
++        task_context=task, memory_history=history, explainer=explainer,

++    observation = PlannerObservation(
++        step=0, query=query,

++    bridge = _ProgressBridge(ctx, loop)
++    planner = _RecordingQwenPlanner(proposer, bridge, str(config["model"]))
++    progress_executor = _ProgressExecutor(executor, bridge)
++
++    def _run_sync():  # executed in a worker thread; progress is bridged to the MCP loop
++        return run_planner(
++            planner=planner, executor=progress_executor,
++            initial_observation=observation, max_steps=int(config["max_steps"]),
++        )
++
++    try:
++        run = await asyncio.to_thread(_run_sync)
++    except HttpPlannerError as exc:
++        raise ToolError(f"qwen_planner_error: {exc.code}: {exc.detail}") from exc
++    except PlannerContractError as exc:

++    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - real executor/transport failure, no Rule fallback
++        raise ToolError(f"agent_execution_error: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
++
++    status = executor.terminal_status
++    if status in {"not_started", "in_progress", "need_more_candidates", "need_more_product_evidence"}:
++        status = "budget_exhausted"
++        executor.terminal_status = status
++    selected = executor.selected_candidate_id
++    gate = executor.last_decision
++    if gate is not None:
++        bridge.emit(tool="decision_gate", label="Decision Gate", status="ok",
++                    detail=f"{gate.get('action')} ({gate.get('reason_code')})")
++    bundle = None
++    if selected:
++        bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="running",
++                    detail="P15 evidence bundle")
++        try:
++            bundle = build_bundle(
++                task=task, selected_item_id=selected, catalogue=context.catalogue,
++                history=history, gate_decision=gate or {},

++        except Exception as exc:  # evidence failure must be visible, not replaced
++            bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="error",
++                        detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")

++

+-/** Merges a `tool_status` event into the timeline: a final status closes the latest running row. */
+-function mergeToolStatus(list: LocalToolStatus[], incoming: ToolStatus): LocalToolStatus[] {
+-  if (incoming.status === "running") {
+-    return [...list, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() }];
+-  }
+-  for (let index = list.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+-    if (list[index].tool === incoming.tool && list[index].status === "running") {
+-      const next = [...list];
+-      next[index] = { ...next[index], ...incoming };
+-      return next;
+-    }
+-  }
+-  return [...list, { ...incoming }];
+-}

++function replaceRow(
++  list: LocalToolStatus[],
++  index: number,
++  incoming: Partial<LocalToolStatus>,
++): LocalToolStatus[] {
++  const next = [...list];
++  next[index] = { ...list[index], ...incoming };
++  return next;
++}
++
++/**
++ * Merges one real `tool_status` event into the timeline.
++ *
++ * `running` opens a row (a repeated identical notification is not duplicated). A terminal status
++ * closes the newest matching running row; rows are matched on tool *and* label first because a
++ * Qwen turn legitimately repeats the same tool with a different label per planner action
++ * (`planner_action` → "Planner Action: RETRIEVE", then "Planner Action: RERANK", …). If the
++ * backend only ever sent a terminal status, it is appended as its own completed row — never
++ * dropped, never turned into a fake animation.
++ */
++export function mergeToolStatus(list: LocalToolStatus[], incoming: ToolStatus): LocalToolStatus[] {
++  if (incoming.status === "running") {
++    const last = list[list.length - 1];
++    if (last && last.status === "running" && last.tool === incoming.tool && last.label === incoming.label) {
++      // MCP progress notifications may repeat; refresh the open row instead of duplicating it.
++      return replaceRow(list, list.length - 1, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() });
++    }
++    return [...list, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() }];
++  }
++  let fallbackIndex = -1;
++  for (let index = list.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
++    const row = list[index];
++    if (row.tool !== incoming.tool || row.status !== "running") continue;
++    if (row.label === incoming.label) return replaceRow(list, index, incoming);
++    if (fallbackIndex < 0) fallbackIndex = index;
++  }

++            .join(" · "),

++      : row,
++  );

-## 6. Tests and verification
+## 6. Qwen Base Live (real GPU validated 2026-11-09)
+
+This section now has **real GPU validation**, not just a contract wiring:
+RTX 4090 24 GB, Qwen3.5-9B Base (`/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3.5-9B-ms`, no SFT adapter), real
+`/plan` calls, real MCP `run_agent_turn`, real executor/Gate/P15, and two real browser Qwen Live
+turns. Full evidence: [`QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md`](QWEN_LIVE_WEB_REPORT.md) and
+`runs/web_demo/qwen_live/`.
+
+This is an **optional** live mode over the existing P17 MCP / ElectronicsPlannerExecutor stack.
+It is not a new Agent loop: the real Qwen3.5-9B Base returns a PlannerAction (`ASK`, `RETRIEVE`,
+`RERANK`, `FINALIZE`), and the existing executor/search/scorer/ledger/eligibility/Gate/P15/M9
+components execute it. A Rule→Qwen rewrite or Qwen→Rule fallback is not implemented.
+
+### Configuration
+
+Set these in the **same Windows PowerShell session** that launches the demo (the launcher forwards
+them into WSL; the endpoint is never shown in the browser):
+
+```powershell
+$env:QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED = "1"                       # explicit opt-in
+$env:QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1:18009"  # existing model server, NOT auto-started
+$env:QWEN_MODEL_NAME = "Qwen3.5-9B-Base"
+$env:QWEN_LIVE_TIMEOUT_S = "180"                    # each /plan call
+$env:QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_S = "60"             # real PlannerAction health probe
+$env:QWEN_LIVE_TURN_TIMEOUT_S = "600"              # bounds the complete multi-step turn
+$env:QWEN_LIVE_MAX_STEPS = "8"
+powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts/run_web_demo.ps1
+```
+
+Availability is deliberately strict: the BFF performs a real `POST /plan` health probe and only
+shows Qwen Live as available after it parses a valid PlannerAction. A configured-but-unreachable
+service leaves **Qwen Live Unavailable**; CPU Live and Qwen Replay continue to work, and a Qwen
+request never silently becomes a Rule request. The BFF does **not** start/stop AutoDL/GPU, create
+SSH tunnels, download or quantize a model. Start the model service yourself, using the repository's
+`training/agent_sft/model_server.py` or your existing endpoint.
+
+The MCP server exposes the original 7 tools plus one conditional 8th tool,
+`run_agent_turn`, only when Qwen Live is configured. `run_agent_turn` returns the model's real
+actions, tool observations, Gate result, P15 evidence, planner/explainer latencies and the
+optional Qwen clarification handle. `/api/chat/stream` accepts
+`{"mode":"qwen_live", ...}` and emits the same SSE event names plus planner metadata on `done`.
+
+### Last verified status (2026-11-09, real GPU)
+
+- Instance: `autodl-container-06fa4ab99e-6a73b008`, GPU RTX 4090 24,564 MiB; CUDA true.
+- Existing `training/agent_sft/model_server.py`, base model only (`adapter=None`), port 8009;
+  model load **12.203 s**; GPU peak observed **19,845 MiB**; `/plan` 2.6–3.6 s direct/tunnel
+  (2.6–4.7 s inside the agent loop); token counts 388–874 input / 69–107 output per call.
+- Temporary WSL tunnel `127.0.0.1:18009 → remote 127.0.0.1:8009`; BFF health reported
+  `qwen_live.status=available`, `planner_action=RETRIEVE`, both MCP servers exposed **8 tools**.
+- Real MCP: 3 finalized turns (`B08D32JM5F`, `B07GPH9WF3`, `B00B93N0PO`, all
+  `ALLOW_FINALIZE/evidence_sufficient`, P15 evidence/`rag` present) and one real non-FINALIZE
+  (`under $1`: 7 bounded planner calls, Gate `NEED_MORE_PRODUCT_EVIDENCE`, no finalize).
+- Real browser: full Playwright **9/9 PASS** (7 CPU/Replay + 2 real Qwen) **plus 1/1 separate
+  real ASK→M9→resume browser test**; screenshots `qwen-live-real.png` / `qwen-live-nonfinal.png`
+  and `runs/web_demo/qwen_ask_resume/browser_final/`.

+  fix now treats "Please avoid wired headphones" as a **task-scoped hard exclusion**, overrides the
+  old historical conflict only for this task, preserves the old M9 audit entry, re-evaluates the
+  preserved candidate pool, and prevents an explicit-wired final product without requiring a second

+  reply was fabricated.

+  instance lifecycle was not touched. Start the service and tunnel yourself for a rerun:
+
+```bash
+# remote GPU host: existing model_server.py + Base model (no adapter)
+cd /root/autodl-tmp/agentrecx
+nohup /root/autodl-tmp/agentrecx-sft/bin/python -m training.agent_sft.model_server \
+  --model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3.5-9B-ms --port 8009 --max-new-tokens 256 \
+  > /root/autodl-tmp/qwen_live_model_server.log 2>&1 &
+```
+
+```bash
+# WSL: temporary tunnel (do not create/stop the GPU instance)
+ssh -N -L 18009:127.0.0.1:8009 -p <port> -i <key> root@<host>
+```
+
+Then use the Qwen env block above and start `scripts/run_web_demo.ps1`. Qwen Live is selectable
+only after `/api/health` reports `qwen_live.status=available`.

+# Qwen Base Live — internal integration contract (P20)
+
+Status: implementation contract for the Web Demo BFF, P17 MCP adapter and frontend.
+Audience: backend/frontend owners in this round. This is not a public claim of live success.
+
+## 0. Hard boundaries
+
+- Qwen Base Live must call the **real Qwen3.5-9B Base `/plan` HTTP service** and feed its
+  PlannerAction(s) through the **existing** `run_planner` + `ElectronicsPlannerExecutor`.
+- No Rule→Qwen rewriting, no Qwen→Rule fallback, no fabricated PlannerAction, no bypass of
+  product role / eligibility / CandidateLedger / Decision Gate / M9 / P15 evidence.
+- Keep the existing 7 MCP tools' schemas and default behaviour. Add exactly one new tool:
+  **`run_agent_turn`** (8 tools total), documented and tested as an addition.
+- CPU Rule Live and Qwen Historical Replay remain available and unchanged in meaning.
+- Qwen Live is **Unavailable** unless (a) explicitly enabled, (b) an endpoint is configured, and
+  (c) a real health check returns a valid PlannerAction. No silent fallback.
+- Never expose the model endpoint, credentials, or hidden chain-of-thought to the browser.
+- Live integration may be `BLOCKED` when no service exists; that is recorded, not faked.
+
+## 1. Environment configuration
+
+BFF/runtime (not sent to browser):
+
+| Variable | Meaning | Default |
+|---|---|---|
+| `QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED` | `1/true/yes/on` enables the optional mode | `0` |
+| `QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT` | Base URL of the existing model server (e.g. a local tunnel URL) | empty |
+| `QWEN_MODEL_NAME` | Informational model label only | `Qwen3.5-9B-Base` |
+| `QWEN_LIVE_TIMEOUT_S` | Planner call timeout | `180` |
+| `QWEN_LIVE_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_S` | Health-check timeout | `60` |
+| `QWEN_LIVE_MAX_STEPS` | Bounded agent step cap (server also hard-caps) | `8` |
+| `QWEN_LIVE_REQUIRE_HEALTH` | require valid PlannerAction health before availability | `1` |
+
+Existing `AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER` / `AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER_URL` remain accepted as internal
+aliases by the MCP server; the BFF maps its public config to them for the MCP child process.
+The BFF must not start/stop AutoDL, create tunnels, download models, or touch GPU state.
+
+## 2. Availability / health
+
+`GET /api/health` adds (no endpoint/credential fields):
+
+```json
+"qwen_live": {
+  "enabled": true,
+  "configured": true,
+  "status": "checking|available|unavailable|error|disabled",
+  "available": false,
+  "model": "Qwen3.5-9B-Base",
+  "planner_action": null,
+  "latency_ms": null,
+  "checked_at": null,
+  "detail": "No live Qwen service detected (disabled/unconfigured/unreachable/invalid PlannerAction)"
+}
+```
+
+`GET /api/demo/config` modes:
+
+```json
+"qwen_live": {
+  "available": false,
+  "label": "Qwen Base Live",
+  "detail": "Real Qwen3.5-9B Base PlannerAction → existing electronics executor → Gate/P15",
+  "badge": "Live Qwen3.5-9B Base",
+  "disabled_reason": "No live Qwen service detected …"
+}
+```
+
+Rules:
+- Default `disabled`/`unavailable`; CPU Live remains true; Replay remains true with its exact
+  `Historical Replay — Not a Live Model Call` badge.
+- If enabled+configured, BFF probes the real `/plan` endpoint in the background (valid action
+  required) and may become available. A failed/late probe must not block BFF/MCP startup.
+- On a later planner failure, the BFF marks Qwen unavailable (explicit error); it never silently
+  runs Rule and labels it Qwen.
+- Health calls are recorded with latency/action but are **not** counted as a user Qwen turn.
+
+## 3. Chat API / SSE
+
+`POST /api/chat/stream` request gains optional `"mode": "cpu_live" | "qwen_live"` (default
+`cpu_live`; extra fields remain forbidden). Qwen Live request before availability → HTTP 409
+`qwen_unavailable` (no SSE), with no fallback.
+
+SSE additions (existing event names kept):
+- `tool_status`: real progress from MCP progress notifications while `run_agent_turn` executes:
+  - `qwen_planner` (label `Qwen Planner Request`) running/ok with model label + latency
+  - `planner_action` (label `Planner Action: RETRIEVE|ASK|RERANK|FINALIZE`)
+  - `retrieve`, `rerank`, `decision_gate`, `ask`, `finalize`, `evidence_preparation`
+  Each row has real status/latency/detail from the execution trace. No hidden CoT.
+- `done`: adds optional `"planner": "qwen_base"|"rule"`, `"planner_calls"`, `"planner_latency_ms"`.
+- `products`/turn views: add optional planner metadata; product/evidence shapes otherwise unchanged.
+- `error` codes: `qwen_unavailable`, `qwen_planner_timeout`, `qwen_planner_invalid`,
+  `qwen_planner_error`. No fake FINALIZE.
+
+Qwen clarification:
+- If the real Qwen action is `ASK`, the MCP response carries a server-issued
+  `clarification_id` and the model's question; BFF displays it and stores pending mode=qwen_live.
+- On `POST /api/clarification/reply`, after the existing M9 `submit_clarification_reply` and
+  preference refresh, the BFF resumes with `run_agent_turn` (not `recommend_products`) when the
+  pending turn was Qwen. If Qwen ASK again, display honestly; no forced clarification.
+
+## 4. P17 MCP addition: `run_agent_turn`
+
+Input schema: `{"query": string}` (only). The server binds identity/search mode; the caller can
+never pass user_id / memory flags / confirmed_by_user.
+
+Output (structured content):
+
+```json
+{
+  "schema": "agentrecx.p17.run_agent_turn.v1",
+  "status": "finalized|awaiting_clarification|no_verified_candidate_in_scope|budget_exhausted|error",
+  "query": "...",
+  "planner": {
+    "kind": "qwen_base",
+    "model": "Qwen3.5-9B-Base",
+    "calls": 2,
+    "actions": [{"step": 1, "action": "RETRIEVE", "arguments": {...}, "rationale": "..."}],
+    "latency_ms": [..],
+    "input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0,
+    "parser_errors": []
+  },
+  "selected_product_id": "B0...|null",
+  "candidate_ids": ["B0...", "..."],
+  "gate": {...}|null,
+  "evidence_bundle": {...}|null,
+  "planner_steps": {...},
+  "clarification": {"clarification_id": "...", "question": "...", "expires_in_seconds": 1800}|null,
+  "search": {"search_mode": "bge|bm25", "score_kind": "...", "catalogue_size": 76263},
+  "rag": {"final_answer": "...", "evidence_bundle": {...}, "claim_validation": {...}}|null,
+  "error": null,
+  "latency_ms": 1234.5
+}
+```
+
+Failure: raise `ToolError("qwen_unavailable: ...")` / `qwen_planner_timeout` /
+`qwen_planner_invalid` / `qwen_planner_error`; never return Rule output as Qwen.
+Progress notifications use `ctx.report_progress(progress, 100, message_json)` where message is a
+JSON object with `tool`, `label`, `status`, `detail`, `latency_ms` fields.
+
+Planner/executor reuse:
+- `HttpPlannerProposer(AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER_URL)` from `recommendation.demo.planner_http_proposer`
+- `run_planner(planner=..., executor=ElectronicsPlannerExecutor(...), max_steps<=8)`
+- `HistoryStateReader` + `prepare_preferences/prepare_hard`
+- `build_bundle` for P15; optional `EvidenceExplainer` only if `/explain` is configured/healthy.
+- `run_agent_turn` must not write M9; writes remain only via `submit_clarification_reply`.
+
+## 5. Tests and acceptance evidence
+
+- Unit/contract: settings/modes/health; MCP tool schema 8 tools; Qwen unavailable path; fake
+  planner HTTP server (clearly labelled test double, not Qwen) proving PlannerAction parsing,
+  executor reuse, no Rule fallback, progress/trace; BFF SSE Qwen path with fake manager.
+- Regression: existing Web E2E 7/7 unchanged in CPU/Replay modes; P17/P16/P15 targeted tests pass;
+  CPU Rule Live default; Replay badge unchanged.
+- Real live (only if a service is actually reachable): save real request/response action(s),
+  model identity, tool observations, planner/explainer latencies, terminal/Gate, browser E2E
+  screenshots. Otherwise `QWEN_LIVE_E2E = BLOCKED` in the report; no GO claim.
+
+## 6. Report verdicts
+
+`QWEN_MODEL_CONNECTION`, `QWEN_PLANNER_EXECUTION`, `MCP_EXECUTOR_INTEGRATION`,
+`WEB_LIVE_E2E`, `MEMORY_CONTINUATION`, `CPU_RULE_REGRESSION`, `REPLAY_SEPARATION`;
+overall `GO | LIMITED | BLOCKED | NO-GO`. `GO` requires real model inference and real browser
+execution evidence. Code-only wiring cannot be GO.

+/**
+ * REAL Qwen Base Live browser acceptance (GPU service, no doubles, no replay).
+ *
+ * The test is intentionally strict: it requires GET /api/health to report
+ * qwen_live.available=true after a real /plan health probe, then drives the real browser
+ * through the existing BFF → P17 MCP run_agent_turn → real Qwen3.5-9B Base planner →
+ * ElectronicsPlannerExecutor → real catalogue/BGE/Gate/P15 path.
+ */
+import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
+import {
+  ensureArtifactDirs,
+  recordCheck,
+  recordPayload,
+  recordTiming,
+  saveScreenshot,
+} from "../fixtures/evidence";
+import {
+  bffGetJson,
+  lastTurn,
+  openDemo,
+  sendChat,
+  waitForHealthReady,
+} from "../fixtures/demo";
+
+const DESKTOP = { width: 1440, height: 900 };
+const RECOMMEND_QUERY = "I need earbuds I can use while working out.";
+const NONFINAL_QUERY = "I need wireless earbuds under $1.";
+
+async function selectQwenLive(page: import("@playwright/test").Page, testInfo: import("@playwright/test").TestInfo) {
+  await page.setViewportSize(DESKTOP);
+  await openDemo(page);
+  await waitForHealthReady(page, testInfo);
+  const qwen = page.getByTestId("mode-qwen-live");
+  await expect(qwen).toBeEnabled({ timeout: 60_000 });
+  await expect(qwen).toHaveAttribute("data-state", "available");
+  await qwen.click();
+  await expect(qwen).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true", { timeout: 30_000 });
+  await expect(page.getByTestId("qwen-live-badge")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 30_000 });
+}
+
+async function assertRealPlannerTurn(turn: import("@playwright/test").Locator, testInfo: import("@playwright/test").TestInfo) {
+  await expect(turn.getByTestId("turn-planner")).toHaveAttribute("data-planner-kind", "qwen_base", {
+    timeout: 30_000,
+  });
+  await expect(turn.getByTestId("turn-planner")).toContainText("Qwen Base Live");
+  const tools = await turn
+    .locator('[data-testid="tool-row"]')
+    .evaluateAll((rows) => rows.map((row) => row.getAttribute("data-tool") ?? ""));
+  expect(tools).toContain("qwen_planner");
+  expect(tools.some((tool) => tool === "planner_action")).toBeTruthy();
+  expect(tools.some((tool) => tool.startsWith("retrieve_"))).toBeTruthy();
+  recordCheck(
+    testInfo,
+    "qwen.real.planner",
+    `real planner badge data-planner-kind=qwen_base; real tool rows=${JSON.stringify(tools)}`,
+    tools.includes("qwen_planner") && tools.some((tool) => tool.startsWith("finalize_")),
+  );
+}
+
+test.describe("REAL Qwen Base Live (GPU)", () => {
+  test("real Qwen planner multi-step recommendation + products/evidence/Gate + screenshot", async ({
+    page,
+    request,
+  }, testInfo) => {
+    ensureArtifactDirs();
+    const started = Date.now();
+    await selectQwenLive(page, testInfo);
+
+    const health = await bffGetJson(request, "/api/health");
+    expect(health.status).toBe(200);
+    expect(health.body.qwen_live?.available).toBe(true);
+    expect(health.body.qwen_live?.status).toBe("available");
+    const modes = health.body.modes ?? {};
+    expect(modes.qwen_live?.available).toBe(true);
+    expect(modes.qwen_live?.badge).toBe("Live Qwen3.5-9B Base");
+    recordPayload(testInfo, "real-qwen-health", {
+      qwen_live: health.body.qwen_live,
+      qwen_mode: modes.qwen_live,
+      tools: (health.body.servers ?? []).map((server: any) => ({
+        profile_id: server.profile_id,
+        tools: server.tools,
+      })),
+    });
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen.real.health",
+      `GET /api/health qwen_live.status=${health.body.qwen_live?.status} available=${health.body.qwen_live?.available} planner_action=${health.body.qwen_live?.planner_action} latency=${health.body.qwen_live?.latency_ms}ms; tools=${JSON.stringify(
+        (health.body.servers ?? []).map((server: any) => (server.tools ?? []).length),
+      )}`,
+      health.body.qwen_live?.available === true && health.body.qwen_live?.planner_action != null,
+    );
+
+    await sendChat(page, testInfo, RECOMMEND_QUERY);
+    const turn = lastTurn(page);
+    await assertRealPlannerTurn(turn, testInfo);
+
+    const gate = turn.getByTestId("gate-card");
+    await expect(gate).toBeVisible({ timeout: 30_000 });
+    const gateAction = await gate.getAttribute("data-gate-action");
+    expect(gateAction).toBe("ALLOW_FINALIZE");
+    await expect(gate).toContainText("evidence_sufficient");
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen.real.gate",
+      `real Qwen turn reached Gate data-gate-action=${gateAction}; Gate text is real`,
+      gateAction === "ALLOW_FINALIZE",
+    );
+
+    const productCards = turn.getByTestId("product-card");
+    await expect(productCards.first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });
+    const productCount = await productCards.count();
+    expect(productCount).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    const blockText = await turn.getByTestId("products-block").innerText();
+    expect(blockText).toContain("Price unavailable / UNKNOWN");
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen.real.products",
+      `real products rendered: ${productCount} cards; UNKNOWN price block present; Gate selected visible`,
+      productCount > 0 && blockText.includes("Price unavailable / UNKNOWN"),

+/**
+ * Real Qwen ASK → single real browser reply → task-scoped constraint priority → M9 history
+ * retained → Qwen resume → fresh candidate re-evaluation → Gate final consistency.
+ *
+ * The isolated profile_0056_ask has historical wired/wireless conflict.  "Please avoid wired
+ * headphones" is a current-purchase exclusion, not a permanent M9 retraction: the old wired
+ * history must remain active in the audit trail, but the resumed task must exclude a product
+ * with trustworthy wired catalogue evidence.  No second "Forget" message is required.
+ */
+import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
+import {
+  ensureArtifactDirs,
+  recordCheck,
+  recordPayload,
+  recordTiming,
+  saveScreenshot,
+} from "../fixtures/evidence";
+import {
+  bffGetJson,
+  bffPostJson,
+  hasPreference,
+  lastTurn,
+  openDemo,
+  preferenceValues,
+  selectProfile,
+  sendChat,
+  waitForHealthReady,
+  waitForTurnIdle,
+} from "../fixtures/demo";
+
+const DESKTOP = { width: 1440, height: 900 };
+const QUERY = "I need headphones. Can you recommend something suitable for me?";
+const FIRST_REPLY = "For this purchase, I prefer wireless headphones for commuting. Please avoid wired headphones.";
+
+test.describe("Qwen ASK → task constraint → resume (real GPU)", () => {
+  test("one real reply changes current task, preserves history and prevents explicit-wired final", async ({
+    page,
+    request,
+  }, testInfo) => {
+    ensureArtifactDirs();
+    await page.setViewportSize(DESKTOP);
+    await openDemo(page);
+    await waitForHealthReady(page, testInfo);
+
+    const health = await bffGetJson(request, "/api/health");
+    expect(health.body.qwen_live?.available).toBe(true);
+    const config = await bffGetJson(request, "/api/demo/config");
+    const testProfile = (config.body.profiles ?? []).find(
+      (profile: any) => profile.profile_id === "profile_0056_ask",
+    );
+    expect(testProfile, "isolated ASK test profile must be enabled").toBeTruthy();
+    expect(testProfile.memory_writes).toBe(true);
+    const session = await selectProfile(page, testInfo, /ASK Test/);
+    expect(session.profile_id).toBe("profile_0056_ask");
+
+    const qwenLive = page.getByTestId("mode-qwen-live");
+    await expect(qwenLive).toBeEnabled({ timeout: 60_000 });
+    await qwenLive.click();
+    await expect(qwenLive).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
+
+    const scenarioStarted = Date.now();
+    const prefsBefore = await bffGetJson(request, `/api/preferences?session_id=${session.session_id}`);
+    const beforeValues = preferenceValues(prefsBefore.body);
+    expect(hasPreference(beforeValues, /wired/)).toBe(true);
+    expect(hasPreference(beforeValues, /wireless/)).toBe(true);
+
+    // ---- real Qwen plan must produce a real ASK ---------------------------------------
+    await sendChat(page, testInfo, QUERY);
+    await waitForTurnIdle(page);
+    const firstCard = page.getByTestId("clarification-card").last();
+    await expect(firstCard).toBeVisible({ timeout: 180_000 });
+    const firstId = (await firstCard.getAttribute("data-clarification-id")) ?? "";
+    expect(firstId.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    const firstQuestion = await page.getByTestId("clarification-question").last().innerText();
+    expect(firstQuestion).toMatch(/wired/i);
+    expect(firstQuestion).toMatch(/wireless/i);
+    await expect(lastTurn(page).getByTestId("turn-planner")).toHaveAttribute(
+      "data-planner-kind",
+      "qwen_base",
+    );
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen_fix.real_ask",
+      `real Qwen planner produced an ASK; handle=${firstId}; question="${firstQuestion}"`,
+      /wired/i.test(firstQuestion) && /wireless/i.test(firstQuestion),
+    );
+    await saveScreenshot(page, testInfo, "qwen-fix-first-ask.png", DESKTOP);
+
+    // ---- no-reply safety --------------------------------------------------------------
+    const prefsAfterAsk = await bffGetJson(
+      request,
+      `/api/preferences?session_id=${session.session_id}`,
+    );
+    expect(preferenceValues(prefsAfterAsk.body)).toEqual(beforeValues);
+    await expect(page.getByTestId("memory-update")).toHaveCount(0);
+    const askGate = page.getByTestId("gate-card").last();
+    await expect(askGate).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });
+    expect(await askGate.getAttribute("data-gate-action")).toBe("NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION");
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen_fix.no_reply_safety",
+      "after ASK and before any reply: no memory-update block, Gate=NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION, preference values unchanged",
+      true,
+    );
+
+    // ---- one real browser-authored reply ---------------------------------------------
+    const replyResponsePromise = page.waitForResponse(
+      (response) =>
+        response.url().includes("/api/clarification/reply") &&
+        response.request().method() === "POST",
+      { timeout: 240_000 },
+    );
+    await page.getByTestId("clarification-input").last().fill(FIRST_REPLY);
+    await page.getByTestId("clarification-submit").last().click();

+    expect(hardExclusions.some((item: any) => String(item.source_text).toLowerCase().includes("avoid wired"))).toBe(true);
+    const reevaluation = replyBody.candidate_reevaluation ?? {};
+    expect(Number(reevaluation?.task_constraint_status?.violations ?? 0)).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(Array.isArray(reevaluation?.excluded_candidate_ids)).toBe(true);
+    recordPayload(testInfo, "qwen-fix-first-reply", replyBody);
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen_fix.task_constraints",
+      `M9 added=${replyBody.memory_update?.added} removed=${replyBody.memory_update?.removed}; task hard exclusions=${JSON.stringify(hardExclusions.map((i: any) => [i.attribute, i.source_text]))}`,

+    );
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen_fix.reevaluation",
+      `resumed candidate re-evaluation: input=${reevaluation?.input_candidate_count}; eligible=${reevaluation?.eligible_count}; excluded=${JSON.stringify(reevaluation?.excluded_candidate_ids)}; violations=${reevaluation?.task_constraint_status?.violations}`,

+        Number(reevaluation?.task_constraint_status?.violations ?? 0) > 0,
+    );
+    await expect(page.getByTestId("memory-update").last()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });
+    await expect(page.getByTestId("memory-update").last()).toContainText(/Added\s*2/);
+    // The answered clarification card remains as an audit row, but its reply control is gone;
+    // there must be no second Qwen question/handle.
+    await expect(page.getByTestId("clarification-card").last()).toHaveAttribute(
+      "data-clarification-id",
+      firstId,
+    );
+    await expect(page.getByTestId("clarification-input")).toHaveCount(0);

+    // exclusion is not a permanent retraction.

+      request,
+      `/api/preferences?session_id=${session.session_id}`,
+    );

+        String(item.polarity ?? "").toLowerCase() === "prefer",

+    );
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen_fix.history_retained",
+      `M9 active after one reply=${JSON.stringify(active.map((item: any) => [item.value, item.polarity]))}; historical wired was not permanantly deleted`,
+      retainedWired.length === 1,
+    );
+

+    await expect
+      .poll(async () => {
+        const turn = lastTurn(page);
+        if ((await turn.getByTestId("turn-meta").count()) === 0) return "no-meta";
+        if ((await turn.getByTestId("products-block").count()) === 0) return "no-products";
+        return "final";
+      }, { timeout: 240_000, intervals: [300, 500, 1000, 2000] })
+      .toBe("final");
+    await expect(lastTurn(page).getByTestId("turn-planner")).toHaveAttribute(
+      "data-planner-kind",
+      "qwen_base",
+    );

+    await expect(gate).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });
+    const gateAction = await gate.getAttribute("data-gate-action");

+    const productCards = lastTurn(page).getByTestId("product-card");
+    expect(await productCards.count()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    // The final Gate-selected item is rendered in the dedicated banner.  Product-card order
+    // still reflects the earlier catalogue result block, so the banner is the authoritative
+    // final-selection UI signal.

+      testInfo,
+      "qwen_fix.reevaluation_pool",

+      Number(reevaluation?.eligible_count ?? 0) > 0,
+    );
+
+    const details = await bffPostJson(request, "/api/products/details", {
+      session_id: session.session_id,
+      product_ref: selectedProductId,
+    });
+    expect(details.status).toBe(200);
+    const evidenceRows = Array.isArray(details.body.evidence) ? details.body.evidence : [];
+    const wiredEvidence = evidenceRows.filter(
+      (row: any) => String(row.attribute ?? "").toLowerCase() === "wired",
+    );
+    expect(wiredEvidence, "final product must not have trustworthy wired evidence").toHaveLength(0);
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen_fix.final_product_not_wired",
+      `final selected=${selectedProductId}; title=${details.body.title}; wired evidence rows=${wiredEvidence.length}; gate=${gateAction}`,
+      wiredEvidence.length === 0,
+    );
+    recordCheck(
+      testInfo,
+      "qwen_fix.gate_final",
+      `real resumed Qwen turn reached Gate action=${gateAction}; product=${selectedProductId}; products=${await productCards.count()}`,
+      gateAction === "ALLOW_FINALIZE",
+    );
+    recordTiming(testInfo, "qwen_fix.total", Date.now() - scenarioStarted,
+      "Qwen ASK → one reply → task constraints → re-evaluation → Gate");
+    await saveScreenshot(page, testInfo, "qwen-fix-resume.png", DESKTOP);
+  });

     def _load_encoder(self):
-        if self._model is None:
+        """Load the encoder exactly once; concurrent callers wait for the same completed state.
+
+        A failed initialisation is retried at most ``MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS`` times by later callers;
+        after that a clear error is raised instead of re-using a partially loaded model.  The model
+        object is published only after the tokenizer, model and ``eval()`` all succeed.
+        """
+        if self._encoder_ready.is_set():
+            return self._tokenizer, self._model
+        if self._encoder_error and self._encoder_attempts >= MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS:
+            raise RuntimeError(
+                "BGE encoder initialization failed after "
+                f"{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS} attempts: {self._encoder_error}"
+            )
+        with self._encoder_lock:
+            # Double-checked after acquiring the lock: another caller may have loaded it while we
+            # were waiting.
+            if self._encoder_ready.is_set():
+                return self._tokenizer, self._model
+            if self._encoder_error and self._encoder_attempts >= MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS:
+                raise RuntimeError(
+                    "BGE encoder initialization failed after "
+                    f"{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS} attempts: {self._encoder_error}"
+                )
+            self._encoder_attempts += 1
+            self._encoder_loading = True
+            started = time.perf_counter()
+            try:
+                if self._encoder_loader is not None:
+                    tokenizer, model = self._encoder_loader(self.model_path)
+                    if hasattr(model, "eval"):
+                        model.eval()
+                else:
+                    import torch
+                    from transformers import AutoModel, AutoTokenizer
+
+                    tokenizer_started = time.perf_counter()
+                    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(self.model_path)
+                    self._tokenizer_load_ms = round(
+                        (time.perf_counter() - tokenizer_started) * 1000.0, 2
+                    )
+                    model_started = time.perf_counter()
+                    model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(self.model_path, torch_dtype=torch.float32)
+                    model.eval()
+                    self._model_load_ms = round(
+                        (time.perf_counter() - model_started) * 1000.0, 2
+                    )
+                # Publish only after every piece is fully initialised.
+                self._tokenizer = tokenizer
+                self._model = model
+                self._encoder_error = None
+                self._encoder_ready.set()
+                return tokenizer, model
+            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - explicit error; caller may retry once
+                self._tokenizer = None
+                self._model = None
+                self._encoder_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
+                raise RuntimeError(
+                    "BGE encoder initialization failed "
+                    f"(attempt {self._encoder_attempts}/{MAX_ENCODER_ATTEMPTS}): "
+                    f"{self._encoder_error}"
+                ) from exc
+            finally:
+                self._encoder_loading = False
+                self._encoder_load_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2)
+

+        """

+                # controlled warm-up.  If the retry also fails, the explicit error below
+                # stops the startup; no half-initialised encoder is published.
+                tokenizer, model = self._load_encoder()

+            text = QUERY_INSTRUCTION + str(query or WARMUP_QUERY)
+            tokenize_started = time.perf_counter()
+            inputs = tokenizer([text], truncation=True, max_length=MAX_LENGTH, return_tensors="pt")
+            tokenize_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - tokenize_started) * 1000.0, 2)
+            forward_started = time.perf_counter()
+            with torch.inference_mode():
+                hidden = model(**inputs).last_hidden_state[:, 0]
+                _ = torch.nn.functional.normalize(hidden.float(), p=2, dim=1)[0].numpy()
+            forward_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - forward_started) * 1000.0, 2)
+            result = {
+                "status": "ok",
+                "query": query,
+                "encoder_load_ms": self._encoder_load_ms,
+                "tokenizer_load_ms": self._tokenizer_load_ms,
+                "model_load_ms": self._model_load_ms,
+                "tokenization_ms": tokenize_ms,
+                "forward_ms": forward_ms,
+                "total_ms": round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2),
+            }
+            self._last_warmup = result
+            return result
+        except Exception as exc:
+            result = {
+                "status": "error",
+                "query": query,
+                "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
+                "total_ms": round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2),
+            }
+            self._last_warmup = result
+            raise
+
+    def encoder_status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        return {
+            "ready": bool(self._encoder_ready.is_set()),
+            "loading": bool(self._encoder_loading),
+            "attempts": int(self._encoder_attempts),
+            "error": self._encoder_error,
+            "encoder_load_ms": self._encoder_load_ms,
+            "tokenizer_load_ms": self._tokenizer_load_ms,
+            "model_load_ms": self._model_load_ms,
+            "last_warmup": dict(self._last_warmup) if self._last_warmup else None,
+        }
 
     def search(self, query: str, *, limit: int = 10) -> list[DenseHit]:
         tokenizer, model = self._load_encoder()

+    def warmup(self, query: str = "wireless headphones") -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Load and exercise the dense encoder/index without changing search state.
+
+        Only the BGE mode has lazy encoder state.  A BM25-only source is already ready and
+        returns an explicit no-op status rather than pretending an encoder exists.
+        """
+        if self._dense is None:
+            return {"status": "not_required", "mode": self._mode}
+        warmup = getattr(self._dense, "warmup", None)
+        if warmup is None:
+            raise RuntimeError("dense index does not expose a warmup method")
+        result = warmup(query)
+        # Exercise the actual dense retrieval path as part of Search Ready.  This is a
+        # real index read; it does not mutate the catalogue or return a fake result.
+        result["retrieval_check"] = self.search(query, limit=1).as_dict()
+        return result
+

+        if self._dense is None:
+            return {"ready": True, "mode": self._mode, "required": False}
+        status = getattr(self._dense, "encoder_status", None)
+        return status() if status is not None else {"ready": True, "mode": self._mode}
+
+    @property
+    def search_ready(self) -> bool:
+        if self._mode not in {"bge", "hybrid"}:
+            return True
+        status = self.encoder_status()
+        return bool(status.get("ready"))

+def _qwen_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """Read the optional Qwen Base service configuration; never log the endpoint."""
+    url = (os.environ.get(ENV_URL) or "").strip()
+    enabled = planner_enabled()
+    try:
+        timeout_s = float(os.environ.get("AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER_TIMEOUT_S") or 120.0)
+    except ValueError as exc:
+        raise ToolError("qwen_misconfigured: planner timeout must be numeric") from exc
+    try:
+        max_steps = int(os.environ.get("AGENTRECX_QWEN_MAX_STEPS") or MAX_STEPS)
+    except ValueError as exc:
+        raise ToolError("qwen_misconfigured: max steps must be an integer") from exc
+    return {
+        "enabled": enabled,
+        "configured": bool(enabled and url),
+        "url": url,
+        "model": (os.environ.get(ENV_MODEL) or "").strip() or "Qwen3.5-9B-Base",
+        "timeout_s": max(1.0, timeout_s),
+        "max_steps": max(1, min(MAX_STEPS, max_steps)),
+        "explain": (os.environ.get("AGENTRECX_QWEN_EXPLAIN", "1").strip().casefold()
+                    not in {"0", "false", "no", "off"}),
+    }
