+class _ProgressBridge:
+    """Small event bridge from the synchronous executor/Qwen calls to MCP progress notifications.
+
+    It does not own any Agent state: the existing ``run_planner`` + ``ElectronicsPlannerExecutor``
+    are still the only loop/executor.  Events carry action/tool names, status, counts and real
+    latency only; prompts and hidden reasoning are never sent.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, context: Context, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop) -> None:
+        self.context = context
+        self.loop = loop
+        self.counter = 0
+        self.pending: list[asyncio.Future[Any]] = []
+
+    def emit(self, *, tool: str, label: str, status: str,
+             detail: str | None = None, latency_ms: float | None = None) -> None:
+        self.counter = min(99, self.counter + 1)
+        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
+            "tool": tool, "label": label, "status": status, "progress": self.counter,
+        }
+        if detail is not None:
+            payload["detail"] = detail
+        if latency_ms is not None:
+            payload["latency_ms"] = round(float(latency_ms), 2)
+        try:
+            future = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(self._send(payload), self.loop)
+            self.pending.append(future)
+            future.add_done_callback(lambda done: done.exception())
+        except Exception:  # progress is observability; it must never fail the real execution
+            LOGGER.debug("progress notification could not be scheduled", exc_info=True)
+
+    async def flush(self) -> None:
+        """Wait until every queued progress notification has been handed to the transport."""
+        while self.pending:
+            future = self.pending.pop(0)
+            try:
+                await asyncio.wrap_future(future)
+            except Exception:
+                LOGGER.debug("queued progress notification failed", exc_info=True)
+
+    async def _send(self, payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
+        try:
+            await self.context.report_progress(
+                float(payload.get("progress") or 0), 100.0,
+                json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")),
+            )
+        except Exception:  # no progress token / disconnected client: execution continues
+            LOGGER.debug("progress notification could not be delivered", exc_info=True)
+
+
+class _RecordingQwenPlanner:
+    """Record real model calls/actions while delegating the decision to HttpPlannerProposer."""
+
+    def __init__(self, inner: HttpPlannerProposer, bridge: _ProgressBridge, model: str) -> None:
+        self.inner = inner
+        self.bridge = bridge
+        self.model = model
+        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+
+    def propose(self, observation: PlannerObservation):  # noqa: ANN201 - existing protocol
+        step = len(self.calls) + 1
+        self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="running",
+                         detail=f"{self.model}; step {step}")
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        try:
+            proposal = self.inner.propose(observation)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="error",
+                             detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
+                             latency_ms=(time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0)
+            raise
+        response = dict(getattr(self.inner, "last_response", {}) or {})
+        latency_ms = response.get("latency_ms")
+        if latency_ms is None:
+            latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
+        self.bridge.emit(tool="qwen_planner", label="Qwen Planner Request", status="ok",
+                         detail=f"{self.model}; step {step}", latency_ms=latency_ms)
+        self.calls.append({
+            "step": step,
+            "action": proposal.action.value,
+            "arguments": dict(proposal.arguments),
+            "rationale": proposal.rationale,
+            "latency_ms": round(float(latency_ms), 2),
+            "input_tokens": response.get("input_tokens"),
+            "output_tokens": response.get("output_tokens"),
+            "parser_errors": list(response.get("errors") or ()),
+            "raw_output": str(response.get("text") or "")[:2000],
+        })
+        args = dict(proposal.arguments)
+        visible = args.get("query") or args.get("question") or ""
+        self.bridge.emit(tool="planner_action",
+                         label=f"Planner Action: {proposal.action.value}", status="ok",
+                         detail=(f"step {step}" + (f"; {visible}" if visible else "")),
+                         latency_ms=latency_ms)
+        return proposal
+
+
+class _ProgressExecutor:
+    """Emit real tool-start/end events around the existing executor's execute()."""
+
+    _LABELS = {
+        "ASK": "ASK", "RETRIEVE": "RETRIEVE", "RERANK": "RERANK", "FINALIZE": "FINALIZE",
+    }
+
+    def __init__(self, inner: ElectronicsPlannerExecutor, bridge: _ProgressBridge,
+                 holder: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
+        self.inner = inner
+        self.bridge = bridge
+        self.holder = holder
+        self.counter = 0
+
+    def execute(self, proposal, observation):  # noqa: ANN001, ANN201 - existing protocol
+        action = proposal.action.value
+        self.counter += 1
+        tool = f"{action.lower()}_{self.counter}"
+        self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="running",
+                         detail="existing ElectronicsPlannerExecutor")
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        try:
+            after, tool_observation, terminal = self.inner.execute(proposal, observation)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="error",
+                             detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
+                             latency_ms=(time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0)
+            raise
+        if self.holder is not None:
+            self.holder["observation"] = after
+            self.holder["terminal"] = terminal
+        latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
+        obs = dict(tool_observation or {})
+        detail_parts = [str(obs.get("status") or "done")]
+        if "candidate_count" in obs:
+            detail_parts.append(f"{obs['candidate_count']} candidates")
+        self.bridge.emit(tool=tool, label=self._LABELS.get(action, action), status="ok",
+                         detail=" - ".join(detail_parts), latency_ms=latency_ms)
+        return after, tool_observation, terminal
+
+
+async def _run_agent_turn(
+    query: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, description="Current natural-language shopping query")],
+    ctx: Context,
+    clarification_id: Annotated[
+        str | None,
+        Field(description="Set only when resuming the same Qwen task after a real user clarification reply"),
+    ] = None,
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """Run the existing electronics executor with the real Qwen3.5-9B Base planner.
+
+    The model's PlannerAction(s) are parsed by the existing contract and executed by
+    ``ElectronicsPlannerExecutor``.  No Rule fallback exists: a missing/invalid model action is a
+    typed tool error.  M9 writes are not performed here; clarification replies still go through
+    ``submit_clarification_reply``.
+
+    When ``clarification_id`` is supplied, the exact executor/observation (CandidateLedger, tool
+    observations, budgets) created before the real Qwen ASK is reused; only M9-derived preferences
+    are refreshed, then Qwen is called again for a new PlannerAction.
+    """
+    started = time.perf_counter()
+    config = _qwen_config()
+    if not config["configured"]:
+        raise ToolError(
+            "qwen_unavailable: QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED/AGENTRECX_QWEN_PLANNER and a model endpoint "
+            "must both be configured"
+        )
+    context = _context()
+    history = context.read_history()
+    task = context.current_task(query)
+    continuation: dict[str, Any] | None = None
+    resume_task_constraints = CurrentTaskConstraints()
+    if clarification_id is not None:
+        with context.lock:
+            state = context.handles.get(str(clarification_id))
+            if state is None:
+                raise ToolError("invalid_or_expired_clarification_id")
+            if state.user_key != context.user_key:
+                raise ToolError("clarification_id belongs to another authorized user")
+            if time.time() > state.expires_at:
+                state.status = "expired"
+                raise ToolError("clarification_id_expired")
+            if state.status != "answered":
+                raise ToolError("clarification_reply_required: no real user reply was persisted yet")
+            continuation = state.continuation
+            if continuation is None:
+                raise ToolError("clarification_already_resumed: this handle has no pending agent continuation")
+            if str(continuation.get("query") or "") != str(query):
+                raise ToolError("clarification_query_mismatch: resume must keep the original query")
+            resume_task_constraints = state.task_constraints
+            state.continuation = None
+            state.status = "resumed"
+        task = dict(continuation["task"])
+        history = context.read_history()
+    proposer = HttpPlannerProposer(
+        str(config["url"]), model=str(config["model"]), timeout=float(config["timeout_s"])
+    )
+    explainer = None
+    if config["explain"]:
+        explainer = EvidenceExplainer(
+            QwenExplanationClient(str(config["url"]), timeout=float(config["timeout_s"]))
+        )
+    prior_planner_calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+    reevaluation: dict[str, Any] | None = None
+    if continuation is not None:
+        executor = continuation["executor"]
+        executor.current_query = query
+        executor.ask_question = None
+        executor.terminal_status = "in_progress"
+        prior_observation = continuation["observation"]
+        # Re-evaluate the preserved candidate pool against the latest M9 state and the
+        # current task's reply constraints.  CandidateLedger / retrieved IDs / budgets are
+        # untouched; only stale scorer rows, eligibility and Gate-derived state are rebuilt.
+        reevaluation = executor.refresh_for_task(
+            history=history,
+            task_constraints=resume_task_constraints,
+            candidate_ids=list(prior_observation.candidate_ids or ()),
+        )
+        valid_candidate_ids = list(reevaluation.get("candidate_ids") or ())
+        unresolved_conflicts = conflicts(executor.preferences)
+        LOGGER.info(
+            "Qwen resume clarification=%s preferences=%s task_constraints=%s "
+            "reevaluation=%s unresolved_conflicts=%s",
+            clarification_id, executor.preferences,
+            resume_task_constraints.as_dict(), reevaluation, unresolved_conflicts,
+        )
+        observation = replace(
+            prior_observation,
+            terminal=False,
+            needs_user_input=bool(unresolved_conflicts),
+            candidate_ids=tuple(valid_candidate_ids),
+            reranked=bool(valid_candidate_ids),
+            allowed_actions=(),
+            m5_state={
+                **prior_observation.m5_state,
+                "phase": PHASE_FINALIZE if valid_candidate_ids else PHASE_PRODUCT_RETRIEVE,
+                "preferences": executor.preferences,
+                "task_constraints": resume_task_constraints.as_dict(),
+                "gate_action": "NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION" if unresolved_conflicts else "USER_CLARIFICATION_REPLIED",
+                "ask_question": None,
+                "clarification_id": clarification_id,
+                "preference_conflicts": unresolved_conflicts,
+                "candidate_reevaluation": reevaluation,
+            },
+        )
+        prior_planner_calls = list(continuation.get("planner_calls") or [])
+    else:
+        executor = ElectronicsPlannerExecutor(
+            catalogue=context.catalogue, current_query=query,
+            preferences=prepare_preferences(history), hard=prepare_hard(query),
+            task_context=task, memory_history=history, explainer=explainer,
+        )
+        observation = PlannerObservation(
+            step=0, query=query,
+            m5_state={"phase": PHASE_PRODUCT_RETRIEVE, "next_query": query,
+                      "preferences": executor.preferences},
+        )
+    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
+    bridge = _ProgressBridge(ctx, loop)
+    planner = _RecordingQwenPlanner(proposer, bridge, str(config["model"]))
+    state_holder: dict[str, Any] = {}
+    progress_executor = _ProgressExecutor(executor, bridge, holder=state_holder)
+
+    def _run_sync():  # executed in a worker thread; progress is bridged to the MCP loop
+        run = run_planner(
+            planner=planner, executor=progress_executor,
+            initial_observation=observation, max_steps=int(config["max_steps"]),
+        )
+        state_holder["run"] = run
+        return run
+
+    try:
+        run = await asyncio.to_thread(_run_sync)
+    except HttpPlannerError as exc:
+        raise ToolError(f"qwen_planner_error: {exc.code}: {exc.detail}") from exc
+    except PlannerContractError as exc:
+        raise ToolError(f"qwen_planner_contract_error: {exc.code}: {exc.detail}") from exc
+    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - real executor/transport failure, no Rule fallback
+        raise ToolError(f"agent_execution_error: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
+
+    status = executor.terminal_status
+    if status in {"not_started", "in_progress", "need_more_candidates", "need_more_product_evidence"}:
+        status = "budget_exhausted"
+        executor.terminal_status = status
+    selected = executor.selected_candidate_id
+    gate = executor.last_decision
+    if gate is not None:
+        bridge.emit(tool="decision_gate", label="Decision Gate", status="ok",
+                    detail=f"{gate.get('action')} ({gate.get('reason_code')})")
+    bundle = None
+    if selected:
+        bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="running",
+                    detail="P15 evidence bundle")
+        try:
+            bundle = build_bundle(
+                task=task, selected_item_id=selected, catalogue=context.catalogue,
+                history=history, gate_decision=gate or {},
+                search_mode=context.search_mode,
+                ledger_source=f"CatalogSearchSource(mode={context.search_mode})",
+            ).as_dict()
+            bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="ok",
+                        detail=f"{len(bundle.get('evidence') or [])} evidence items")
+        except Exception as exc:  # evidence failure must be visible, not replaced
+            bridge.emit(tool="evidence_preparation", label="Evidence Preparation", status="error",
+                        detail=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
+            bundle = None
+
+    clarification = None
+    if status == "awaiting_clarification" and executor.ask_question:
+        clarification_id = "qclar_" + uuid.uuid4().hex
+        now = time.time()
+        final_observation = state_holder.get("observation") or observation
+        state = ClarificationState(

+            task_constraints=executor.task_constraints,
+            continuation={

+                "observation": final_observation,

+                "planner_calls": prior_planner_calls + list(planner.calls),
+            },
+        )
+        with context.lock:

+        clarification = {
+            "clarification_id": clarification_id,
+            "question": str(executor.ask_question),
+            "reason": "qwen_planner_ask",
+            "expires_in_seconds": context.clarification_ttl_seconds,
+            "memory_write_enabled": context.writes_enabled,
+        }
+

+    for item in (observation.candidate_ids or ()):

+        }
+    elif executor.explanation_error:
+        rag = {"status": "error", "final_answer": None, "error": executor.explanation_error}
+
+    latency_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2)
+    await bridge.flush()
+    return {
+        "schema": "agentrecx.p17.run_agent_turn.v1",
+        "status": status,
+        "query": query,
+        "resumed": bool(continuation is not None),
+        "resumed_from_clarification_id": clarification_id if continuation is not None else None,
+        "task_constraints": executor.task_constraints.as_dict(),
+        "candidate_reevaluation": reevaluation,
+        "planner": {
+            "kind": "qwen_base",
+            "model": str(config["model"]),
+            "max_steps": int(config["max_steps"]),
+            "calls": len(planner.calls),

+            "input_tokens": sum(int(call.get("input_tokens") or 0) for call in planner.calls),
+            "output_tokens": sum(int(call.get("output_tokens") or 0) for call in planner.calls),

+                              if call.get("parser_errors")],
+        },

index 0000000..8325f09
--- /dev/null
+++ b/recommendation/preference/current_task_constraints.py

+"""Current-purchase constraints recovered from an explicit clarification reply.
+
+These constraints are **task-scoped**.  They are not written to M9 and never delete or
+rewrite a historical preference.  A current avoidance ("for this purchase, avoid wired")
+overrides a historical positive preference only for the current task's effective
+preference list; the M9 audit trail remains intact.  An explicit retraction ("forget my
+wired preference") is handled by M9's existing writer and is not turned into a task
+constraint here.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
+
+from experiments.p14b1.diagnose import canonical
+from recommendation.memory.schemas import PreferenceKind
+
+CURRENT_TASK_CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA = "agentrecx.current_task_constraints.v1"
+FORM_ATTRS = ("over_ear", "on_ear", "in_ear")
+
+
+def _norm(value: Any) -> str:
+    if hasattr(value, "value"):
+        value = value.value
+    return " ".join(str(value or "").lower().split())
+
+
+def _kind_value(kind: Any) -> str:
+    return str(getattr(kind, "value", kind) or "").lower()
+
+
+def constraint_attribute(kind: Any, value: Any) -> str:
+    """Map a stated preference to the catalogue attribute it constrains.
+
+    Known connection/form/preference vocabulary is canonicalised (``wired``,
+    ``wireless``, ``over_ear``...).  Brand is a named slot, not a substring match.
+    Unknown values stay as explicit context (``constraint:<value>``) rather than being
+    guessed into an unrelated catalogue attribute.
+    """
+    known = canonical(value)
+    if known:
+        return known
+    kind_value = _kind_value(kind)
+    if kind_value == PreferenceKind.BRAND.value:
+        return "brand"
+    if kind_value == PreferenceKind.CATEGORY.value:
+        return "product_type"
+    if kind_value in {PreferenceKind.COLOR.value, PreferenceKind.MATERIAL.value}:
+        return kind_value
+    return "constraint:" + _norm(value)
+
+
+def constraint_value(kind: Any, value: Any) -> str:
+    known = canonical(value)
+    return known or _norm(value)
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CurrentTaskConstraint:
+    """One explicit constraint from the current clarification reply."""
+
+    attribute: str
+    value: str
+    kind: str
+    polarity: str  # "exclude" | "prefer"
+    source_text: str
+    source_turn_id: str
+
+    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        return {
+            "attribute": self.attribute,
+            "value": self.value,
+            "kind": self.kind,
+            "polarity": self.polarity,
+            "source_text": self.source_text,
+            "source_turn_id": self.source_turn_id,
+        }
+
+
+@dataclass
+class CurrentTaskConstraints:
+    """Task-scoped hard exclusions and soft preferences.
+
+    ``hard_exclusions`` are explicit current requirements (AVOID).  They override
+    historical positive preferences for the same attribute **in this task only**.
+    ``soft_preferences`` are current positive preferences; they add ranking signal but
+    do not prove an unknown catalogue fact.
+    """
+
+    hard_exclusions: list[CurrentTaskConstraint] = field(default_factory=list)
+    soft_preferences: list[CurrentTaskConstraint] = field(default_factory=list)
+    source_turns: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
+
+    @property
+    def is_empty(self) -> bool:
+        return not self.hard_exclusions and not self.soft_preferences
+
+    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        return {
+            "schema": CURRENT_TASK_CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA,
+            "hard_exclusions": [item.as_dict() for item in self.hard_exclusions],
+            "soft_preferences": [item.as_dict() for item in self.soft_preferences],
+            "source_turns": list(self.source_turns),
+        }
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_payload(
+        cls,
+        payload: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
+        *, source_turn_id: str = "",
+    ) -> "CurrentTaskConstraints":
+        """Build from the M9 extraction payload (``preferences`` only).
+
+        ``removals`` are deliberately ignored: they belong to M9's permanent retraction
+        semantics, not to the current task constraint layer.
+        """
+        state = cls()
+        if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
+            return state
+        preferences = payload.get("preferences") or ()
+        for item in preferences:
+            if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
+                continue
+            mode = _norm(item.get("mode") or "add")
+            if mode == "remove":
+                continue
+            raw_value = str(item.get("value") or "").strip()
+            if not raw_value:
+                continue
+            kind = _kind_value(item.get("kind"))
+            attribute = constraint_attribute(kind, raw_value)
+            value = constraint_value(kind, raw_value)
+            polarity = _norm(item.get("polarity") or "prefer")
+            source_text = str(item.get("source_text") or raw_value)
+            constraint = CurrentTaskConstraint(
+                attribute=attribute,
+                value=value,
+                kind=kind or "unknown",
+                polarity="exclude" if polarity in {"avoid", "negative", "dislike"} else "prefer",
+                source_text=source_text,
+                source_turn_id=str(source_turn_id or ""),
+            )
+            state = state.merged(cls(
+                hard_exclusions=[constraint] if constraint.polarity == "exclude" else [],
+                soft_preferences=[constraint] if constraint.polarity == "prefer" else [],
+                source_turns=[str(source_turn_id)] if source_turn_id else [],
+            ))
+        return state
+
+    def merged(self, other: "CurrentTaskConstraints") -> "CurrentTaskConstraints":
+        """Merge a later current-turn statement over this task state.
+
+        A current explicit preference for an attribute wins over an earlier current
+        exclusion of that same attribute.  A later avoidance wins over a current soft
+        preference.  Explicit M9 retractions do not clear current task constraints.
+        """
+        hard = [dict(item.as_dict()) for item in self.hard_exclusions]
+        soft = [dict(item.as_dict()) for item in self.soft_preferences]
+        for item in other.hard_exclusions:
+            item_dict = item.as_dict()
+            soft = [row for row in soft if row["attribute"] != item.attribute]
+            hard = [row for row in hard if row["attribute"] != item.attribute]
+            hard.append(item_dict)
+        for item in other.soft_preferences:
+            item_dict = item.as_dict()
+            hard = [row for row in hard if row["attribute"] != item.attribute]
+            if not any(row["attribute"] == item.attribute for row in soft):
+                soft.append(item_dict)
+        turns: list[str] = []
+        for turn in list(self.source_turns) + list(other.source_turns):
+            if turn and turn not in turns:
+                turns.append(turn)
+        return CurrentTaskConstraints(
+            hard_exclusions=[CurrentTaskConstraint(**row) for row in hard],
+            soft_preferences=[CurrentTaskConstraint(**row) for row in soft],
+            source_turns=turns,
+        )
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        return self.as_dict()
+
+
+def effective_preferences(
+    history_preferences: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
+    constraints: CurrentTaskConstraints | None,
+) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
+    """Return the preference list used for the **current task**.
+
+    Historical entries are copied, never mutated.  A current hard exclusion removes a
+    historical positive entry with the same canonical attribute from this task view and
+    adds an explicit negative preference; the original M9 entry remains active and
+    auditable.
+    """
+    out = [dict(item) for item in history_preferences]
+    if constraints is None or constraints.is_empty:
+        return out
+    for exclusion in constraints.hard_exclusions:
+        attribute = exclusion.attribute
+        out = [
+            item for item in out
+            if not (
+                str(item.get("polarity") or "positive") == "positive"
+                and _norm(item.get("value")) == attribute
+            )
+        ]
+        # Keep exactly one current negative entry for this attribute.
+        out = [
+            item for item in out
+            if not (
+                str(item.get("polarity") or "positive") == "negative"
+                and _norm(item.get("value")) == attribute
+            )
+        ]
+        out.append({
+            "value": attribute if not attribute.startswith("constraint:") else exclusion.value,
+            "polarity": "negative",
+            "kind": "attribute",
+            "source_turn_id": exclusion.source_turn_id,
+            "source_text": exclusion.source_text,
+        })
+    for soft in constraints.soft_preferences:
+        attribute = soft.attribute
+        if any(
+            str(item.get("polarity") or "positive") == "positive"
+            and _norm(item.get("value")) == attribute
+            for item in out
+        ):
+            continue
+        # Do not turn a canonical exclusion into a positive at the same time.
+        out = [
+            item for item in out
+            if not (
+                str(item.get("polarity") or "positive") == "negative"
+                and _norm(item.get("value")) == attribute
+            )
+        ]
+        out.append({
+            "value": attribute if not attribute.startswith("constraint:") else soft.value,
+            "polarity": "positive",
+            "kind": "attribute",
+            "source_turn_id": soft.source_turn_id,
+            "source_text": soft.source_text,
+        })
+    return out
+
+
+def _attribute_present(
+    attributes: Mapping[str, Any],
+    form_factor: str | None,
+    constraint: CurrentTaskConstraint,
+    product_type: str | None = None,
+) -> bool:
+    attribute = constraint.attribute
+    if attribute in FORM_ATTRS:
+        return _norm(form_factor) == attribute
+    if attribute == "product_type":
+        return _norm(product_type) == constraint.value
+    if attribute == "brand":
+        if "brand" not in {_norm(name) for name in attributes}:
+            return False
+        fact = attributes.get("brand")
+        evidence = _norm(fact.get("evidence") if isinstance(fact, Mapping) else fact)
+        wanted = _norm(constraint.value)
+        return bool(wanted and wanted in evidence.split())
+    if attribute in {_norm(name) for name in attributes}:
+        # A catalogue fact can be present lexically under negation ("not wired",
+        # "no wireless").  Do not turn that into a positive constraint match.
+        fact = next(
+            (value for name, value in attributes.items() if _norm(name) == attribute), None
+        )
+        evidence = _norm(fact.get("evidence") if isinstance(fact, Mapping) else fact)
+        if re.search(rf"\b(?:not|no|without|non)[ -]+{re.escape(attribute)}\b", evidence):
+            return False
+        return True
+    if attribute.startswith("constraint:"):
+        wanted = attribute.split(":", 1)[1]
+        for name, fact in attributes.items():
+            evidence = _norm(fact.get("evidence") if isinstance(fact, Mapping) else fact)
+            if _norm(name) == wanted or wanted in evidence.split():
+                return True
+    return False
+
+
+def candidate_constraint_status(
+    *,
+    attributes: Mapping[str, Any],
+    form_factor: str | None,
+    constraints: CurrentTaskConstraints | None,
+    product_type: str | None = None,
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """Check one candidate's trusted catalogue attributes against current constraints."""
+    if constraints is None or constraints.is_empty:
+        return {"violations": [], "unknown": [], "matched": [], "status": "none"}
+    violations: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+    unknown: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+    matched: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+    for item in constraints.hard_exclusions:
+        if _attribute_present(attributes, form_factor, item, product_type=product_type):
+            violations.append(item.as_dict())
+        else:
+            unknown.append(item.as_dict())
+    for item in constraints.soft_preferences:
+        if _attribute_present(attributes, form_factor, item, product_type=product_type):
+            matched.append(item.as_dict())
+        else:
+            unknown.append(item.as_dict())
+    status = "violated" if violations else ("unknown" if unknown else "verified")
+    return {
+        "violations": violations,
+        "unknown": unknown,
+        "matched": matched,
+        "status": status,
+    }

+"""Targeted BGE single-flight and warm-up tests (no network, no GPU)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import threading
+import time
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+
+import numpy as np
+import pytest
+
+from experiments.p14c1.search_backend import LocalBGEDenseIndex
+from recommendation.control.catalog_search import CatalogSearchSource
+from recommendation.inference.dense_catalog import DenseHit
+
+
+def _index(loader):
+    vectors = np.vstack([np.eye(768, dtype=np.float32)[0], np.eye(768, dtype=np.float32)[1]])
+    return LocalBGEDenseIndex(
+        identities=["A", "B"], vectors=vectors, titles=["A title", "B title"],
+        model_path="unused-model", encoder_loader=loader,
+    )
+
+
+def test_encoder_single_flight_loads_once_and_publishes_ready():
+    calls = 0
+    lock = threading.Lock()
+
+    def loader(_path):
+        nonlocal calls
+        with lock:
+            calls += 1
+        time.sleep(0.05)
+        return SimpleNamespace(), SimpleNamespace(eval=lambda: None)
+
+    index = _index(loader)
+    barrier = threading.Barrier(8)
+    results: list[object] = []
+
+    def worker():
+        barrier.wait()
+        results.append(index._load_encoder())
+
+    threads = [threading.Thread(target=worker) for _ in range(8)]
+    for thread in threads:
+        thread.start()
+    for thread in threads:
+        thread.join()
+    assert calls == 1
+    assert len(results) == 8
+    status = index.encoder_status()
+    assert status["ready"] is True
+    assert status["attempts"] == 1
+    assert status["loading"] is False
+
+
+def test_encoder_failure_is_retried_once_and_can_recover():
+    calls = 0
+
+    def loader(_path):
+        nonlocal calls
+        calls += 1
+        if calls == 1:
+            raise RuntimeError("simulated meta-device load failure")
+        return SimpleNamespace(), SimpleNamespace(eval=lambda: None)
+
+    index = _index(loader)
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="attempt 1/2"):
+        index._load_encoder()
+    assert index.encoder_status()["ready"] is False
+    assert index.encoder_status()["error"]
+    tokenizer, model = index._load_encoder()
+    assert tokenizer is not None and model is not None
+    assert calls == 2
+    assert index.encoder_status()["ready"] is True
+    assert index.encoder_status()["error"] is None
+
+
+def test_encoder_repeated_failures_stop_after_two_attempts():
+    calls = 0
+
+    def loader(_path):
+        nonlocal calls
+        calls += 1
+        raise RuntimeError("always fails")
+
+    index = _index(loader)
+    for _ in range(2):
+        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+            index._load_encoder()
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="after 2 attempts"):
+        index._load_encoder()
+    assert calls == 2
+    assert index.encoder_status()["ready"] is False
+
+
+class _FakeDense:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.warmup_queries: list[str] = []
+        self.search_queries: list[str] = []
+
+    def warmup(self, query):
+        self.warmup_queries.append(query)
+        return {"status": "ok", "query": query, "total_ms": 1.0}
+
+    def search(self, query, *, limit=10):
+        self.search_queries.append(query)
+        return [DenseHit(parent_asin="amazon_electronics_c4:A", rank=1, score=0.5, title="A title")]
+
+    def encoder_status(self):
+        return {"ready": True, "attempts": 1}
+
+
+class _Catalog:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.records = {
+            "amazon_electronics_c4:A": SimpleNamespace(title="A title"),
+            "amazon_electronics_c4:B": SimpleNamespace(title="B title"),
+        }
+
+
+def test_catalog_source_warmup_exercises_real_dense_search_and_is_ready():
+    dense = _FakeDense()
+    source = CatalogSearchSource(_Catalog(), mode="bge", dense_index=dense)
+    assert source.search_ready is True
+    result = source.warmup("wireless headphones")
+    assert result["status"] == "ok"
+    assert dense.warmup_queries == ["wireless headphones"]
+    assert dense.search_queries == ["wireless headphones"]
+    assert result["retrieval_check"]["matched"] is True
+    assert source.status()["search_ready"] is True
+
+
+def test_bm25_source_warmup_is_explicitly_not_required():
+    source = CatalogSearchSource(_Catalog(), mode="bm25")
+    assert source.warmup()["status"] == "not_required"
+    assert source.search_ready is True

+"""Regression tests for current-task constraint priority after a clarification reply."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from recommendation.preference.current_task_constraints import (
+    CurrentTaskConstraints,
+    candidate_constraint_status,
+    effective_preferences,
+)
+from recommendation.preference.decision_gate import (
+    ALLOW_FINALIZE,
+    DecisionGate,
+    GateBudget,
+    GateContext,
+)
+from experiments.p14b1.diagnose import conflicts
+
+
+NS = "amazon_electronics_c4"
+
+
+def _reply_constraints() -> CurrentTaskConstraints:
+    return CurrentTaskConstraints.from_payload({
+        "preferences": [
+            {
+                "kind": "feature",
+                "value": "wireless headphones for commuting",
+                "polarity": "prefer",
+                "source_text": "For this purchase, I prefer wireless headphones for commuting.",
+                "mode": "add",
+            },
+            {
+                "kind": "feature",
+                "value": "wired headphones",
+                "polarity": "avoid",
+                "source_text": "Please avoid wired headphones.",
+                "mode": "add",
+            },
+        ],
+        "removals": [],
+    }, source_turn_id="reply-1")
+
+
+def test_current_avoid_overrides_history_only_in_effective_task_view():
+    constraints = _reply_constraints()
+    assert [item.attribute for item in constraints.hard_exclusions] == ["wired"]
+    assert [item.attribute for item in constraints.soft_preferences] == ["wireless"]
+
+    historical = [
+        {"value": "wired", "polarity": "positive", "source_turn_id": "old"},
+        {"value": "wireless", "polarity": "positive", "source_turn_id": "old"},
+    ]
+    effective = effective_preferences(historical, constraints)
+
+    # The historical entries are untouched; only the task effective view changes.
+    assert historical[0]["polarity"] == "positive"
+    assert not any(
+        item["value"] == "wired" and item["polarity"] == "positive"
+        for item in effective
+    )
+    assert any(
+        item["value"] == "wired" and item["polarity"] == "negative"
+        for item in effective
+    )
+    assert any(
+        item["value"] == "wireless" and item["polarity"] == "positive"
+        for item in effective
+    )
+    assert conflicts(effective) == []
+
+
+def test_current_avoid_does_not_turn_into_permanent_retraction():
+    constraints = _reply_constraints()
+    assert constraints.hard_exclusions[0].polarity == "exclude"
+    assert constraints.hard_exclusions[0].source_turn_id == "reply-1"
+    payload = constraints.as_dict()
+    assert "removed" not in payload
+
+
+def test_product_evidence_matching_distinguishes_wired_wireless_unknown_and_negation():
+    constraints = _reply_constraints()
+    wired = candidate_constraint_status(
+        attributes={"wired": {"evidence": "Earphones Wired Headphones"}},
+        form_factor="in_ear", constraints=constraints, product_type="headphones",
+    )
+    assert wired["status"] == "violated"
+    assert wired["violations"][0]["attribute"] == "wired"
+
+    wireless = candidate_constraint_status(
+        attributes={"wireless": {"evidence": "Wireless Bluetooth Headphones"}},
+        form_factor="in_ear", constraints=constraints, product_type="headphones",
+    )
+    assert wireless["status"] == "unknown"  # absence of wired evidence is not proof
+    assert wireless["violations"] == []
+    assert wireless["matched"][0]["attribute"] == "wireless"
+
+    unknown = candidate_constraint_status(
+        attributes={}, form_factor=None, constraints=constraints, product_type="headphones",
+    )
+    assert unknown["status"] == "unknown"
+    assert unknown["violations"] == []
+
+    negated = candidate_constraint_status(
+        attributes={"wired": {"evidence": "wireless headphones, not wired"}},
+        form_factor="in_ear", constraints=constraints, product_type="headphones",
+    )
+    assert negated["violations"] == []
+
+
+def test_brand_exclusion_requires_trusted_brand_evidence():
+    constraints = CurrentTaskConstraints.from_payload({
+        "preferences": [{
+            "kind": "brand", "value": "Acme", "polarity": "avoid",
+            "source_text": "Please avoid Acme.", "mode": "add",
+        }],
+        "removals": [],
+    }, source_turn_id="reply-brand")
+    assert constraints.hard_exclusions[0].attribute == "brand"
+
+    trusted = candidate_constraint_status(
+        attributes={"brand": {"evidence": "Acme Bluetooth Headphones"}},
+        form_factor=None, constraints=constraints, product_type="headphones",
+    )
+    assert trusted["status"] == "violated"
+
+    unknown = candidate_constraint_status(
+        attributes={}, form_factor=None, constraints=constraints, product_type="headphones",
+    )
+    assert unknown["status"] == "unknown"
+    assert unknown["violations"] == []
+
+
+def test_explicit_remove_is_not_a_current_task_hard_exclusion():
+    constraints = CurrentTaskConstraints.from_payload({
+        "preferences": [],
+        "removals": [{
+            "kind": "feature", "value": "wired",
+            "source_text": "Forget my wired preference.", "mode": "remove",
+        }],
+    }, source_turn_id="reply-retract")
+    assert constraints.is_empty
+
+
+def _row(item_id: str, rank: int, *, task_conflicts=None):
+    return {
+        "product_ref": {"namespace": NS, "item_id": item_id},
+        "query_rank": rank,
+        "hard_tier": 0,
+        "role_status": "MATCH",
+        "product_type": "headphones",
+        "candidate_role": "main",
+        "matched_preferences": [],
+        "conflicting_preferences": [],
+        "unknown_preferences": [],
+        "task_constraint_conflicts": task_conflicts or [],
+        "task_constraint_status": {"status": "violated" if task_conflicts else "unknown"},
+        "evidence_refs": [],
+    }
+
+
+def test_gate_filters_current_task_violation_and_never_finalizes_wired_candidate():
+    gate = DecisionGate()
+    constraint = _reply_constraints().hard_exclusions[0].as_dict()
+    context = GateContext(
+        query="I need headphones.",
+        candidates=[_row("WIRED", 1, task_conflicts=[constraint]), _row("UNKNOWN", 2)],
+        active_preferences=[{"value": "wireless", "polarity": "positive"}],
+        hard_requirements=[],
+        preference_conflicts=[],
+        namespace=NS,
+        query_specificity=True,
+        expected_product_type="headphones",
+        expected_product_role="main",
+        budget=GateBudget(),
+        task_constraints=[constraint],
+    )
+    decision = gate.check(context)
+    assert decision.allow_finalize is True
+    assert decision.action == ALLOW_FINALIZE
+    assert decision.selected_candidate_id == "UNKNOWN"
+    assert decision.task_constraint_status == "unknown"
+
+    blocked = gate.check(GateContext(
+        query="I need headphones.",
+        candidates=[_row("WIRED", 1, task_conflicts=[constraint])],
+        active_preferences=[{"value": "wireless", "polarity": "positive"}],
+        hard_requirements=[],
+        preference_conflicts=[],
+        namespace=NS,
+        query_specificity=True,
+        expected_product_type="headphones",
+        expected_product_role="main",
+        budget=GateBudget(max_retrievals=1),
+        task_constraints=[constraint],
+    ))
+    assert blocked.allow_finalize is False
+    assert blocked.task_constraint_status == "violated"
+    assert blocked.reason_code == "current_task_constraint_violation"

+}

+def _qwen_result(status: str = "finalized") -> dict[str, Any]:
+    return {
+        "schema": "agentrecx.p17.run_agent_turn.v1",
+        "status": status,
+        "query": "I need earbuds for working out",
+        "planner": PLANNER,
+        "selected_product_id": PRODUCT_ID if status == "finalized" else None,
+        "candidate_ids": [PRODUCT_ID] if status == "finalized" else [],
+        "gate": GATE if status == "finalized" else {"action": "NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION", "reason_code": "ambiguity"},
+        "evidence_bundle": None,
+        "planner_steps": {"steps": [], "final_ids": [PRODUCT_ID]},
+        "clarification": (
+            {"clarification_id": "qclar_test", "question": "Do you prefer over-ear or in-ear?",
+             "reason": "qwen_planner_ask", "expires_in_seconds": 1800, "memory_write_enabled": True}
+            if status == "awaiting_clarification" else None
+        ),
+        "search": {"search_mode": "bm25", "score_kind": "bm25", "catalogue_size": 76263},
+        "rag": {"status": "validated", "final_answer": "A validated P15 answer.", "bundle": {}, "claim_validation": {}},
+        "error": None,
+        "latency_ms": 444.0,
+    }
+
+
+class _Health:
+    def mark_unavailable(self, detail: str) -> None:
+        self.unavailable = detail
+
+
+class _Manager:
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self.settings = Settings(search_mode="bm25", qwen_live_enabled=True, qwen_model_endpoint="http://127.0.0.1:9")
+        self.calls: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []
+        self.health = _Health()
+
+    def ensure_ready(self, profile_id: str):  # noqa: ANN001
+        class _Ready:
+            writes_enabled = True
+        return _Ready()
+
+    def catalogue_size(self) -> int:
+        return 76263
+
+    async def call_tool(self, profile_id: str, tool: str, arguments: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
+                        **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        args = dict(arguments or {})
+        self.calls.append((tool, args))
+        callback = kwargs.get("progress_callback")
+        if tool == "run_agent_turn":
+            if callback is not None:
+                await callback(10, 100, json.dumps({"tool": "qwen_planner", "label": "Qwen Planner Request", "status": "running", "detail": "Qwen3.5-9B-Base; step 1"}))
+                await callback(20, 100, json.dumps({"tool": "qwen_planner", "label": "Qwen Planner Request", "status": "ok", "detail": "Qwen3.5-9B-Base; step 1", "latency_ms": 111.5}))
+                await callback(30, 100, json.dumps({"tool": "planner_action", "label": "Planner Action: RETRIEVE", "status": "ok", "detail": "step 1"}))
+                await callback(40, 100, json.dumps({"tool": "retrieve_1", "label": "RETRIEVE", "status": "ok", "detail": "20 candidates", "latency_ms": 2.0}))
+            return _qwen_result()
+        if tool == "get_product_info":
+            return INFO
+        if tool == "submit_clarification_reply":
+            return {
+                "update": {"added": 2, "superseded": 0, "removed": 0, "skipped_duplicates": 0,
+                           "already_processed": False},
+                "confirmation_source": "web_ui_explicit_user_reply",
+                "active_preferences": [{"memory_id": "mem_1", "value": "wireless", "polarity": "positive",
+                                        "kind": "feature", "status": "active", "source_text": "wireless"}],
+                "inactive_preferences": [],
+            }
+        if tool == "get_active_preferences":
+            return {"authorized_user_key": "p14e2_lite_0057", "history_status": "ok",
+                    "active_preferences": [{"memory_id": "mem_1", "value": "wireless", "polarity": "positive",
+                                            "kind": "feature", "status": "active", "source_text": "wireless"}],
+                    "inactive_preferences": []}
+        if tool == "recommend_products":
+            raise AssertionError("Qwen resume must not call the Rule recommendation tool")
+        raise AssertionError(f"unexpected tool {tool}")
+
+
+def _session() -> Session:
+    return Session(session_id="web-test", profile_id="profile_0049")
+
+
+def test_qwen_live_stream_renders_real_progress_products_evidence_and_planner_metadata():
+    manager = _Manager()
+    session = _session()
+    settings = manager.settings
+    events: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+    health = _Health()
+
+    async def collect() -> None:
+        async for item in demo_flow.chat_events(
+            manager=manager, session=session, settings=settings,
+            message="I need earbuds for working out", mode="qwen_live", qwen_health=health,
+        ):
+            events.append(item)
+
+    asyncio.run(collect())
+    names = [item["event"] for item in events]
+    assert "products" in names and "evidence" in names and "text" in names and "done" in names
+    tools = [item["data"]["tool"] for item in events if item["event"] == "tool_status"]
+    assert "qwen_planner" in tools and "planner_action" in tools and "retrieve_1" in tools
+    done = next(item["data"] for item in events if item["event"] == "done")
+    assert done["planner"] == "qwen_base"
+    assert done["planner_calls"] == 2
+    assert done["planner_latency_ms"] == 334.0
+    assert session.turns[-1].planner == "qwen_base"
+    assert session.turns[-1].planner_model == "Qwen3.5-9B-Base"
+    assert session.turns[-1].view()["planner_calls"] == 2
+

+"""Contract integration test for the optional P20 run_agent_turn MCP tool.
+
+A local HTTP double (NOT Qwen) returns deterministic PlannerActions.  The test proves that the
+real MCP server + real executor/catalogue/BM25/Gate path executes those actions, exposes the
+conditional 8th tool and forwards real progress notifications.  It is gated because it loads the
+real catalogue:
+
+    AGENTRECX_RUN_QWEN_MCP_INTEGRATION=1 .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q \
+        tests/test_qwen_live_mcp_integration.py
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+import json
+import os
+import threading
+import time
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import pytest
+
+from web_demo.config import REPO, Settings
+from web_demo.mcp_manager import MCPManager, MCPManagerError
+
+pytestmark = [
+    pytest.mark.slow,
+    pytest.mark.skipif(
+        os.environ.get("AGENTRECX_RUN_QWEN_MCP_INTEGRATION") != "1",
+        reason="real-catalogue MCP contract test; set AGENTRECX_RUN_QWEN_MCP_INTEGRATION=1",
+    ),
+]
+RUNS = REPO / "runs/web_demo/qwen_live"
+QUERY = "I need earbuds I can use while working out."
+
+
+class _FakePlanner(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+    """Deterministic PlannerAction test double; never claimed to be Qwen."""
+
+    requests: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+
+    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
+        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
+        payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length).decode("utf-8"))
+        self.__class__.requests.append(payload)
+        if self.path == "/explain":
+            self.send_error(404)
+            return
+        if self.path != "/plan":
+            self.send_error(404)
+            return
+        step = int(payload.get("step") or 0)
+        query = str(payload.get("query") or QUERY)
+        candidate_count = int(payload.get("candidate_count") or 0)
+        reranked = bool(payload.get("reranked"))
+        if candidate_count <= 0:
+            action = "RETRIEVE"
+            arguments = {"query": query}
+        elif not reranked:
+            action = "RERANK"
+            arguments = {}
+        else:
+            action = "FINALIZE"
+            arguments = {}
+        response = {
+            "payload": {"action": action, "arguments": arguments, "rationale": "contract test double"},
+            "text": json.dumps({"action": action, "step": step}),
+            "errors": [],
+            "latency_ms": 7.5,
+            "input_tokens": 100,
+            "output_tokens": 10,
+        }
+        body = json.dumps(response).encode("utf-8")
+        self.send_response(200)
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
+        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
+        self.end_headers()
+        self.wfile.write(body)
+
+    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args) -> None:
+        return
+
+
+async def _wait_ready(manager: MCPManager, timeout_s: float = 300.0) -> None:
+    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_s
+    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
+        if manager.ready():
+            return
+        if manager.status() == "error":
+            raise AssertionError(f"MCP startup failed: {manager.health_servers()}")
+        await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
+    raise AssertionError(f"MCP not ready after {timeout_s}s: {manager.health_servers()}")
+
+
+async def _scenario(server: ThreadingHTTPServer, endpoint: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    settings = Settings(
+        search_mode="bm25",
+        allow_memory_writes=False,
+        qwen_live_enabled=True,
+        qwen_model_endpoint=endpoint,
+        qwen_explain=False,
+        qwen_turn_timeout_s=300.0,
+        mcp_timeout_s=300.0,
+    )
+    manager = MCPManager(settings)
+    progress: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+    started = time.perf_counter()
+
+    async def on_progress(current: float, total: float | None, text: str | None) -> None:
+        del current, total
+        try:
+            event = json.loads(text or "")
+        except (TypeError, ValueError):
+            return
+        if isinstance(event, dict):
+            progress.append(event)
+
+    try:
+        manager.start()
+        await _wait_ready(manager)
+        runtime = manager.runtimes["profile_0049"]
+        tool_names = [str(tool["name"]) for tool in (runtime.tools or [])]
+        assert len(tool_names) == 8, tool_names
+        assert "run_agent_turn" in tool_names
+        result = await manager.call_tool(
+            "profile_0049", "run_agent_turn", {"query": QUERY},
+            timeout_s=300.0, progress_callback=on_progress,
+        )
+        transcript = {
+            "schema": "agentrecx.p20.qwen_mcp_contract.v1",
+            "model": "HTTP test double (not Qwen)",
+            "query": QUERY,
+            "tools": tool_names,
+            "tool_count": len(tool_names),
+            "runtime_profile": runtime.profile_id,
+            "terminal": {
+                "status": result.get("status"),
+                "selected_product_id": result.get("selected_product_id"),
+                "candidate_ids": result.get("candidate_ids"),
+            },
+            "planner": result.get("planner"),
+            "gate": result.get("gate"),
+            "evidence_bundle_present": bool(result.get("evidence_bundle")),
+            "progress_events": progress,
+            "latency_ms": round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2),
+        }
+        RUNS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        (RUNS / "mcp_contract_transcript.json").write_text(
+            json.dumps(transcript, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
+        )
+        return transcript
+    finally:
+        await manager.stop()
+
+
+def test_run_agent_turn_contract_with_real_catalogue(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
+    del tmp_path
+    handler = type("Handler", (_FakePlanner,), {"requests": []})
+    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), handler)
+    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
+    thread.start()
+    endpoint = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}"
+    try:
+        transcript = asyncio.run(_scenario(server, endpoint))
+    finally:
+        server.shutdown()
+        server.server_close()
+
+    planner = transcript["planner"] or {}
+    actions = [item.get("action") for item in planner.get("actions") or []]
+    assert planner.get("kind") == "qwen_base"
+    assert planner.get("calls", 0) >= 2
+    assert "RETRIEVE" in actions
+    assert transcript["terminal"]["status"] in {
+        "finalized", "awaiting_clarification", "no_verified_candidate_in_scope",
+        "budget_exhausted",
+    }
+    assert transcript["progress_events"], "MCP progress notifications were not delivered"
+    progress_tools = {str(item.get("tool")) for item in transcript["progress_events"]}
+    assert "qwen_planner" in progress_tools
+    qwen_rows = [item for item in transcript["progress_events"] if item.get("tool") == "qwen_planner"]
+    assert any(item.get("status") == "running" for item in qwen_rows)
+    assert any(item.get("status") == "ok" for item in qwen_rows)
+    assert not any(item.get("status") == "running" for item in qwen_rows[-1:]), qwen_rows[-1:]
+    assert any(item.startswith(("retrieve_", "rerank_", "finalize_")) for item in progress_tools)
+    # No hidden Rule fallback: the model's action sequence is what the executor consumed.
+    assert transcript["planner"]["calls"] == len(planner.get("actions") or [])

+"""Qwen Base Live config/health contract tests (no model: test HTTP double)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+import json
+import threading
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
+
+from web_demo.config import Settings, load_settings, modes_public
+from web_demo.qwen_health import QwenHealthMonitor
+
+
+class _PlannerDouble(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+    action = "RETRIEVE"
+
+    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
+        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
+        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length).decode("utf-8"))
+        if self.path != "/plan":
+            self.send_error(404)
+            return
+        action = self.action
+        arguments = {"query": body.get("query") or "wireless earbuds"}
+        payload = {
+            "payload": {"action": action, "arguments": arguments, "rationale": "test double"},
+            "text": json.dumps({"action": action}),
+            "errors": [],
+            "latency_ms": 12.5,
+            "input_tokens": 10,
+            "output_tokens": 5,
+        }
+        data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
+        self.send_response(200)
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
+        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
+        self.end_headers()
+        self.wfile.write(data)
+
+    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args) -> None:
+        return
+
+
+def _server(action: str = "RETRIEVE") -> tuple[ThreadingHTTPServer, str, type]:
+    handler = type("Handler", (_PlannerDouble,), {"action": action})
+    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), handler)
+    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
+    thread.start()
+    return server, f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}", handler
+
+
+def test_qwen_settings_default_disabled_and_aliases():
+    settings = load_settings({})
+    assert settings.qwen_live_enabled is False
+    assert settings.qwen_live_configured is False
+    modes = modes_public(cpu_live_available=True)
+    assert modes["cpu_live"]["available"] is True
+    assert modes["qwen_live"]["available"] is False
+    assert "No live Qwen service detected" in modes["qwen_live"]["disabled_reason"]
+
+    enabled = load_settings({
+        "QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED": "1",
+        "QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT": "http://127.0.0.1:9",
+        "QWEN_MODEL_NAME": "Qwen3.5-9B-Base",
+        "QWEN_LIVE_MAX_STEPS": "6",
+    })
+    assert enabled.qwen_live_configured is True
+    assert enabled.qwen_max_steps == 6
+    assert enabled.qwen_model_endpoint == "http://127.0.0.1:9"
+
+
+def test_health_requires_real_planner_action_and_does_not_leak_endpoint():
+    server, endpoint, _ = _server("RETRIEVE")
+    try:
+        settings = Settings(qwen_live_enabled=True, qwen_model_endpoint=endpoint)
+        monitor = QwenHealthMonitor(settings)
+        result = asyncio.run(monitor.check())
+        assert result["available"] is True
+        assert result["status"] == "available"
+        assert result["planner_action"] == "RETRIEVE"
+        assert result["latency_ms"] >= 0
+        serialized = json.dumps(result)
+        assert endpoint not in serialized
+    finally:
+        server.shutdown()
+        server.server_close()
+
+
+def test_health_rejects_invalid_or_missing_action():
+    server, endpoint, _ = _server("NOT_A_PLANNER_ACTION")
+    try:
+        settings = Settings(qwen_live_enabled=True, qwen_model_endpoint=endpoint)
+        monitor = QwenHealthMonitor(settings)
+        result = asyncio.run(monitor.check())
+        assert result["available"] is False
+        assert result["status"] == "unavailable"
+        assert "PlannerAction" not in (result["detail"] or "") or "invalid" in (result["detail"] or "")
+        modes = modes_public(cpu_live_available=True, qwen_health=result)
+        assert modes["qwen_live"]["available"] is False
+    finally:
+        server.shutdown()
+        server.server_close()
+
+
+def test_health_unreachable_is_unavailable_not_rule():
+    settings = Settings(
+        qwen_live_enabled=True,
+        qwen_model_endpoint="http://127.0.0.1:9",
+        qwen_health_timeout_s=0.2,
+    )
+    monitor = QwenHealthMonitor(settings)
+    result = asyncio.run(monitor.check())
+    assert result["available"] is False
+    assert result["status"] == "unavailable"
+    assert result["planner_action"] is None
+    assert "No live Qwen service detected" in modes_public(
+        cpu_live_available=True, qwen_health=result
+    )["qwen_live"]["disabled_reason"]

 Do not invent candidates or claim a tool was run. The trusted executor validates the
 action, role, price constraints and catalogue provenance. If no allowed action is useful,
 return a final action only when the observation has candidates; otherwise choose ASK.
+
+Trusted Decision Gate authority (generic rule, applies to every shopping task):
+- When the observation says needs_user_input=true, or m5_state.gate_action is
+  NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION, choose ASK with a question about the unresolved issue. Do not
+  FINALIZE while that signal is present, even if candidates exist.
+- When the latest observation says m5_state.gate_action is USER_CLARIFICATION_REPLIED and
+  m5_state.preference_conflicts is empty, the user's clarification has been resolved:
+  use the existing candidates and choose FINALIZE rather than asking the same question
+  again. Only ask again if a new, genuinely unresolved ambiguity remains.
+- FINALIZE is legal only when the observation has candidates and the Gate does not require
+  user input. Never bypass the Gate or invent a resolution.
+- ASK is reserved for that Gate/needs_user_input clarification signal. If
+  needs_user_input is false and m5_state.gate_action is not NEED_USER_CLARIFICATION,
+  ASK is forbidden. Do not ASK merely because a numeric budget looks unusual or because
+  you want to confirm a possible typo; continue with the allowed planning actions and let
+  the existing executor/Gate produce its bounded non-recommendation when the candidates
+  cannot be finalized.
 """

+/**
+ * Real planner metadata for one assistant turn (docs/QWEN_LIVE_CONTRACT.md §3).
+ *
+ * Rendered only when the backend reported a planner `kind`; the label comes from that kind and
+ * nothing is inferred when it is absent (an older BFF or a Rule turn without metadata).
+ */
+function PlannerBadge({ planner }: { planner: PlannerMeta }) {
+  const latency = plannerLatencyTotal(planner);
+  const title = [
+    planner.model ? `model ${planner.model}` : null,
+    latency != null ? `planner latency ${formatLatency(latency)}` : null,
+    planner.actions?.length ? `${planner.actions.length} reported actions` : null,
+  ]
+    .filter(Boolean)
+    .join(" · ");
+  return (
+    <div
+      className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1"
+      data-testid="turn-planner"
+      data-planner-kind={planner.kind}
+      title={title || undefined}
+    >
+      <Pill tone={planner.kind === "qwen_base" ? "accent" : "muted"} dot>
+        <Icon name="spark" size={10} />
+        {plannerSummary(planner)}
+      </Pill>
+      {latency != null ? (
+        <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-(--ink-faint)">planner {formatLatency(latency)}</span>
+      ) : null}
+      {planner.model ? <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-(--ink-faint)">{planner.model}</span> : null}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+// AgentRec-X Web Demo (2026-11) - new frontend module for the local shopping-assistant demo.
+// No upstream code is copied in this file; adapted Apache-2.0 components are listed in
+// THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md and keep their Copyright 2026 Anthropic PBC + SPDX headers.
+
+"use client";
+
+import type { QwenLiveState } from "@/lib/modes";
+import { formatLatency } from "@/lib/format";
+import { Icon } from "./icons";
+import { Button, Pill } from "./ui";
+import type { UiMode } from "./Header";
+
+/**
+ * Explicit Qwen Base Live availability, from the real BFF health/config.
+ *
+ * - Unavailable: the backend's own `disabled_reason` is shown verbatim; the mode cannot be
+ *   selected and there is no fallback.
+ * - Unavailable *while selected*: an additional danger notice states that nothing is being run
+ *   and the user must switch back (never a silent CPU fallback).
+ * - Available: nothing is rendered here; the header carries the live badge.
+ */
+export default function QwenLiveNotice({
+  qwenLive,
+  mode,
+  onSwitchToCpu,
+}: {
+  qwenLive: QwenLiveState;
+  mode: UiMode;
+  onSwitchToCpu: () => void;
+}) {
+  if (qwenLive.available) return null;
+  const selected = mode === "qwen_live";
+  const reason = qwenLive.disabledReason ?? "No live Qwen service detected";
+  const meta: string[] = [];
+  if (qwenLive.status) meta.push(`status=${qwenLive.status}`);
+  if (qwenLive.enabled != null) meta.push(`enabled=${qwenLive.enabled}`);
+  if (qwenLive.configured != null) meta.push(`configured=${qwenLive.configured}`);
+  if (qwenLive.model) meta.push(`model=${qwenLive.model}`);
+  if (qwenLive.latencyMs != null) meta.push(`probe=${formatLatency(qwenLive.latencyMs)}`);
+
+  return (
+    <section
+      data-testid="qwen-unavailable-notice"
+      data-state={selected ? "selected-unavailable" : "unavailable"}
+      role={selected ? "alert" : "status"}
+      aria-live="polite"
+      className={`mt-4 rounded-xl border p-3 ${
+        selected ? "border-(--danger)/50 bg-(--danger-soft)/40" : "border-(--line) bg-(--card)"
+      }`}
+    >
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+        <Icon
+          name="alert"
+          size={14}
+          className={selected ? "text-(--danger)" : "text-(--ink-faint)"}
+        />
+        <span className="text-[12px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.04em] text-(--ink)">
+          {selected ? "Qwen Live is unavailable — this turn was not run" : "Qwen Live — Unavailable"}
+        </span>
+        <Pill tone={selected ? "danger" : "muted"} className="ml-auto">
+          {qwenLive.status ?? "unavailable"}
+        </Pill>
+      </div>
+      <p
+        className={`mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-[12px] leading-relaxed ${
+          selected ? "text-(--danger)" : "text-(--ink-2)"
+        }`}
+      >
+        {reason}
+      </p>
+      {selected ? (
+        <p className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] leading-snug text-(--ink-2)">
+          The backend no longer reports Qwen Base Live as available, so nothing is executed and no
+          CPU/Replay fallback is substituted. Switch back to CPU Live to continue this session.
+        </p>
+      ) : (
+        <p className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] leading-snug text-(--ink-soft)">
+          Qwen Live can only be selected when the backend health probe reports it available. CPU
+          Live remains the default live mode and Qwen Replay remains a read-only historical replay.
+        </p>
+      )}
+      {meta.length ? (
+        <p className="mt-1 break-words font-mono text-[10px] text-(--ink-faint)">{meta.join(" · ")}</p>
+      ) : null}
+      {selected ? (
+        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" icon="refresh" className="mt-2" onClick={onSwitchToCpu}>
+          Switch back to CPU Live
+        </Button>
+      ) : null}
+    </section>
+  );
+}

+        return true;
+      }

 }
+
+function replaceRow(
+  list: LocalToolStatus[],
+  index: number,
+  incoming: Partial<LocalToolStatus>,
+): LocalToolStatus[] {
+  const next = [...list];
+  next[index] = { ...list[index], ...incoming };
+  return next;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Merges one real `tool_status` event into the timeline.
+ *
+ * `running` opens a row (a repeated identical notification is not duplicated). A terminal status
+ * closes the newest matching running row; rows are matched on tool *and* label first because a
+ * Qwen turn legitimately repeats the same tool with a different label per planner action
+ * (`planner_action` → "Planner Action: RETRIEVE", then "Planner Action: RERANK", …). If the
+ * backend only ever sent a terminal status, it is appended as its own completed row — never
+ * dropped, never turned into a fake animation.
+ */
+export function mergeToolStatus(list: LocalToolStatus[], incoming: ToolStatus): LocalToolStatus[] {
+  if (incoming.status === "running") {
+    const last = list[list.length - 1];
+    if (last && last.status === "running" && last.tool === incoming.tool && last.label === incoming.label) {
+      // MCP progress notifications may repeat; refresh the open row instead of duplicating it.
+      return replaceRow(list, list.length - 1, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() });
+    }
+    return [...list, { ...incoming, receivedAt: performance.now() }];
+  }
+  let fallbackIndex = -1;
+  for (let index = list.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+    const row = list[index];
+    if (row.tool !== incoming.tool || row.status !== "running") continue;
+    if (row.label === incoming.label) return replaceRow(list, index, incoming);
+    if (fallbackIndex < 0) fallbackIndex = index;
+  }
+  if (fallbackIndex >= 0) return replaceRow(list, fallbackIndex, incoming);
+  return [...list, { ...incoming }];
+}
+
+/**
+ * Closes progress rows whose completion the backend never reported.
+ *
+ * A finished turn (SSE `done`, an aborted stream, or a persisted turn restored from the session)
+ * cannot still have a running step. Such a row is shown as browser-local `unknown` with an
+ * explicit note — never as an endless spinner (fake animation) and never as a fabricated "ok".
+ */
+export function finaliseStaleToolRows(list: LocalToolStatus[]): LocalToolStatus[] {
+  if (!list.some((row) => row.status === "running")) return list;
+  return list.map((row) =>
+    row.status === "running"
+      ? {
+          ...row,
+          status: "unknown",
+          detail: [row.detail, "Completion was not reported by the backend before the turn ended."]
+            .filter(Boolean)
+            .join(" · "),
+        }
+      : row,
+  );
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+// AgentRec-X Web Demo (2026-11) - new frontend module for the local shopping-assistant demo.
+// No upstream code is copied in this file; adapted Apache-2.0 components are listed in
+// THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md and keep their Copyright 2026 Anthropic PBC + SPDX headers.
+
+/**
+ * Mode resolution against the real BFF (docs/QWEN_LIVE_CONTRACT.md §2).
+ *
+ * The UI never decides that Qwen Base Live is available by itself: availability is true only when
+ * the backend explicitly reports `modes.qwen_live.available === true` (GET /api/health first, then
+ * GET /api/demo/config for an older/startup snapshot). Everything else is Unavailable and shows
+ * the backend's own `disabled_reason`.
+ */
+
+import type { DemoConfig, Health } from "./types";
+
+/**
+ * Fallback shown only when a BFF has not supplied any reason yet (e.g. the first health response
+ * of an older build). It keeps the honest "no live service" wording, and is replaced as soon as
+ * the backend sends a real `disabled_reason` / `qwen_live.detail`.
+ */
+export const QWEN_DISABLED_FALLBACK = "No live Qwen service detected (waiting for /api/health)";
+
+export interface QwenLiveState {
+  /** True only when the backend explicitly reports the mode as available. */
+  available: boolean;
+  label: string;
+  badge: string | null;
+  /** The backend's real reason while unavailable; shown verbatim (no client invention). */
+  disabledReason: string | null;
+  detail: string | null;
+  status: string | null;
+  model: string | null;
+  checkedAt: string | null;
+  enabled: boolean | null;
+  configured: boolean | null;
+  latencyMs: number | null;
+}
+
+/** Real availability of the Qwen Live mode, exactly as the backend reported it. */
+export function resolveQwenLive(health: Health | null, config: DemoConfig | null): QwenLiveState {
+  const block = health?.qwen_live ?? null;
+  const healthMode = health?.modes?.qwen_live ?? null;
+  const configMode = config?.modes?.qwen_live ?? null;
+  // Selectable only when the live backend explicitly reports modes.qwen_live.available === true.
+  // /api/health carries the current probe; /api/demo/config is the startup advertisement. Order:
+  // health modes block → health qwen_live snapshot → config modes → "not available".
+  const available =
+    typeof healthMode?.available === "boolean"
+      ? healthMode.available
+      : typeof block?.available === "boolean"
+        ? block.available
+        : configMode?.available === true;
+
+  const meta = configMode ?? healthMode;
+  const disabledReason = available
+    ? null
+    : (meta?.disabled_reason ?? healthMode?.disabled_reason ?? block?.detail ?? QWEN_DISABLED_FALLBACK);
+  const badge = available ? (meta?.badge ?? healthMode?.badge ?? null) : null;
+
+  return {
+    available,
+    label: meta?.label ?? "Qwen Live",
+    badge,
+    disabledReason,
+    detail: meta?.detail ?? null,
+    status: block?.status ?? null,
+    model: block?.model ?? null,
+    checkedAt: block?.checked_at ?? null,
+    enabled: typeof block?.enabled === "boolean" ? block.enabled : null,
+    configured: typeof block?.configured === "boolean" ? block.configured : null,
+    latencyMs: block?.latency_ms ?? null,
+  };
+}
diff --git a/web/lib/planner.ts b/web/lib/planner.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c3defb7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/web/lib/planner.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,172 @@
+// AgentRec-X Web Demo (2026-11) - new frontend module for the local shopping-assistant demo.
+// No upstream code is copied in this file; adapted Apache-2.0 components are listed in
+// THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md and keep their Copyright 2026 Anthropic PBC + SPDX headers.
+
+/**
+ * Real planner metadata → the per-turn planner badge (docs/QWEN_LIVE_CONTRACT.md §3).
+ *
+ * Sources, in the order the BFF may send them:
+ *  - SSE `done`: `planner: "qwen_base"|"rule"`, `planner_calls`, `planner_latency_ms`
+ *  - SSE `products` / persisted turn views: an optional planner block
+ *  - the P17 `run_agent_turn` result's `planner` object (kind/model/calls/latency_ms array)
+ *
+ * Nothing is invented: a turn without backend planner metadata renders no planner badge, and the
+ * label for a known kind comes only from the backend's own `kind` value.
+ */
+
+import type {
+  ClarificationReplyResponse,
+  DonePayload,
+  PlannerMeta,
+  PlannerMetaWire,
+  ProductsPayload,
+  TurnView,
+} from "./types";
+
+function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
+  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
+    ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
+    : null;
+}
+
+function nonEmptyString(value: unknown): string | null {
+  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : null;
+}
+
+function finiteNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
+  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
+}
+
+function latencyValue(value: unknown): number | number[] | null {
+  const scalar = finiteNumber(value);
+  if (scalar != null) return scalar;
+  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return null;
+  const values = value.filter((item): item is number => typeof item === "number" && Number.isFinite(item));
+  return values.length ? values : null;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Normalises one planner value from the wire. Returns null when the backend did not actually name
+ * a planner (`kind` missing): the UI must not guess "rule" or "qwen" from surrounding context.
+ */
+export function normalisePlanner(value: PlannerMetaWire): PlannerMeta | null {
+  if (!value) return null;
+  if (typeof value === "string") {
+    const kind = nonEmptyString(value);
+    return kind ? { kind } : null;
+  }
+  const object = record(value);
+  if (!object) return null;
+  const kind = nonEmptyString(object.kind ?? object.planner_kind ?? object.planner);
+  if (!kind) return null;
+  const parserErrors = Array.isArray(object.parser_errors)
+    ? object.parser_errors.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
+    : null;
+  return {
+    kind,
+    model: nonEmptyString(object.model),
+    calls: finiteNumber(object.calls ?? object.planner_calls),
+    latency_ms: latencyValue(object.latency_ms ?? object.planner_latency_ms),
+    actions: Array.isArray(object.actions) ? object.actions : null,
+    parser_errors: parserErrors,
+  };
+}
+
+function withOptionalMeta(
+  base: PlannerMeta,
+  calls: unknown,
+  latencyMs: unknown,
+  model: unknown,
+): PlannerMeta {
+  return {
+    ...base,
+    model: base.model ?? nonEmptyString(model),
+    calls: base.calls ?? finiteNumber(calls),
+    latency_ms: base.latency_ms ?? latencyValue(latencyMs),
+  };
+}
+
+/** Planner metadata from the SSE `done` event (flat fields per the contract). */
+export function plannerFromDone(done: DonePayload): PlannerMeta | null {
+  const base = normalisePlanner(done.planner);
+  if (!base) return null;
+  return withOptionalMeta(base, done.planner_calls, done.planner_latency_ms, done.planner_model);
+}
+
+/** Planner metadata from a `products` SSE event, if the backend attached it. */
+export function plannerFromProducts(payload: ProductsPayload | null | undefined): PlannerMeta | null {
+  if (!payload) return null;
+  const base = normalisePlanner(payload.planner ?? payload.planner_kind ?? null);
+  if (!base) return null;
+  return withOptionalMeta(base, payload.planner_calls, payload.planner_latency_ms, payload.planner_model);
+}
+
+/** Planner metadata from a persisted TurnView (session restore). */
+export function plannerFromTurnView(view: TurnView): PlannerMeta | null {
+  const base = normalisePlanner(view.planner ?? view.planner_kind ?? null);
+  if (!base) return null;
+  return withOptionalMeta(base, view.planner_calls, view.planner_latency_ms, view.planner_model);
+}
+
+/** Planner metadata from POST /api/clarification/reply when the resumed turn was Qwen. */
+export function plannerFromReplyResponse(
+  response: ClarificationReplyResponse | null | undefined,
+): PlannerMeta | null {
+  if (!response) return null;
+  const base = normalisePlanner(response.planner ?? null);
+  if (!base) return null;
+  return withOptionalMeta(
+    base,
+    response.planner_calls,
+    response.planner_latency_ms,
+    response.planner_model,
+  );
+}
+
+/** Later backend reports win, but a missing field never erases an earlier real value. */
+export function mergePlanner(
+  current: PlannerMeta | null,
+  incoming: PlannerMeta | null,
+): PlannerMeta | null {
+  if (!incoming) return current;
+  if (!current) return incoming;
+  return {
+    kind: incoming.kind || current.kind,
+    model: incoming.model ?? current.model ?? null,
+    calls: incoming.calls ?? current.calls ?? null,
+    latency_ms: incoming.latency_ms ?? current.latency_ms ?? null,
+    actions: incoming.actions ?? current.actions ?? null,
+    parser_errors: incoming.parser_errors ?? current.parser_errors ?? null,
+  };
+}
+
+/** Human label for the backend's planner kind only; unknown kinds are shown raw, not prettified. */
+export function plannerLabel(planner: PlannerMeta): string {
+  switch (planner.kind) {
+    case "qwen_base":
+      return "Qwen Base Live";
+    case "rule":
+      return "Rule Planner";
+    case "none":
+      // The backend explicitly reports that no planner drove this turn (e.g. a Skill/comparison
+      // call); "No planner used" states that without claiming a model.
+      return "No planner used";
+    default:
+      return planner.kind;
+  }
+}
+
+/** e.g. "Qwen Base Live · 2 planner calls" — the example copy from the contract. */
+export function plannerSummary(planner: PlannerMeta): string {
+  const parts = [plannerLabel(planner)];
+  if (planner.calls != null) parts.push(`${planner.calls} planner call${planner.calls === 1 ? "" : "s"}`);
+  return parts.join(" · ");
+}
+
+/** Total planner latency: scalar from `done`, or the sum of the MCP per-call array. */
+export function plannerLatencyTotal(planner: PlannerMeta): number | null {
+  const value = planner.latency_ms;
+  if (typeof value === "number") return value;
+  if (Array.isArray(value) && value.length) return value.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item, 0);
+  return null;
+}

+def _env_float(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: float) -> float:
+    raw = str(env.get(name, default)).strip()
+    try:
+        value = float(raw)
+    except ValueError as exc:
+        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a number, got {raw!r}") from exc
+    if value <= 0:
+        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be positive, got {value}")
+    return value
+
+
+def _env_int(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: int) -> int:
+    raw = str(env.get(name, default)).strip()
+    try:
+        return int(raw)
+    except ValueError as exc:
+        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be an integer, got {raw!r}") from exc
+
