+async def _qwen_live_turn(
+    *,
+    manager: MCPManager,
+    session: Session,
+    settings: Settings,
+    turn: Turn,
+    recorder: TurnRecorder,
+    message: str,
+    state: dict[str, Any],
+    qwen_health: Any | None,
+) -> AsyncIterator[dict[str, Any]]:
+    """Real Qwen Base Live: MCP run_agent_turn + MCP progress notifications + P15 products.
+
+    No Rule fallback: any planner/transport failure becomes a visible error.  The BFF only calls
+    this path after the background health check has validated a real PlannerAction.
+    """
+    profile_id = session.profile_id
+    entry = recorder.start("run_agent_turn", label="Qwen Agent")
+    yield event("tool_status", entry)
+
+    progress_queue: asyncio.Queue[dict[str, Any]] = asyncio.Queue()
+
+    async def progress_callback(progress: float, total: float | None, text: str | None) -> None:
+        del progress, total
+        try:
+            payload = json.loads(text or "")
+        except (TypeError, ValueError):
+            return
+        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
+            return
+        tool = str(payload.get("tool") or "qwen_progress")
+        item = {
+            "tool": tool,
+            "label": str(payload.get("label") or TOOL_LABELS.get(tool, tool)),
+            "status": str(payload.get("status") or "ok"),
+            "detail": payload.get("detail"),
+            "latency_ms": payload.get("latency_ms"),
+        }
+        _record_progress(turn, item)
+        await progress_queue.put(event("tool_status", item))
+
+    started = time.perf_counter()
+    task = asyncio.create_task(
+        manager.call_tool(
+            profile_id, "run_agent_turn", {"query": message},
+            timeout_s=settings.qwen_turn_timeout_s,
+            progress_callback=progress_callback,
+        ),
+        name="qwen-live-run-agent-turn",
+    )
+    try:
+        while True:
+            if task.done():
+                while not progress_queue.empty():
+                    yield progress_queue.get_nowait()
+                break
+            try:
+                item = await asyncio.wait_for(progress_queue.get(), timeout=0.25)
+            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
+                continue
+            yield item
+        result = await task
+    except MCPManagerError as exc:
+        recorder.error(entry, detail=exc.message)
+        if qwen_health is not None:
+            qwen_health.mark_unavailable(exc.message)
+        raise
+    except Exception:
+        if qwen_health is not None:
+            qwen_health.mark_unavailable("Qwen agent turn failed")
+        raise
+    latency_ms = _ms(started)
+    recorder.ok(entry, latency_ms=latency_ms)
+    yield event("tool_status", entry)
+
+    planner = result.get("planner") or {}
+    turn.planner = "qwen_base"
+    turn.planner_model = planner.get("model")
+    turn.planner_calls = planner.get("calls")
+    planner_latencies = [float(value) for value in (planner.get("latency_ms") or []) if value is not None]
+    turn.planner_latency_ms = round(sum(planner_latencies), 2) if planner_latencies else None
+
+    status = str(result.get("status") or "error")
+    gate = result.get("gate") or {}
+    selected_id = result.get("selected_product_id")
+    candidate_ids = [str(item) for item in (result.get("candidate_ids") or []) if str(item).strip()][:10]
+    search = result.get("search") or {}
+    retrieval_source = vm.search_retrieval_source(search)
+
+    info_entry = recorder.start("get_product_info", label="Product Info")
+    yield event("tool_status", info_entry)
+    infos: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
+    fetch_started = time.perf_counter()
+    fetch_ids = list(candidate_ids)
+    if selected_id and str(selected_id) not in fetch_ids:
+        fetch_ids.append(str(selected_id))
+    for item_id in fetch_ids:
+        infos[item_id] = await manager.call_tool(
+            profile_id, "get_product_info", {"product_ref": item_id}
+        )
+    recorder.ok(
+        info_entry,
+        latency_ms=_ms(fetch_started),
+        detail=f"{len(infos)} real catalogue records for Qwen candidates",
+    )
+    yield event("tool_status", info_entry)
+
+    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+    selected_card: dict[str, Any] | None = None
+    for item_id in candidate_ids:
+        card = vm.product_card(
+            infos[item_id], source="search", retrieval_source=retrieval_source,
+            match_status="search_result",
+            recommended=bool(selected_id) and item_id == str(selected_id),
+            product_role=(gate.get("product_role") if selected_id and item_id == str(selected_id) else None),
+        )
+        items.append(card)
+        if selected_id and item_id == str(selected_id):
+            selected_card = card
+    if selected_id and str(selected_id) not in candidate_ids and str(selected_id) in infos:
+        selected_card = vm.product_card(
+            infos[str(selected_id)], source="recommendation",
+            retrieval_source=vm.RETRIEVAL_CATALOGUE, match_status="recommended",
+            recommended=True, product_role=gate.get("product_role"),
+        )
+        items.append(selected_card)
+
+    products = vm.products_payload(
+        query=message,
+        search_mode=search.get("search_mode") or settings.search_mode,
+        score_kind=search.get("score_kind"),
+        catalogue_size=search.get("catalogue_size") or manager.catalogue_size(),
+        items=items,
+        recommended_product_id=str(selected_id) if selected_id else None,
+        gate=gate,
+    )
+    turn.products = products
+    yield event("products", products)
+
+    if status == "awaiting_clarification":
+        clarification_raw = result.get("clarification") or {}
+        clarification = vm.clarification_payload(clarification_raw)
+        clarification["memory_write_enabled"] = bool(clarification_raw.get("memory_write_enabled"))
+        turn.clarification = clarification
+        session.pending_clarification = PendingClarification(
+            payload=clarification,
+            query=message,
+            turn_id=turn.turn_id,
+            reply_turn_id=session.next_reply_turn_id(),
+            mode="qwen_live",
+        )
+        yield event("clarification", clarification)
+        narrative = vm.narrative_qwen(
+            status=status, gate=gate, planner=planner, selected_card=None,
+            clarification=clarification, catalogue_size=products.get("catalogue_size"),
+        )
+        turn.assistant_text = narrative
+        yield event("text", {"text": narrative, "final": True})
+        state["stop_reason"] = "awaiting_clarification"
+        return
+
+    if selected_id and selected_card is not None:
+        evidence = vm.evidence_payload(infos.get(str(selected_id)))
+        yield event("evidence", evidence)
+
+    rag = result.get("rag") or {}
+    final_answer = rag.get("final_answer") if isinstance(rag, Mapping) else None
+    narrative = vm.narrative_qwen(
+        status=status, gate=gate, planner=planner, selected_card=selected_card,
+        clarification=None, catalogue_size=products.get("catalogue_size"),
+        final_answer=str(final_answer) if final_answer else None,
+        planner_steps=(result.get("planner_steps") or {}),
+    )
+    turn.assistant_text = narrative
+    yield event("text", {"text": narrative, "final": True})
+    state["stop_reason"] = status
+
+
+def _record_progress(turn: Turn, item: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
+    """Persist real progress rows on the turn (same view model as live SSE)."""
+    incoming = dict(item)
+    if incoming.get("status") == "running":
+        turn.tool_activity.append(incoming)
+        return
+    for index in range(len(turn.tool_activity) - 1, -1, -1):
+        row = turn.tool_activity[index]
+        if row.get("tool") == incoming.get("tool") and row.get("status") == "running":
+            turn.tool_activity[index] = {**row, **incoming}
+            return
+    turn.tool_activity.append(incoming)
+

+"""Background health monitor for the optional real Qwen Base Live mode.
+
+The monitor performs a real ``POST {endpoint}/plan`` with the existing planner contract and only
+marks Qwen Live available when the response is a valid PlannerAction.  It never falls back to Rule,
+never starts/stops AutoDL or a tunnel, and never exposes the endpoint to the browser or logs.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+import json
+import logging
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from typing import Any
+
+from web_demo.config import Settings
+
+LOGGER = logging.getLogger("agentrecx.web_demo.qwen_health")
+PROBE_QUERY = "wireless earbuds for working out"
+# With no candidates yet, FINALIZE/RERANK are not contract-legal.  A health pass therefore proves
+# the service returned a valid action for the current observation, not just any JSON.
+PROBE_ACTIONS = ("ASK", "RETRIEVE")
+
+
+def _now_iso() -> str:
+    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
+
+
+class QwenHealthMonitor:
+    """One shared health state for both fixed profiles (the model service is process-wide)."""
+
+    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
+        self.settings = settings
+        self._task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None
+        self._stop = asyncio.Event()
+        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        self._status = "disabled" if not settings.qwen_live_configured else "checking"
+        self._available = False
+        self._planner_action: str | None = None
+        self._latency_ms: float | None = None
+        self._checked_at: str | None = None
+        self._detail: str | None = None
+
+    # -- public snapshot -----------------------------------------------------
+    def snapshot(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        return {
+            "enabled": bool(self.settings.qwen_live_enabled),
+            "configured": bool(self.settings.qwen_live_configured),
+            "status": self._status,
+            "available": bool(self._available),
+            "model": self.settings.qwen_model_name,
+            "planner_action": self._planner_action,
+            "latency_ms": self._latency_ms,
+            "checked_at": self._checked_at,
+            "detail": self._detail,
+        }
+
+    def mark_unavailable(self, detail: str) -> None:
+        """A real planner call failed after health passed: stop advertising availability."""
+        self._status = "unavailable"
+        self._available = False
+        self._detail = str(detail)[:400]
+        self._checked_at = _now_iso()
+
+    # -- lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------
+    def start(self) -> None:
+        if not self.settings.qwen_live_configured:
+            self._status = "disabled"
+            self._detail = "QWEN_LIVE_ENABLED / QWEN_MODEL_ENDPOINT not configured"
+            LOGGER.info("Qwen Live disabled (not configured)")
+            return
+        LOGGER.info("Qwen Live configured; starting real PlannerAction health checks")
+        self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._loop(), name="qwen-live-health")
+
+    async def stop(self) -> None:
+        self._stop.set()
+        task = self._task
+        self._task = None
+        if task is not None:
+            task.cancel()
+            try:
+                await task
+            except (asyncio.CancelledError, Exception):  # noqa: BLE001
+                pass
+
+    async def _loop(self) -> None:
+        while not self._stop.is_set():
+            await self.check()
+            try:
+                await asyncio.wait_for(
+                    self._stop.wait(), timeout=self.settings.qwen_health_interval_s
+                )
+            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
+                continue
+
+    async def check(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        if not self.settings.qwen_live_configured:
+            self._status = "disabled"
+            self._available = False
+            return self.snapshot()
+        async with self._lock:
+            self._status = "checking"
+            self._detail = None
+            try:
+                result = await asyncio.to_thread(self._probe)
+            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - explicit unavailable state
+                self._status = "unavailable"
+                self._available = False
+                self._detail = f"{type(exc).__name__}: probe failed"
+                self._checked_at = _now_iso()
+                LOGGER.warning("Qwen Live health unavailable: %s", self._detail)
+                return self.snapshot()
+            self._status = "available"
+            self._available = True
+            self._planner_action = result["action"]
+            self._latency_ms = result["latency_ms"]
+            self._checked_at = _now_iso()
+            self._detail = f"valid PlannerAction from {self.settings.qwen_model_name}"
+            LOGGER.info(
+                "Qwen Live health ok action=%s latency_ms=%s",
+                self._planner_action, self._latency_ms,
+            )
+            return self.snapshot()
+
+    # -- real HTTP probe -----------------------------------------------------
+    def _probe(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Blocking health probe; called in a worker thread.
+
+        A valid PlannerAction is required. This is a real model call and is intentionally the only
+        availability gate; no Rule output can satisfy it.
+        """
+        endpoint = self.settings.qwen_model_endpoint.rstrip("/")
+        body = {
+            "contract_version": "agentrecx.planner_action.v1",
+            "step": 0,
+            "query": PROBE_QUERY,
+            "candidate_ids": [],
+            "candidate_count": 0,
+            "candidate_evidence": [],
+            "allowed_actions": list(PROBE_ACTIONS),
+            "remaining_steps": max(1, self.settings.qwen_max_steps),
+            "needs_user_input": False,
+            "reranked": False,
+        }
+        request = urllib.request.Request(
+            endpoint + "/plan",
+            data=json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
+            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
+            method="POST",
+        )
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        try:
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(
+                request, timeout=self.settings.qwen_health_timeout_s
+            ) as response:
+                payload = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
+        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
+            # Never include the endpoint in the error detail.
+            raise RuntimeError(type(exc).__name__) from exc
+        latency_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 2)
+        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
+            raise RuntimeError("health response is not a JSON object")
+        errors = payload.get("errors")
+        if isinstance(errors, list) and errors:
+            raise RuntimeError("health response reported parser errors")
+        action_payload = payload.get("payload")
+        if isinstance(action_payload, dict):
+            payload = action_payload
+        raw_action = str(payload.get("action") or "").strip().upper()
+        if raw_action not in PROBE_ACTIONS:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"health returned invalid PlannerAction {raw_action!r}")
+        return {"action": raw_action, "latency_ms": latency_ms}

+def narrative_qwen(
+    *,
+    status: str,
+    gate: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
+    planner: Mapping[str, Any],
+    selected_card: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
+    clarification: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
+    catalogue_size: int | None,
+    final_answer: str | None = None,
+    planner_steps: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+) -> str:
+    """Deterministic narrative for a real Qwen turn; P15 final_answer wins when present."""
+    if final_answer:
+        # The P15 explanation already carries validated evidence claims; do not rewrite it.
+        return str(final_answer)
+    calls = planner.get("calls")
+    lines = [
+        f"Planner: {planner.get('model') or 'Qwen3.5-9B-Base'} "
+        f"({calls or 0} real model calls). Status: {status}.",
+    ]
+    if gate:
+        lines.append(f"Decision Gate: {gate.get('action')} ({gate.get('reason_code')}).")
+    if clarification:
+        lines.append(f"Question: {clarification.get('question')}")
+        lines.append("No product recommendation has been finalized in this turn.")
+    elif selected_card:
+        lines.append(
+            f"Selected product: {selected_card.get('title')} ({selected_card.get('product_id')})."
+        )
+        unknown = [item.get("attribute") for item in selected_card.get("unknown_fields") or ()]
+        if unknown:
+            lines.append("UNKNOWN fields: " + ", ".join(str(item) for item in unknown) + ".")
+    else:
+        lines.append("No product was finalized by the Decision Gate; nothing was invented.")
+    if catalogue_size:
+        lines.append(f"Catalogue: {int(catalogue_size):,} products; evidence is catalogue-only.")
+    lines.append(
+        "Catalogue evidence only; not a claim about real-world quality, price, stock or ratings."
+    )
+    return "\n".join(lines)
+
+

         "Recommendation paused: the Decision Gate requires a user clarification before it can select a product.",
